📝Yanayi ne na lokacin sanyi, garin yayi lif ba iska a keyi sosei  ba amma yanayi ne mai dadi, domin sanyin baya ratsa jiki sosei.

Wata hadadiyar mota Naga tayi parking a harabar shagalinku 🏨 hotel , sai da a ka dau wurin 5 minute kamin naga an bude marfin motar tare da zuro kafa daya waje anan na gano kafar mace ce,saboda takalmin dana gani Mai tsiini sosei.


          Harta fito daga motar waya takeyi,duk da banajin me take fada amma da alama tanajin dadin wayar,saboda irin smile din da yake kan fiskarta. 

Doguwa ce sosei duk da takalmin kafarta ya Kara taimakawa wajan fitar da tsawonta,kananan kayane ajikinta bakin juns tare da farar tesheet ta dora bakar afterdress budadda tasa dan siririn  mayafi akanta sai red din hand bag kalar takalminta duk da bana ganin fuskata sosei amma kirar jikinta Yana da kyau,domin bata da kiba Kuma ba ramammiya ce ba.


    Rufe marfin motar tayi,ta nufi kofar da zata sadata da cikin hotel din.sai a sannan ta daina wayar tana tafiya a slow kaman wadda tasha wani abu.
 
A reseption ta tsaya, ta fara magana da Wanda yake akan kanta,ga dukkan alamu daki zata Kama saboda  Naga ta ciro kudi daga cikin Jakarta ta mika,an bata keys tare da recite,saman matakalar benen dake baran hagu Naga ta nufa,,tabi wani dan sukuroto Mai tsayi,tana ta wuce dakuna daidai daki no 101 ta tsaya tare da sanya key jikin kofar ta bude...

Iska Naga ta firzar da ga bakinta,ta cilla jakar kan kujerar falon tare da cire dan gyalen data yane kanta da shi...

Tayi rigingine bisa doguwar kujera,hawaye nagani sunabin gefen fuskarta, wayarta ta dau Kara ta duba tare da cillar da ita kasan kafet.

Ta firta"BAZAN IYA BA" da karfi,ni kaina ta tsorata ni danjin  karajin  da tayi, ta Kara rushewa da kuka sosei.akalla ta dau tsawon 20 minutes tana kukannan,sai da ta gaji Dan kanta ta tashi ta dauki karamin galenta tana kokarin rufe kanta.

Bedroom din ta shiga ta nufi hanyar toilet bakin kofar ta tsaya Naga tayi addu'a ta shiga,fuskarta ta wanke tare da kuskure bakinta ta fito.

      
           Tana dawowa falo wayanta ta Kara daukar Kara a karo na 2, dauka tayi ta kalli screen din wayan,da sauri ta furta na shiga uku hannunta daya akan kirjinta!!! 

Oh my God !!!! Yaya Yasir yake kirana ? tayi saurin saka wayan a Jakarta tare da sanya takalminta cikin sauri ta fita daga dakin zuwa reseption,ta ce pls helf me ina sauri zan tafi ka rife rooms din ankirani yanzu emergency . Ya ce okey madam.

      Cikin sauri kaman zata kifa ta bude motar ta shiga tare da Bata wuta tayi hanyar gida. tafiyar 5 minute ta kaita bakin gate din BARHIM masu gadin wurin Naga sun taso da uniforms a jikinsu Mai shigen kalar na sojoji, dayan ya ce " barka da dawowa madam.   ta zuge gilas din motar  ta ce " ka ga yaya YASIR ya dawo??? Eh hajiya ya shigo bada jimawa ba,, ok, 

Dayan ya budemata gate ta shiga ciki da gudu tayi tafiya Mai  dan nisa,ta wuce gidaje sosei Naga tayi horn a bakin wani gate milk color Mai gadin ya leko tare da ce wa "barka da zuwa hajiya.                    

'Yauwa baba'

"Ya budemata gate'

Ta samu wuri kusa da wata mota kirar benz,tayi parking.

Addu'a ta farayi kamin ta bude motar ta nufi hanyar shiga babban falon gidan .


A hankali ta daga coteing  din falon tare da yin sallama kasa kasa idan ba kana kusa da ita sosei ba lallai ne kaji me ta ce ba, ba kowa  a falon sai Karan fanka da sanyin A C da yake kadawa.

A hanakali  ta sauke boyayyar ajiyar zuciya,tare da furta Alhamdlillah ta cire takalmin kafarta ta rike a hannuta.

          Kiran sallar asubar farko akan kunnan FARRAH, tayi addu'ar tashi da ga  bacci(ALLAHUMMA AHAYANA BA'AD MA AMAUTINA WA'ILAIHIN NUSHUR)
Ta tofa a tafin hannunta tare da shafawa a fuskarta zuwa kafafunta.

"Tasan yau aiki ne zata sha kaman me?" Gwamma ta fara da wuri.

 Arwallah ta dauro ta shimfida prayermate,tare da dora Zan akan kayan baccinta,ta tayar da sallar nafila.

Bayan an tayar da sallah a masallaci,itama ta gabatar da tata,tayi sauran azikar dinta da addu'o'in safiya.

Ta nufi dakin MOMY,tayi knocking a bakin kofar.

MOMY ta ce "shigo".

Akan prayermate na taddata da qur'ani a hannunta tana tilawa.

Na durkuwa bisa kafet din dakin.

"Lafiya Lau FARRAH"
"Jiya da daddare baki fito denner ba"?

"Eh wallahi MOMY na ji gajiya ne shiyasa nasha molt na kwanta".

"Tau kije ki fara aiki dukda yau weekend ne" 

"Tau"   MOMY  na nufi kiching.

Cikin ikon Allah karfe 6:40 am na fara,Amma zuwa 8:00 am na gama hada soyayyar doya ,nayi yar miya ta danyun kayan miya.na hadamasu ruwan zafi a placks Wanda yaji danyar citta da kaninfari.

Kiching din na fara gyarawa fes,nakai kayan Kan dinning table.

         Na Kara gyara falon,tare da kunna bunner ta turaren wuta,na nufi dakina Nima domin na shirya.

   Sauri nakeyi na gyara gashina saboda Naga har karfe 10:01 yayi.

"Lallai na bata time ga rashin baccin safe,ni nasan na bani tunda wannan jalimun  ya zo sai a slow.

BILKISU : a a saikin fada.

"Duk lokacin danayi yunkurin hada wannan connection din sai ansamu matala".

BILKISU; Karki damu zan hadamaki Mai zafi akwai wani abokin Mubarak tun last time daya ganmu a school duk yabi ya dameni akan maganarki,Amma zanyimashi magana karki damu kawata gwamma kema ki shigo layi a dama dake 🤣🤣.

 Ke nifa tsoro nake ji 😔🙄.

Karki wani damu dakin zama yar hannu shikenan.

Tau ba matsala zamu karayin magana,Amma sai idan zalimu ya tafi 😔.

Don't worry 🤣 kawata saina jiki.

Da ga haka na ajiye wayan tare da gyara kwanciyata Dan inaso nadanyi bacci kamin karfe 1 tayi.





✍️✍️✍️✍️✍️✍️✍️✍️

🅿️4
      📝 Farrah ita ce bata farka da ga baccin daya dauketa ba sai misalin karfe 1:35,tayi mamakin yanda tayi nisa a baccin.

Plant shues ta saka a kafarata ta nufi kicin da sauri danta dora girkin rana.

Momy ta tadda a kiching din harta dora.

"Momy ai dakin tasheni na dora"

"Karki damu Farrah na leka naga kinyi bacci,nasan akwai gajiya saboda dazu kinsha aiki".

"Tau Momynah shiyasa nake Kara kaunarki"

"Hmmmm ja'ira kawai haka kike so daman, je kiyi sallah sai kizo mu karasa aikin".

                A bangaran Yasir kuwa, shi ne bai dawo gida ba sai wuraren karfe takwas na dare.saboda Yana fitowa da ga Goruba road cikin unguwar kwado ya tsaya wurin freind dinshi Jamil, saida yayi sallar isha'i kafin ya nufo Barhim.

Saboda haryanzu yanaji zuciyarashi tanayimashi zafi,abubuwa sun taru sunyimashi yawa.ga furucin da Abbah yayimashi haryanzu Yana yimashi yawo a kwakwalwarshi.

Yana karsowa gidan direct part dinshi ya wuce danya dan watsa ruwa ya rage kayan jikinshi.

             _________________
Farrah ce da Momy a falo suna kallan tashar zeeword ana film din zarah nikah,na Kabir da zarah ita dai tana San wannan series din sosai saboda masoyan suna birgeta.

  Ta ce "Dan Allah kiyima Yaya Yasir magana ya maidoman da key din motana,saboda kinga offer week zamu koma school".

"To zanyimashi magana insha Allah kafin ya tafi"

"To amma Momy...............

Daddadan kanshin turaren da dogon hancinta ya jiyo,shiya hanata karasa maganar dake bakinta.

Da sauri ta zabura zata ruga daki,Momy tayi saurin riketa ta zaunar da ita akan kujerar .

Sallamar ya karayi a karo na biyu Yana kokarin zama Kan kafet din da yayima falon kawanya .

Momy ta amsa sallamar tana kallan yadda duk yayi wani sanyi kamar ba Yasir din da ta sani ba jarimin maza.

"Momy barka da dare"

Muryarshi ta dawo da ita da ga Dan gajeran tunanin da ta tafi.

"Yauwa magaji yaka baro su Umman Jabir"?

"Suna lafiya sunce suna gaisheki".

"To muna amsawa"

"Ke tashi ki kawoma yayanki abincinshi,Dan naga ba sasai ya fiye San zaman dinning na".
Cewar Momy tana kallan Farrah da take ta faman wur-wurga idanu, saboda yanda take jin bugun zuciyarta na karuwa da duk dakika guda na karuwar numfashinta.

Kokarin tashi takeyi amma ta kasa,saboda irin kallan da taga Yana jifanta da shi ta kasan idonsa.

Kananun kayane a jikinta bakin wandon juens da yar karamar riga yellow colour mai gajeran hannu ,domin kuwa ana hangen kyakkyawan damtsen hannunta.ta dora black cap mai kyau gashin kanta ya fito ta kasan hular zuwa bayanta Baki mai santsi .

In Ina ta farayi alamar Bata da gaskiya.

"Ehmm in wallahi mantawa nayi in saka hijaf,nazo daukan abu ne a kiching".

Ta nufi dakinta da sauri Dan kada ya tsaidata.

Shi dai baicemata komai ba harta shige, sai kallo daya bita da shi Yana girgiza kai.

Kallanshi ya mayar ga Momy kamar mai Shirin yin kuka.

   Da ido Tayimashi alamar yayi hakuri.

"Yi hakuri magaji na gane matsalarka ,idan Allah ya kaddara Farrah matarka ce to insha Allah komai zai dai


Ya ce" afuwan  UMMANA"Yana hade hannuwanshi wuri daya alamar ban hakuri .

"Idan Allah ya nufa Farrah zata zama mata a gareka  komai zai daidaita insha Allah".

"Amma sai kabi komai a hankali wannan takuramatan da kakeyi ba shi ne mafita ba".

Addu"ar mu take bukata, saboda haryanzu akwai yarinta tattare da ita.A wannan zamanin takurawa, ko yawan tsangwama da fada ba shi ke gyara tarbiyar yara ba,musamman yanmata,addu'a da nasiha sune kadai mafita.

Ta dora da "Amma Ina tabbatarma saika cire kokwanto da take sanyaka wasi-wasi a zuciyarkadomin Ina jiyemaka wata rana da zakayi nadama,domin kuwa ita yarinya ce amma duk abinda kake nunamata Yana zaunawa a zuciyarta".

Ta Mike hadi da cewa"nidai na fadama gaskiya".

Kara sadda kanshi kasa yayi,saboda TSANANIN kunyar Momy da ta Kara kamshi .
Kara muskudawa yayi saboda wata zufa da take ketomashi,hadi da daduwar bugun zuciyarshi.

A hankali ya nisa tare da Sanya hannunshi na dama mai daure da hadaddiyar agogon silver irin na kamfanim gucci, bisa kyakkyawan bakin sajenshi Wanda yammatan wannan zamanin suke ce ma quater million Yana shafawa,a hankali ya bude bakinshi kamar Wanda bayasan yin magana.

Ya ce "Afuwan Momy wallahi zuciyata ce ta kasa nutsuwa,Amma Ina ta addu'ar Allah ya kawoman nafita"

"Amin ya Allah "cewar Momy tana kokarin shiga daji.

"Ka je ka ci abincindan kasan tunda ta shige daki bazata Kara fitowa ba".


A hankali ya Mike saboda gaba daya jijiyoyin jikinshi sun gama yin sanyi.tunani yakeyi ya akayi Momy tasan wannan sirrin nashi da shi kadai yasani sai abokinshi Jamil.

Jiyayi abincinma bazai iya cinshi ba,Daman ya Dan ci dambun shinkafa da miyar alhasa a gidan su Jamil.

Dan haka kawai ya wuce part dinshi.


Yaga har kafe Tara da Yan mintina tayi.

🅿️3
      
            📝 YASIR Yana isa G R A ya tadda su JABIR a falo suna kallo a system.

Yayi sallama 
Bayan sun amsa,ya zauna suka gaisheshi.

Ya ce "Ina UMMAH"?

UMAR ya amsa da tana off-site dakin ABBA.

Saman ya haura Naga yabiyo ta wani hadaddan falo komai na falon kalar brown ne da milk.

Yayi sallama dai-dai UMMAH ta fito da ga dakin ABBA.

Kaman daman jiranshi takeyi ya ta fara fada,baka sati 2 baka zo garinnan ba Kai ba mata ka aje a garin ba,Amma baka iya zama kayi aikinka.

Kamo hannunta yayi ya ce "Haba UMMANA kiyi hakuri mu gaisa Mana" 

Bayan ta zauna kan daya daga cikin kujerun dake falo,kannan ya samu damar zama kasan kafet din ya tankwashe kafafunshi Yana kallanta .

Ya ce" afuwan  UMMANA"Yana hade hannuwanshi wuri daya alamar ban hakuri .

Shafa kanshi tayi ta ce"Allah ya albarkaci rayuwarka da sauran yan'uwanka.

Ameen UMMAN MU Allah ya karamaku nisan kwana.

Amen Amma kasan me zakayi dan kaga farincikina .

Tau UMMAH kuyi hakuri adan karaman lokaci insha Allah komai zai daidata.

Tau Allah yarda ka jira ABBANKU ya fito ku gaisa .

Dai dai ta fita da ga falon ya amsa da "To".


Jima kadan ABBAH ya fito ya gaisheshi cikin girmamawa.

Ya amsa cikin sakin fuska.

Ya Kara da ce wa duk naji irin maganar da kukayi da UMMANKU,karka damu MAGAJI na gano matsalarka Amma Insha Allahu komai ya zo karshe.tashi ka tafi ni zan fita cikin gari.

Haka ya tashi sororo kamar Wanda kwai ya fashewa a ciki.

Yayima su UMMAH da kannanshi jabir da umar sallama ya nufi BARHIM.


           ______________

__ASALINSU_

Asalin iyayensu haifaffun  garin DUTSIMMA ne daya da cikin local government din jahar KATSINA STATE. Sunan mahaifinsu Alhaji UMAR HASHIM UMAR.
       

A wannan lokacin Alhaji UMAR HASHIM UMAR duka iyayenshi sun rasu Yana zaunene a hannun Kanan mahaifinshi  wato Alhaji YUSUF  UMAR matar shi ta rike Alhaji UMAR HASHIM UMAR,Anan yayi dukkanin karatunshi tunda ga matsayin primary School aka kaishi bording school BINDAWA.  Bayan ya gama Alhaji YUSUF HASHIM  ya hadashi da Yan uwanshi guda biyu ya kaisu B U K KANO anan kowanne ya kammala digreen shi na farko.bayan sun dawo gida kowanne aka bashi kasuwancinshi a hannu shi Alhaji UMAR HASHIM UMAR aka budemashi shago a cikin centeral market cikin garin katsinan dukko shida Dan Alhaji YUSUF din ya kamamasu gida a katsina a cikin unguwar kofar dirbi sukaci gaba da harkokin kasuwancinsu.

Daman shi Alhaji UMAR HASHIM UMAR shi kadai iyayenshi suka haifa a wannan lokacin.

Cikin ikon Allah business dinsu ya bunkasa musamman shi Alhaji UMAR HASHIM UMAR saboda an hadamashi da dukiyar da iyayenshi suka barmashi bayan sun rasu.

A bangare daya Kuma Yana soyayya da masoyiyarshi wato  HINDATU itama haifafiyar garin DUTSIMMA ce babanta Yana koyarwa a G. D .S.S DUTSIMMA .

             _BAYAN_ _SHEKARA_ _DAYA_

Saida Alhaji umar ya tabbatar sun mayar da hankalinsu akan business dinsu kafin ya fara yimasu maganar aure.inda Alhaji UMAR HASHIM UMAR ya gabatar da HINDATU shi Kuma Dan uwanshi Abdullahi ya yadu da wata nutsasiyar yarinya HAUWA'U a anan unguwar filin samji.

Cikin ikon Allah ba'a dauki lokaci ba akayi aurensu inda aka siyanmasu gidaje anan cikin KASTINA .

_BAYAN_  _AURANSU_  _DA_ _SHEKARA_ _26__

A lokacin Alhaji UMAR HASHIM UMAR ya Kara zama babban mutum Yana da yara guda 3 USMAN, BISHIR, sai auta SAFIYYA.

Daga su haihuwa ta tsayamasu.


Hajiya HINDATU ta Kara zama babbar mace tana rayuwa da yaranta guda uku cikin jindadi da annashuwa.


Alhaji ABDULLAHI shima Yana zaune da matar shi HAUWA'U lafiya da yaransu biyar IBRAHIM shi ne babba sai mai bimasa YUSUF wato sunan mahifin Alhaji ABDULLAHI kenan.sai UBAIDULLAH,FATIMA da karamarsu BILKISU.


A lokacin USMAN Yana da shekara 25, BISHIR nada 22,sau autarsu SAFIYYA tana da 18 years.


Inda USMAN yake Aji karshe A B U ZARIYA Yana karanta LAWYER saboda ya ce gwmma ya sauya course saboda Babanshi BUSINESS ADMINISTRATION ya karanta.
 
Shiko BISHIR Yana aji 2 inda shi ya gaji mahaifinshi yake karantar business administration.

    Itakuwa Autah  little      
 SAFIYYA a wannan lokacin tana S S THREE bording school din AJIWA local government

~BAYAN~ ~SHEKARA~ 22
     _22_ _YEARS_  _LETTERS_
 
Garin ABUJA QUATTERS wani kyakkyawan sairayi ne durkushe a gaban wata mata mai kwarjini da cikar haiba,kamaninta zai nunama Bata tsufaba domin bazata gaza 36 years ba,HAJIYA SAFIYYA KENAN mata ga Alhaji UBA KWANGILA.
        Saurayin da yake durkushe gabanta ya ce "AUNTY Dan Allah ki barni gobe naje KATSINA nan"
                 Ta nisa a hankali tare da maida hankalinta wurinshi ta ce "banaso kana nisa da ni YASIR saboda kaga FARRAH ta koma can shi ne kaima zaka gudu"?
A a AUNTY kinsan bana wuce 2 days saboda aikina.

Tau Allah ya kiyaye hanya Kai gaisheman da su Yaya.tana nifin (ALHAJI USMAN UMAR YAYANTA DA ALHAJI BISHIR UMAR).

tau AUNTY insha Allah zasu ji.

Wannan shi ne asalin tahowar YASIR da ga ABUJA zuwa KATSINA.

Bayan auran Alhaji USMAN UMAR da hajiya MARYAMA,ALLAH ya azurtasu da yara guda 5 AUNTY ZEENAT it's ce babab tana aure a KADUNA unguwar sarki sai mai bimata tana HALIMATUS SA'ADIYYA tana auren wani DOCTOR  suna zaune anan cikin G R A wurin gidan babansu.sai YASIR Wanda a yanzu yake aiki a CBN BANK na ABUJA yana zaune wurin AUNTY SAFIYYA maman FARRAH kenan JABIR yake bimashi sai karaminsu UMAR suna kiranshi da ABBA saboda sunan baban su ALHAJI USMAN aka sanyamashi.

Alhaji BISHIR UMAR yaranshi biyu da ga HUSNAH da take aure a ABUJA sai auta NUSAIBA da take da 10 years.

HAJIYA SAFIYYA diyar ta daya wato FARRAH.
saboda ta dade Bata samu haihuwa ba bayan auranta da Alhaji UBAH,sai bayan shekara 10 da ga ita Basu Kara samuba.

Wannan shi ne takaitaccen tarihin familyn su ALHAJI BISHIR UMAR Wanda yake zaune a BARHIM cikin garin KATSINA.

            ____________

YASIR ingarman namiji ne kyakkyawan gaske dukda ba fari bane sosai Amma Yana da diri tafiyarshi ma abin kallo ce Kuma Broad minded ne.uwa uba Kuma idanunshi masu kaman na Yan chaina 🤣🤣 hakan yasa FARRAH Bata San hada ido da shi saboda Yana sanyata faduwar gaba jijiyoyin jikinta duk suyi sanyi,shi Kuma hakan yasa ya ke cemata MUNAFIKA saboda duk abinda zai mata Bata kallan idanunshi saboda ta ce Yana da ido irin na UMMAHN shi saboda ita UMMAH MARYAMA haifafiyar fulanin BAUCHI ce.

FARRAH ta gudo KATSINA ne saboda Bata San zamanta da YAYA YASIR wuri daya .domin shi a lokacin bai Dade da fara aiki a CBN BANK na ABUJA ba,Yana takurata sosai dan inhar yananan to baya barinta zuwa ko ina.hakan yasa da taga Bata komai saboda lokacin ta gama secondary School ta hada kayanta ta ce ma AUNTY SAFIYYA a kaita KASTINA hutu dakyar ABBANTA ya amince saboda Yana kaunarta baya so tayi nesa da shi ..

To shi ne asalin tahowar ta Bata Kara komawa ba har DADY BISHIR ya sanyata a ALKALAM UNIVERSITY yanzu tana second semester a shekarta ta farko.da farko direver yake kaita Amma ta tada balli saida aka Bata key din motar take zuwa da kanta.

YASIR baya cikakken sati 2 ba tare da yazo KATSINA ba hakan yasa UMMAN shi takeyimashi fada kullun Yana faman yawo kaman Wanda ya aje mata🤣😜.


HAkan yasa FARRAH take Kara jin ta tsaneshi saboda yawan zuwa da yakeyi,Kuma dan gulma idan ya zo baya zama a gidansu sai gidan dady BISHIR.( 🤔 Ka ji su FARRAH harda su gori sai ka ce itama gidansu ne.

Kaman daman jiranshi takeyi ya ta fara fada,baka sati 2 baka zo garinnan ba Kai ba mata ka aje a garin ba,Amma baka iya zama kayi aikinka.

Kamo hannunta yayi ya ce "Haba UMMANA kiyi hakuri mu gaisa Mana" 

Bayan ta zauna kan daya daga cikin kujerun dake falo,kannan ya samu damar zama kasan kafet din ya tankwashe kafafunshi Yana kallanta .

Ya ce" afuwan  UMMANA"Yana hade hannuwanshi wuri daya alamar ban hakuri .

Shafa kanshi tayi ta ce"Allah ya albarkaci rayuwarka da sauran yan'uwanka.

Ameen UMMAN MU Allah ya karamaku nisan kwana.

Amen Amma kasan me zakayi dan kaga farincikina .

Tau UMMAH kuyi hakuri adan karaman lokaci insha Allah komai zai daidata.

Tau Allah yarda ka jira ABBANKU ya fito ku gaisa .

Dai dai ta fita da ga falon ya amsa da "To".


Jima kadan ABBAH ya fito ya gaisheshi cikin girmamawa.

Ya amsa cikin sakin fuska.

Ya Kara da ce wa duk naji irin maganar da kukayi da UMMANKU,karka damu MAGAJI na gano matsalarka Amma Insha Allahu komai ya zo karshe.tashi ka tafi ni zan fita cikin gari.

Haka ya tashi sororo kamar Wanda kwai ya fashewa a ciki.

Yayima su UMMAH da kannanshi jabir da umar sallama ya nufi BARHIM.


           ______________

__ASALINSU_

Asalin iyayensu haifaffun  garin DUTSIMMA ne daya da cikin local government din jahar KATSINA STATE. Sunan mahaifinsu Alhaji UMAR HASHIM UMAR.
       

A wannan lokacin Alhaji UMAR HASHIM UMAR duka iyayenshi sun rasu Yana zaunene a hannun Kanan mahaifinshi  wato Alhaji YUSUF  UMAR matar shi ta rike Alhaji UMAR HASHIM UMAR,Anan yayi dukkanin karatunshi tunda ga matsayin primary School aka kaishi bording school BINDAWA.  Bayan ya gama Alhaji YUSUF HASHIM  ya hadashi da Yan uwanshi guda biyu ya kaisu B U K KANO anan kowanne ya kammala digreen shi na farko.bayan sun dawo gida kowanne aka bashi kasuwancinshi a hannu shi Alhaji UMAR HASHIM UMAR aka budemashi shago a cikin centeral market cikin garin katsinan dukko shida Dan Alhaji YUSUF din ya kamamasu gida a katsina a cikin unguwar kofar dirbi sukaci gaba da harkokin kasuwancinsu.

Daman shi Alhaji UMAR HASHIM UMAR shi kadai iyayenshi suka haifa a wannan lokacin.

Cikin ikon Allah business dinsu ya bunkasa musamman shi Alhaji UMAR HASHIM UMAR saboda an hadamashi da dukiyar da iyayenshi suka barmashi bayan sun rasu.

A bangare daya Kuma Yana soyayya da masoyiyarshi wato  HINDATU itama haifafiyar garin DUTSIMMA ce babanta Yana koyarwa a G. D .S.S DUTSIMMA .

             _BAYAN_ _SHEKARA_ _DAYA_

Saida Alhaji umar ya tabbatar sun mayar da hankalinsu akan business dinsu kafin ya fara yimasu maganar aure.inda Alhaji UMAR HASHIM UMAR ya gabatar da HINDATU shi Kuma Dan uwanshi Abdullahi ya yadu da wata nutsasiyar yarinya HAUWA'U a anan unguwar filin samji.

Cikin ikon Allah ba'a dauki lokaci ba akayi aurensu inda aka siyanmasu gidaje anan cikin KASTINA .

     _22_ _YEARS_  _LETTERS_
 
Garin ABUJA QUATTERS wani kyakkyawan sairayi ne durkushe a gaban wata mata mai kwarjini da cikar haiba,kamaninta zai nunama Bata tsufaba domin bazata gaza 36 years ba,HAJIYA SAFIYYA KENAN mata ga Alhaji UBA KWANGILA.
        Saurayin da yake durkushe gabanta ya ce "AUNTY Dan Allah ki barni gobe naje KATSINA nan"
                 Ta nisa a hankali tare da maida hankalinta wurinshi ta ce "banaso kana nisa da ni YASIR saboda kaga FARRAH ta koma can shi ne kaima zaka gudu"?
A a AUNTY kinsan bana wuce 2 days saboda aikina.

Tau Allah ya kiyaye hanya Kai gaisheman da su Yaya.tana nifin (ALHAJI USMAN UMAR YAYANTA DA ALHAJI BISHIR UMAR).

tau AUNTY insha Allah zasu ji.

Wannan shi ne asalin tahowar YASIR da ga ABUJA zuwa KATSINA.

Bayan auran Alhaji USMAN UMAR da hajiya MARYAMA,ALLAH ya azurtasu da yara guda 5 AUNTY ZEENAT it's ce babab tana aure a KADUNA unguwar sarki sai mai bimata tana HALIMATUS SA'ADIYYA tana auren wani DOCTOR  suna zaune anan cikin G R A wurin gidan babansu.sai YASIR Wanda a yanzu yake aiki a CBN BANK na ABUJA yana zaune wurin AUNTY SAFIYYA maman FARRAH kenan JABIR yake bimashi sai karaminsu UMAR suna kiranshi da ABBA saboda sunan baban su ALHAJI USMAN aka sanyamashi.

Alhaji BISHIR UMAR yaranshi biyu da ga HUSNAH da take aure a ABUJA sai auta NUSAIBA da take da 10 years.

HAJIYA SAFIYYA diyar ta daya wato FARRAH.
saboda ta dade Bata samu haihuwa ba bayan auranta da Alhaji UBAH,sai bayan shekara 10 da ga ita Basu Kara samuba.

Wannan shi ne takaitaccen tarihin familyn su ALHAJI BISHIR UMAR Wanda yake zaune a BARHIM cikin garin KATSINA.

YASIR ingarman namiji ne kyakkyawan gaske dukda ba fari bane sosai Amma Yana da diri tafiyarshi ma abin kallo ce Kuma Broad minded ne.uwa uba Kuma idanunshi masu kaman na Yan chaina 🤣🤣 hakan yasa FARRAH Bata San hada ido da shi saboda Yana sanyata faduwar gaba jijiyoyin jikinta duk suyi sanyi,shi Kuma hakan yasa ya ke cemata MUNAFIKA saboda duk abinda zai mata Bata kallan idanunshi saboda ta ce Yana da ido irin na UMMAHN shi saboda ita UMMAH MARYAMA haifafiyar fulanin BAUCHI ce.

FARRAH ta gudo KATSINA ne saboda Bata San zamanta da YAYA YASIR wuri daya .domin shi a lokacin bai Dade da fara aiki a CBN BANK na ABUJA ba,Yana takurata sosai dan inhar yananan to baya barinta zuwa ko ina.hakan yasa da taga Bata komai saboda lokacin ta gama secondary School ta hada kayanta ta ce ma AUNTY SAFIYYA a kaita KASTINA hutu dakyar ABBANTA ya amince saboda Yana kaunarta baya so tayi nesa da shi ..

To shi ne asalin tahowar ta Bata Kara komawa ba har DADY BISHIR ya sanyata a ALKALAM UNIVERSITY yanzu tana second semester a shekarta ta farko.da farko direver yake kaita Amma ta tada balli saida aka Bata key din motar take zuwa da kanta.

YASIR baya cikakken sati 2 ba tare da yazo KATSINA ba hakan yasa UMMAN shi takeyimashi fada kullun Yana faman yawo kaman Wanda ya aje mata🤣😜.


HAkan yasa FARRAH take Kara jin ta tsaneshi saboda yawan zuwa da yakeyi,Kuma dan gulma idan ya zo baya zama a gidansu sai gidan dady BISHIR.( 🤔 Ka ji su FARRAH harda su gori sai ka ce itama gidansu ne.

BISMILLAHI RAHMANIR RAHIM

🅿️5
        📝 A washe garin ranar lahadi Yasir ne yake shiryawa,sauri yakeyi yanaso ya ga ya shiga Abuja dawuri.


Karfe 10:00 ya nufi farta din Momy dan yayi sallama da su. Blac blue din boyan ne a jikinshi mai santsi da sheki,yasha dinki irin na samarin zamani.sai milk din hula daya dora tare da takalmimshi hap cover.wayanshi yake dannawa Yana takunshi mai cike da natsuwa.

Dai dai shiga kofar falon yayi sallama Yana duba agogon dake daure a tsintsiyar hannunshi.

Shiru ya ji falon ba kowa sai Karan palisma da take kunne.

A hankali yayi sallama ,ya nufi bisa daya da ga kujerun falon.

A ta fito tana taku, kiching ta nufa da waya sakale a kunnenta da alama waya takeyi, Saboda yanda take ta faman doka murmushi.

Cikin nutsuwa ya kuramata ido yanda take tafiya kaman mai jin tsoran karta taka wani abu.

Cup ta dauko a hannunta ta nufi daninng,bata bar wayan da takeyi ba har yanzu.

Duk sai yaji ranshi ya Kara baci.

A ranshi ya ce "wannan yarinyar ta gama rainaman hankali wato bazata gaisheni ba saboda yanzu tana kallan na zama sa'anta?"

Daga murya yayi da dan karfi ya ce " *ke* *zonan* ".

Saura kadan wayan ta kufce da ga hannunta Allah yada tayimata kyakkyawan ruko.

Da sauri ta juyo tana mayar da numfashi da sauri da sauri.Ita dai harga Allah ta tsorata batayi tinanin akwai mutum cikin falon ba.

A hankali ta mike tsaye,cikin sanyin jiki ta nufi wurinshi kanta a kasa.

Dan nesa da shi ta tsugunna akan kafet din.

Ba tare da ta kalleshi ba ta ce "Ina kwana".

Yi yayi kaman bai ji ba,Yana ta faman danna tamfatsetsiyar wayanshi mai kyau kirar iPhone plus.

Tafi 3 minutes a durkushe dan kafanta harta fa ra yin sanyi,yatsine fuska tayi tare da dan zakudawa gefe daya.A ranta ta ce "mugu sai ya bataman rai kamin ya tafi".

Duk abinda takeyi Yana kallanta ta kasan eyes balls dinsa.

Cikin sanyin voice dinshi ba tare da ya dago da ga kallan screen din wayanshi ba ya ce "Kawoman tea kadi nasha" .

Da "to ta amsa" ta nufi daninng dan kawomashi.

Momy ce ta fito da ga dakinta tana ce wa"A a magaji badai harka shirya ba"?

Sai a sannan ya dago,zamowa kasa yayi Yana gaisheta.

Cikin girmamawa ya ce "Momy Ina kwana".

"Lafiya lau magaji kayi breakfast ko?".
Ta fada tana kallan fuskashi.

"A a yanzu Beby zata Kawoman "

"Ok tau ba damuwa dan banso ka fita baka karya ba saboda tafiyar da nisa".

Kiching din ta nufa daidai Farrah ta fito da babban tire a hannunta.

"A Farrah kin tashi kenan"?

Dan murmushinta tayi har dimple dinta ya lotsa,Ta ce "na tashi Momy bari na kaimasa saina zo mu gaisa",tayi gaba.

Ba tare da ta kalleshi ba ta ajiye kusa da kafafunshi, ta juya .

Kaman saukan aradu ta ji muryanshi yana ce wa "give me your phone ".

Sororo tayi ba tare da ta juyo ba, saboda yanda taji kirjinta ta amsa da karfi kaman an doramata karfe mai nauyi.

"You no hearing me"?.

A hankali ta juyo tare da Mika ma shi wayan.

Amsa yayi ba tare da ya Kara magana ba.

Da sauri ta juya dakinta saboda wani kuka da ta ji Yana Shirin kufcemata.



Kasa ya sauka tare da fara kurban tea dinshi cikin nutsu Yana jinshi kaman zai rabu da wani abu mai mahimmanci a rayuwanshi.


Sosai zuciyarshi ta fara tariyomai abubuwan da suka faru a wasu lokutta da suka shude.wanda a duk time din daya Tina ji yake kaman ya kashe kanshi saboda bakinciki .Kuma shi harga Allah tun daga wannan lokacin bai Kara samun nutsuwa ba.


Sosai ya lula duniyar tunanin lokacin Yana Kan ganuwar kuruciyarshi,Wanda ko ni kaina bansan me yake baruwa ba a wannnan lokacin 🤣🤣😜😜.

Maganar Momy ta dawo da shi da ga duniyar da ya lula.

A hankali ya goge hawayan da ya ji suna silalowa bisa kumatunshi ba tare da ya bari Momy ta lura ba.


"Wai ni Magaji me yake San samunka eh"?.

"Wallahi Momy kaina yake saraman"

"Tau ka karasa break din sai ka sha magani,saboda bakayi tafiya Kai na ciwo ba".

Wayar da ta dauki tsuwa ne ta sakashi juyawa da sauri,sai yanzu ya tina da ya anshi wayam Beby.

Screen din wayan ya duba da sauri,dan ganin mai kiran.

MY SPECIAL ya ga an rubuta a saman screen din.

Sosai ya ji zuciyanshi na beating,kaman zata fasa kirjinshi.

Wurin da Momy take ya kalla,ya ga hankalinta nakan TV.

A hankali ya Danna reject yayi switch off na wayan gaba daya.

Ya ce "Momy na koshi bari na wuce kawai saboda na ga rana tana Kara yi".

"Tau Magaji Allah ya kiyaye hanya ka gaishe da Auntyn taku".

Da sauri ya fice da ga falon dankar ta fahinci abinda ya ke faruwa.

            ________________

Farrah kuwa tana shiga dakinta da gudu ta fada bisa faffadan gadanta mai kyau.


Kife kanta tayi tare da fashewa da kuka sosai ta ke shashheka.

Ita Bata San meta tsaremashi ba ya tsaneta ,kwata-kwata bayasan ya ganta cikin walwala sai ya yu abinda zai sanyata kuka.

Banda wulakanci duk irin cutarta da yayi a shekarun baya bai isheshi ba sai ya Kara tura mata wani bakin cikin.

Tafi minti 10 tana wannan kukan ,kamin ta gaji dan kanta ta shiga toilet ta wanke fuskarta.

Rigar jikinta ta sauya zuwa doguwar milk din riga mai dogon hannu ta koma kangadon tayi tagumi,tare da lilawa duniyar tunani.

Da karfi ta ce "IMPOSSIBLE!!!"
Tana share zafafan hawayan da suke bin farar fuskarta.

Ta ce "wallah da sake dan bazan yarda ba idan Dady ya dawo dole na sanar masa da wannan Sirrin dake addabar zuciyata".......

BISMILLAHI RAHMANIR RAHIM
🅿️6
     📝 A takaice Farrah duk yinin wannan rana a cikin kunci tayi shi .

Kin fitowa tayi daga dakinta,Har saida Momy taga shiru bata da niyar fitowa,kannan ta leka dankinta.
Har zuwa wannan lokacin Farrah a kwance take.
"Farrah wai yau meyake damunki duk yinin yau na ganki cikin damuwa,?Dan Ina tinanin ko break banga kinyi ba".

Tashi zaune tayi tana kokarin gyara hular kanta.

"Walllahi Momy banjin dadin jikina ne,Amma yanzu zan fito insha Allah".

"Tau ki fito ki ci abinci sai kisha magani ko"?
Cewar Momy ta juya zuwa kiching dan ta karasa girkin da ta dora.

" Da addu'a a bakinta  bakinta  ta shiga toilet ta fara kokarin yin wanka,sosai take durzar fatar jikinta kamar mai shirin sauya colour jikinta.

Bayan ta fito cikin nitsuwa take shiryawa,riga ta saka orange colour ta material doguwar riga ce hap gount.gashin kanta tayi parking  a tsakiyar kanta,Daurin dankwali ta murza yayi kyau sosai domin ya zauna das a kanta. Duk da ba wata kwalliya tayi sosai ba amma tayi kyau.

Tana fitowa ta  tadda Yaya Jabir a dinning table,Bata yi mamaki ba dan ya saba irin haka zuwa da sassafe.Har ta ji sanyi a ranta,dan shi Yaya Jabir baya da matsala jininsu ya hadu,Koda tana yarinya idan har suka zo katsina tana makale da shi.

Da sauri ta karasa gurinshi da murmushi akan fuskarta.

"Ah Yaya Jabir Kai ne yau  a gidanmu " ?tana kama bakinta alamar mamaki.

Kallanta yakeyi sosai yanda jajayen labbanta suke motsawa gwanin sha'awa.

"Oh! ni Yaya Jabir irin wannan kallo haka kamar na sauya kamanni"?

Kujerar kusa da shi ta zauna ta riko hannunshi,kayi hakuri ba laifina ne ba wallahi Yaya Yasir ne ya hanani fita ko'ina.

Hannunshi ya yi kokarin zarewa daga nata Yana kallanta ya ce "ai nasan ba laifinki ne ba,shiyasa ni na zo".

"Tau nagode daka fahimce ni, Ina kwana ? Ya su Ummah?.

"Duk suna lafiya lau"

Plate din da yake gabanshi ya turamata,mu ci abinci dan nasan Baki ci ba ko? Ya yi tambayar yana tsareta da ido.

Zunburo bakinta tayi alamar shagwaba, "wallahi kaman ka sa ni banyi break ba".

"Ja'ira aiko yanzu zanyi maki dura,dan haka gwamma ki ci da yawa,"oya maza ki shanye wannan, Yana turamata cup din kakkauran tea gabanta.

Sosai ya bata dariya har fararan hakoranta suka fito,ta ce wallahi har ka tinaman lokacin kana yiman duren abinci".

"Ai lokacin bakya san cin abinci".


Abinci suka ci gaba da ci cikin nitsuwa,da annashuwa.sosai suke kama duk da ta fishi haske da dogon hanci.

           _____________

Yinin wannan ranar cikin farin ciki tayi shi,sai ta ji kamar bata da wata damuwa a rayuwarta.Dan girkima tare suka shiga kiching ya tayata sukayi.

Momy kuwa tuni tana bangaranta Nusaiba kuwa tana Auwal direver ya kaita islamiyya.


Bayan ya dawo daga masallacin magriba,dakinta ya nufa knocking  ya yi.Budewa tayi ganin shine ta ce" har ka dawo kenan"

"Eh ki zo muyi denner ni wucewa zanyi"

Ciki ta koma ta cire hijaf din da tayi sallah ta fito.

Suna cikin cin abinci Momy ta fito da waya a hannunta ta mikawa Farrah, "Anshi Auntyn ku ce ta kira watanki a kashe".

Ita harga Allah sai yanzu ta tina da batun wayanta da zalimu ya ansa,shaf ta manta saboda zuwan Yaya Jabir ya daukemata kewa sosai.

Karawa tayi a kunnanta tare da yin sallama.

"Amen wa'alaikas salam Farrah Ina kika saka wayanki inata kiranki swich off".

Ta danyi shiru saboda ta rasa abida zata ce,sai wata dibara ta fadomata tayi saurin ce wa
"Aunty wayan matsala ta samu sai na ajeta".


"To farrah da kin fadama yayanki tun kafin ya taho,Amma ba matsala ki ba matar Yaya wayar" (tana nufin Momy).

Da sauri ta Mika wayan saboda yadda ta ji kirjinta na bugawa da karfi .

Momy daki ta nufa tana ci gaba da waya da maman Farrah.

Jabir kuri ya yi Yana kallanta , kamar mai San gano wani abu,saboda yana kokwanto akan abinda ya ji ta fada.

Kara sadda kanta ta yi tana jujjuya cokalin hannunta.
Ji tayi yana ce wa "Farrah abinda kika fada gaskiya ne kuwa?. Ya kafeta da idanunshi.

Sosai Yaya Jabir yake yimata kwarjini , Amma duk da haka tana ji a zuciyarta bazata iya sanar da shi ba Wanda ya anshe wayanta ba.

Hmmmm "ni zan wuce" Yana kokarin mikewa tsaye ya tattara wayoyinshi ya zuba a Ajjihun wandon juens dinshi .

Tare suka jero zuwa inda yayi parking din motar shi,Bayan ya zauna a cikin motar ya Kai dubansa inda take tsaye,ya ce "wallahi ki daina tsoransa,ya anshe maki waya amma bazaki iya fadi ba".

"A a Yaya Jabir ba haka ne ba"

"Matsa daga nan ko na mintile bakinnan mai ciwa,Amma idan Yaya Yasir ne sai kiyi kaman ruwa ya cinye ko".

Ita dai Bata Kara ce wa komai ba sai wasa takeyi da zoben hannunta.

"Ni na wucesai Allah ya kaimu next week"

"Tau Allah kiyaye Nima zanzo insha Allah"

"Allah yarda" cewar zabir da yake kokarin fita daga gidan.

Ta dade tsaye a wurin har aka budemashi gate ya fita,kafin ta samu damar Jan kafafuwanta da sukayimata sanyi zuwa cikin gida.

🅿️7
       📝A takaice Yasir sai karfe 3:00 am Yasir ya isa Abuja.Da gajiya sosai ya sauka,ga wata yunwa da take addabar sa.Domin kuwa tunda ya taho a kaduna kawai ya tsaya yayi salla.

Detect a part din shi ya yada zango.

Aunty safiya bata san ya karaso  ba,tun a hanya da suka yi waya ya sanar da ita tasowar shi.A gurguje ya shirya ya sauya kayanshi zuwa riga da wando color milk masu taushi.jakar kayan shi ya janyo,wayar Farrah yayi cin karo da ita,budeta yayi anyi sa'a kuwa ba security a kanta.sosai ya hau binciken abubuwan da suke Kan wayar,da text ya fara cin karo wanda Hasan ya turo mata tun ranar nan.
Yayi ta karanta sakon yafi sau biyar,Yana mamaki a cikin zuciyar shi,a fili ya firta "Tabbas Beby zargi na ya kusa tabbata a kanki,Idan ko haka ne to tabbas  ina cikin tashin hankali ".

Wata nannauyar ajiyar zuciya ya sauke,Ya furzar da iskar bakin shi ya ci gaba da duba WhatsApp dinta.Gani yayi bata da wani groups masu yawa,da ga na family din su sai na Novels din Rukayya Ibrahim lawal(ummu entesar),(takwaranscy).🤣

Sosai yayi ta mamaki ashe tana karanta novels,firarta da wata Bilkisu Abbas ya gani,hirar ta dau hankalin shi.

Ya fara tambayar kanshi "wannan wata shedaniyar yarinya ce?,shiyasa yaga dabi'un Bebyn shi duk sun fara sauyawa.

Da karfi ya furta " *Ya* *Allah*! " tare da dafe barin kanshi na dama da hannun shi,saboda ji yayi yana Sarama shi da karfi.

Wayar shi dake gefen shi yaga tayi haske,alamar Kira ya shigo.Janyo ta yayi yana dubawa,Aunty Safiyya ce take kiran shi.

Mikewa yayi tsam ya nufi site dinta ba tare da yayi picking call din ba.

Tana zaune a falo yayi sallama,tare da zunawa kasan kafet din wurin.

"Aunty Ina yini"?

"Lafiya lau Yasir,Ya kabaro su"?

"Lafiya lau duk sun ce na gaishe ku".

"Muna amsawa,naga shiru baka zo ka ci abinci ba"?.

"Na dan tsaya duba abu ne a system dina ".

"Ok ba damuwa,idan ka idar da cin abincin za miyi magana anjima,ni zan shiga ciki Alhaji ya kusa dawo wa". Ta haura saman benen falonta.


A hankali ya nufi dinning,cikin nitsuwa ya fara bude warmas din,Tuwon shinkafa ne da miyar danyar kubewa taji busashshen kifi da dandakwashi,Tuni ya ji yawun bakin shi ya tsinke  sosai yake san irin abincinnan.A plate ya zuba tare da yin Bismillah ya fara cin abinsa(Nima har yasa yawun mouth dina sun tsinke 😋😋).

         __________________

A bangaran Farrah ko dakyar ta karasa shiga cikin falon,saboda yadda kafafunta sukayi sanyi ga kewar Yaya Jabir Dan ita harga Allah bata so ya tafi ba,ji take kaman ta bishi ya yanzu take ji haushin rashin waya da ko chat ta yi.

Ji tayi kirjinta yayi wani irin bugawa,da sauri ta dafe kanta tana firta "Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraju'un" 
"Na bani ni Farrah,Ya zanyi idan Zalimu yaga texs din da Hasan ya turoman na manta banyi delete ba".

Sosai ta tsorata har kana iya hango damuwa karara akan fuskarta.

Karasa wa tayi dakinta,hannuwan ta duka biyu ta dora bisa saman kant,ta sulale a kasan tayis din dakin,wasu hawaye masu zafi ta ji suna bin kuncinta .

A zuciyar ta ta ce "Tun ban aikata ba na fara samun matsala? Ina ga a ce n aikata"Da sau ta furta "Astagfirullah 3" Ya Allah na tuba tana mai daga hannuwan ta sama.

Hawayen fuskar ta ta goge,ta dauro Arwalla tare da kabbara sallar isha'i.

       _________________

Yasir yana kammala cin abincin fita yayi,shi ne bai dawo ba gidan ba sai bayan sallar isha'i. Part din Aunty Safiyya ya nufa,A main falo ya risketa ita da Alhaji ubah suna kallan news a tashar NTA 2.

Sallama yayi yana kokarin zama saman kafet din.
Alhaji Ubah ya ce "A a dana ka zauna bisa kujera,nan gidan ku ne amma kayi ta abu kaman bako eh? fisabilillahi ai Kai dan gida ne ko?.

"Hakane Alhaji nagode Allah ya saka da alkhairi"

"Ameen cewar" Alhaji Ubah.

Bayan sun amsa Aunty Safiyya ta dora da cewa "Bayan ka taho nayi ta Kiran wayar Farrah bata shiga Abban ta yake so yayi magana da ita amma wayanta switch off,sai ta Momyn ta na Kira,Bayan ta bata take cemin wayan ta samu matsala".

"Ai ko bata fadaman ba,da tun kafin na taho na siyanma ta sabuwa,Amma ba matsala nexs week zan je na kaima ta insha Allah".

"To ba damuwa Yasir Allah yayi albarka" cewar Aunty Safiyya".

Alhaji uba ya anshe zancen da cewar "A a dana ba za'ayi haka ba ka kyaleta tunda sakaci gareta idan ta dade ba wayan na siyan mata wata,kila ta rinka adana wa".

"A a Alhaji ba komai zan kaimata insha Allah".
Cewar Yasir yana mikewa tsaye.
Sai da safen ku.

"Tau ba komai dana mungode y yayi maka albarka".

"Amen ya Allah".

Bayan fitar shi Alhaji Uba ya kalli matar shi cikin so da kauna juna ya ce "wallahi Safiyya yarannan yana birge ni ba ruwan shi,Yana da nitsuwa".

"Hakane kam shi baya da yawan magana irin na Jabir shi yasa wasu suke kallan kaman Jabir ya fishi San mutane".

"Haka ne Allah ya Kara shirya mana zuri'a "Yana kiyasta wani abu a ranshi.

"Amen ya Allah" cewar Hajiya Safiyya.

🅿️10
       📝Cikin takun birgewa yake tafiya,ga alamun nutsuwa a tattare da shi.yana karasawa gaf da bakin kofar falon ya ji kaman alamun sautin dariya.dan Kara kanshi kadan yayi jikin kofar kasa-kasa yake jin muryar mutanan Amma ya kasa gane su wanene? saboda baya jin su sosai.
A hankali ya murda kofar Yana  tura kanshi cikin falon.Turus yayi lokacin da idanuwan shi suka yi arba da Wanda suke zaune bisa kujerun falon.

A ranshi ya ce "Jabir Kuma meya? kawo shi a irin wannan time din"? Yasan ba  mai ba shi amsar wannan tambayar sai ita.
Su Jabir kuwa da Farrah hirar su suke tayi tana kwasar dariya,Har ta ji zuciyar ta tayi sakal kaman wancan zalimun bai bata mata raiba.dan shiyasa take Kara San Yaya Jabir koba koma Yana saka ta dariya.

Yasir lura yayi da basu San da ruwan tsirar shi a wurin ba,hakan yasa yaji ranshi ya Kara baci,bai san lokacin da ya  yi tsalle b ya dira gaban su ba.kawai sai ganin mutum suka yi tsidim a gaban su yana huci kaman zaki.sosai Farrah ta tsora ta ganin irin kallan da yake yi masu kaman zai duke su.cikin sauri ta runtse idanuwan ta.Jabir kuwa bawan Allah kokarin dago fiskarta yake yi Amma ta kiya.kara cusa fuskar take yi a kirjin shi a kokarin ta na San ganin ta boye.

Yasir ya Kai makura wurin kulewa,Dan haka cikin fushi ya daka masu tsawa tare da cewa" Jabir are you a mad"?.

Da sauri Jabir ya dago kanshi jin bro dinshi yayi ma shi wannan furucin.Haba bro meya faru ka dau zafi har haka?,Dan Allah idan laifi ta yima kayi hakuri kada ka ce zaka duke ta ka ji mai bro Yana mai hade hannuwan shi wuri guda 👏 alamar ban hakuri.

"Au tambaya ta kake yi saboda tsabar rainin hankali ko?,Duk sallamar da na rinka dokawa ku ka ki amsawa kuna ta faman gaggaba dariya kaman kunzo gidan sakarkaru ehh?, Kai Wai bari ma in tambaye ka meya kawoka nan da wannan daren eyeee?k
Sosai Jabir ya hau mamakin bro dinna shi yanda ya zage Yana ta zazzaga masifa kaman anmashi wani babban laifi.Cikin kwantar da murya ya ce "Dan Allk kayi hakuri wallahi Sam bamu ji ba"A hankali ya samu ya janye Farrh daga jikin shi Yana ce mata matar  kema ki ba shi hakuri kin ji".
Cikin nutsuwa ta furta "kayi hakuri" tana Kara boyewa bayan Jabir.

Kar ku so kuga yadda fuskar Jabir ta koma saboda Tsakanin bacin rai😔.
 Tas yake kallan su ba tare da ya Kara furta ko a ba kawai ya juya.Mutuwar tsaye yayi lokacin da yayi arba da Momy a tsaye tana kallan su.sosai yaji hantar cikin sa ta kada saboda tsoratar da yayi a zuciyar shi ya ce "Yau nidai na bani".

A hankali Momy ta nuna mai hanyar fita daga falon da hannunta ba tare da ta ce uffan ba.yana sadda Kai kasa ya fita kaman marar gaskiya.

Hankalin ta ta mayar wurin su Jabir ta ce "Farrah zo nan".
A  hankali ta karasa wurinta tana mai rungume ta hawaye ke ta ambakiya daga idanuwan ta. Bedroom dinta ta nufa da ita ba tare da ta kalli inda Jabir yake ba.

Sororo yayi Yana mamakin meke Shirin faruwa haka?
Fita yayi ya ja motar shi da mugun gudu yabar gidan,Zuciyar shi cike da tunani barkatai.

A hakali Momy ta zaunar da ita bakin bed,Ta ce ya kamata kisan me kike yi saboda yanzu ke ba yarinya ba ce,Kuma insha Allah idan Dadyn ku da dawo Komai zai daidai ta.anan ta fita ta barta zuwa dakinta.


Ita kuwa Farrah anan takonta zuciya ta cike da tunanin  maganganun Momy suka dosa amma ta kasa gano ko daya. 

       *_Bari_* *_mu_* *_leko_* *_Yasir_* 😂🤣

            -----------------------
Tafiya yake yana hada hanya saboda wani duhu da ya mamaye gaban dakyar ya karasa falon shi ya fada bisa doguwar kujerar yana mayar da numfashi kaman Wanda yayi gasar tsere,hannun shi daya ya aza bisa goshin shi Yana cik leban shi na kasa idanun shi ya Kara runtsewa saboda shi kadai ya san yadda yake ji.abin mamaki hawaye na gani 😭 suna bin gefen fuskar shi.Da kyar Yana laliban hanya ya koma bedroom dincik anan bisa kafet din gaban gabon ya zube yana jujjuya kanshi hannun shi daya bisa cikin shi,sosai yake mirgina wa bisa kafet din sunan Allah kawai yake ambata a cikin  bakin shi.

Karan waya yake ji yana tashi a slow dakyar ya rarrafa ya saka hannu ya janyo ta Wayar Beby ce ya manta bai kashe ta ba,kallan screen din wayan yake yi sunan Wanda yaga texs din ranar shi ne ya ke Kira again.
Swich off din ta yayi gaba daya saboda ji yake bazai iya picking call din ba.A ranshi ya ce wannan yau so suke su kashe ni.(hhhhh  Yasir karka sare tun yanzu mana😂😂).

A bangaran Momy kuwa, waya naga ta dauka tana Kiran wata nomber,jim kadan aka yi picking Bayan tayi sallama ta ce Umman Jabir kina jina gobe idan Allah ya kaimu zan kawo Farrah nan wurinki dan gaskiya in har Baban Nusaiba ya dawo zan fuskanci matsala gwamma ayita ta kare, Dan bazan bari ku sanya ni a tsaka mai wuya ba nasan da wani abu ya faru cewa za'ayi sakaci na ne gwamma asan abinyi kawai .

BISMILLAHI RAHMANIR RAHIM


🅿️8
        📝Bayan kwana biyu,Farrah abin duniya duk ya dameta saboda ita dai harga Allah rabata da wayan da yayi ji take kaman ya cire wani bargo na jikin ta.

Wani tinani ya fado a ranta,tsam ta mike da ga kwanciyar da tayi bisa doguwar kujerar falon ta,takalma ta saka ta dauko hijaf dinta ta nufi hanyar gate.sosai take sauri domin kuwa bata san  Momy ta ganta,wurin Baba Auwal mai gadin gate din ta nufa,zaune yake bisa farar kujerar roba Yana sauraron labaran gidan radio,Yana hango ta ya tashi da saurinsa.
"A Hajiya da kanki kika fito,ko kina bukatar wani abu ne"?.

Karasawa tayi   tana gaishe shi "Ina yini Baba Auwal"?Lafiya lau Hajiya" 
"Am dan Allah Baba so nake yi ka aroman yar karamar wayarka,tawa ta kiyi ne ga shi ina so inyi Kira".

Da saurin shi ya amsa da "To to ba damuwa Hajiya"Dakin shi ya koma ya dauko mata.
"Ga shi bata karasa yin chajin ba,Amma nasan ya isheki har ki gama abinda zakiyi,akwai sauran kati a ciki dan jiya Yusuf ya turoman".

Amsa tayi tana godiya "Yanzu zan dawoma da ita insha Allah".

Bayan gidan ta nufa inda yake da wata yar rumfa kaman ta zana,da fashion na roba masu kyau kalan orange,ciki ta shiga tare da zama Kan daya da ga cikin kujerun, Nomber Hasan ta sanya ta danna call sai da ta kusan cinkewa kamin ya daga,Ba tare da ta bari yayi magana ba ta ce "ka Kira ni ce Farrah" ta katse Kiran dan bata so ta cinyema dan tsohonnan katin shi.

Dan danan ya biyo nomber da ta Kira shi.

Tana dagawa cikin nutusuwar ta furta "Assalamu alaikum sweetyna".

Sosai ya sauke ajiyar zuciya dan kuwa har tana jiyo Jan numdashin da yake.

"Sweety Ina kika shige Ina ta Kiran phone dinki bata shiga, why meya yake faruwa ne"?
"Wallahi matsala aka samu,kasan bazan kashe wayata ba har tsawon kwana hudu,ni kaina duk na damu Ina so inji muryan ka amma ba hali yanzuma wayan Baba Auwal ce na amso duk dan na kiraka".

"Cikin jin haushi ya ce "waye ya anshe wayar taki"?

Shiru ta danyi kafin ta ce "sweety ba fa anshewa aka yi ba,ta lalace ne amma da Dady ya dawo da ga tafiyan da yayi zai siyaman sabuwa".Dan ita har ga Allah a zuciyar ta ta ji ba zata iya fadamai zalimu ne ya anshe mata waya ba,saboda duk da tana San Hasan bata jin dadin yadda yake kushe mata dan uwa,dan ko ba komai Yaya Yasir jininta ne bata da yadda zata yi da shi. (Kai Farrah kodai-kodai kina........🤣🤣ehmmm bari dai inyi shiru kar Jabir ya ji ni 🤭.)

"Ok ai nayi tinanin wannan yayan naki ne ya anshe ta". Amma kina ji na?
"Eh ina jinka sweety na"
"Gobe zan zo in kawo maki sabuwa sai ki dora layin ki,dan kinsan ban jin dadi idan ban ji muryar ji ba".

Hmmm murmushi tayi sosai dan tasan halin rigimar shi."kasan ya za'ayi"?
"A a saikin fada"
"Ka bari basai kar Kawoman waya ba,saboda zan fuskanci matsala idan aka ganni da wata,idan har ba su suka saiman ba,Amma Insha Allah zan rinka aron wannan muna waya kamin su bani wata ka ji ko sweetyna,karka damu ni nasan ba mai rabamu insah Allah"ta karasa fada tana turo karamin bakinta kaman tana gaban shi.

Ajiyar zuciya yayi yana dunkule hannunshi kaman zai naushi wani abu.
"Ba matsala sweetyna na yarda da ke , saboda nasan kina so na".
Daga nan suka ci gaba da firar su irin ta masoya,har sai da ta kwantar mai da hankali kamin suka yi sallama.
Sai yanzu taga dadewar da suka yi suna wayar kusan awa biyu suka shafe.Baba Auwal ta mayar wa da wayar shi ta nufi cikin gidan,Allah ya taimake ta ba Wanda ya ganta momy tana site dinta Nusaiba ko tana makaranta.

          ***************
Duk cikin week dinnan haka al'amura suka yi ta tafiya.inda a bangaran Farrah duk basu yi mata dadi,sai dai godiyar Allah.

Suna zaune a Falo ita da Momy a yammacin ranar juma'a hira suke yi sosai,inda Momy take Kara yimata nasiha akan al'amuran zamani tare da bata shawarwari a gameda 'al'amuran samarin wannan zamanin.Nasihar tana shigarta sosai dan sai yanzu take gane illar da Yaya Yasir yayi kokarin yimata a shekarun baya,Wanda har zuwa wannan lokacin ba Wanda ya sa ni,Daga ita sai shi,sai Kuma Allah.
Sosai ta lula duniyar tunani,inda kunnuwan ta suka daina jin Nasihar da Momy take yimata.

Momy lura tayi kamar hankalin ta baya wurin,ta dan girgiza ta da hannu.
"Farrah tunanin me kike yi haka kinyi shiru"?

Firgigit tayi alamar bata cikin nutsuwa.
"Ah ehm Momy ina jinki"ta na Yan kame -kame alamar rashin askiya.

Momy tsayawa tayi tana kallanta ba tare da ta ce komai ba,har tatsawon wasu mintina.l

"Farrah akwai abinda yake damunki,amma tunda bakya san ki sanar da ni ba komai,Amma ki sa ni ni dai a matsayin diya kike a wajena,kuma Ina iya bakin kokari na dan ganin na nusar dake daga hatsarin dake cikin wannan zamanin".

"Ba komai momy,Ina jin dadin zama da ke,dan ko Aunty bata yiman irin nasihar da kike yiman,nagode Allah ya saka da alkhairi.kuma insha Allah idan Ina da matsala zan sanar maki,duk da yanzu akwai abinda yake damuna amma na barwa zuciya ta"ta karasa fada tana mai rungume Momy ta kwantar da kanta bisa kafadar ta.

"Ba komai Farrah ki ci gaba da addu'a komai zai daidai ta insha Allah".
Sunyi shiru na dan tsawon wani lokaci,sai Farrah da take ta sauke ajiyar zuciya.

Sai daga bisani ta kalli Momy tana ce wa"Dan Allah idan ya zo yau ki amsoman key din mota ta,saboda wannan Monday din hutun mu yake karewa".

"Tau karki damu insha Allah zan amso maki".

"Tau Allah yarda,bari na je na kwanta Ina jin gajiya"ta na mikewa tsaye.

"Kada kiyi bacci yanzu,kinga yammaci ne ba'a son baccin yammaci".

Ta amsa da "To" tana shigewa bedroom dinta.


             * *  ** *** ** ****
Cikin ikon Allah Yasir a wannan ranar ya shigo katsin,sai karfe 8:00 daidai Baba mai gadi ya budema shi gate ya danno hancin motar sa.yana mai cike da farin ciki,Yana fitowa site din Momy ya nufa.Fitowar ta kenan daga kiching hannunta rike da cup tana juya coppe da sporn ,Tana kallan shi ta wuciyar idanun ta ta yi fiska da sauri ta shige bedroom dinta ta sanya key da sauri dan tasan ba wuya ya biyota.Tana sauke ajiyar zuciya ta ce"Dan kwawa duk weekend sai ya zo,duk kudin salaryn  shi anan suke karewa dan rashi aikin yi irin na shi.

Haushi ya ji ya kama shi,juyawa yayi cike da jarumta ya koma part din shi.Wayar Momy ya Kira tana daga wa bayan sun gaisa ya ce"Momy yunwa nake ji,Dan Allah kiba Beby abinci ta Kawoman"

"Tau gatanan,Kuma ka bata key din motar ta da ka amsa".
"Tau Momy zan bata"

Dakin Farrah ta nufa,tana murda kofan ta ji shi a rufe.knocking ta yi.

"Farrah ta ce "waye"

"Ni ce bude kofar Farrah meye kika rufe kofa"?

A hankali ta bude duk da ta ji muryar Momy,Amma a tsorace take.

Tana budewa Momy ta ce"ki zo yanzu za ki kaima yayan ki abincin sa Yana part din shi,daga nan zai baki key din ki".

Ita dai sororo ta yi,saboda yadda ta ji kirjin ta ya amsa.ta rasa abinyi ta ce "Momy ganinan zuwa".

Momy juyawa tayi ta koma kiching din dan shirya abincin a babbabn faranti.ita kuwa farrah tunani take yi ta je ko Kuma ta ki zuwa,har ga Allah tana so ta amso key dinta Kuma Bata iya yiwa Momy gardama.

🅿️12
       📝Bayan rabuwar su derect Barhim ya koma,tunda ya je yana part din shi dan yayi ma kanshi alkawarin bazai Kara shiga site din ba sai idan zai koma Abuja abinci ma ya rinka zuwa restaurant ya siyo .

Momy bayan sun karayin waya da Ummahn su Jabir ta ce tayi hakuri ba sai ta maido Farrah ba saboda ta fadama Abban su matsalar da ke faruwa akan haka suka bar maganar,Dakin Farrah ta nufa dan ganin lafiyar ta dan tasan  halin ta Sarai tana iya ta zama da yunwa.Tana tura kofar dakin ta hango ta a Kan prayer mat ta kudundu ne cikin hijaf kanta a tsakanin cinyoyin ta,cikin sauri Momy ta karasa wurin  dago kanta tayi tana ce wa "Farrah me yake San ya same ki ne eyeh? Ok tau idan na fahimce ki abida ya faru ne yasa kike so ki saka Kan ki a cikin damuwa ko?taso mu je"ta mikar da ita tsaye suka nufi wurin danning,tea ta fara hada mata mai kauri ta sha kafin ta zuba mata jolop din makaroni da ya ji danyun kayan miya da kifi.sosai ta ci saboda daman yunwa na addabar ta kawai bacin ran da zalimu ya saka ta shi ya hanata ci.



           ***************

Jabir ne na hango a gaeding din bayan gidan su da alama waya ce yake yi cikin sanyin voice dinshi yake cewa "Dan Allah aunty ki taimake ni wallahi Ina santa da yawa bazan iya hakura Ina kallo wani ya aure ta ba".
A dayan bangaran na ji ta ce"ka bari Dady  ya dawo za'a san abinda za'a yi insha Allah, karka damu ka ji dan kanina"
"Hmm to ba matsala Aunty nagode Allah ya tabbatar Mana da alkhairin sa".
"Amen kanina nagode,sai mun Kara magana"cewar Aunty Husna.sallama suka yi ya katse wayar Yana jin karfin gyuwa a zuciyar shi.


         ------------------------
A washe garin ranar lahadi misalin karfe 2:00 am na rana jirgin su Dady ya sauka a filin jirgin usman na gwaggo airport katsina.ita Momy Daman tasan da zuwan shi dan haka ta hau shirye shirye dan tarbar mai gidan nata.wayar Yasir ta Kira tana fadama shi ya je ya dauko Dadyn su a Airport sun kusa karaso wa.A lokacin Yana kwance a bedroom din shi abin duniya duk ya dame shi,Hakan yasa ya mike Dan Shirin zuwa dauko Dadyn.

A bangaran Farrah ko tana karasa cin abincin bedroom dinta ta koma saboda ta tuno da wata yar karamar wayar ta da ta dade a aje dan harta manta da ita,wani layin waya naga ta ciro daga cikin yar jikar post dinta ta bude ta saka  kunnanta tayi taga da dan sauran caji a ciki.Dan haka cikin sauri ta saka nomber Hasan ko plashing ne tayi,cikin sa'a kuwa ta ji tayi ringing alamar Kiran ya shiga.Yana dagawa" ta ce sweety ka Kira ni ce ".
"Tau bari na kira" cewar Hasan.
Tana katsewa call din shi ya biyo baya,Cikin azama yayi picking tare da karawa a kunnan ta.A tare suka sauke a jiyar zuciya,Da sauri ya ce "wallahi sweety na kusa biyo ki gidan ku koma mai zai faru sai dai ya faru dan wallahi na gaji da wannan boye-boyen da kike yiman,kamar ba da gaske kike sona ba?.

A hankali ta ja numfashi cikin kasala ta ce "ka yi hakuri ba laifina ne ba,kasan Dady baya barin hira ko? Amma ai cikin week dinnan zamu koma school kaga shikenan zaka rinka ganina ko?ta Kara she maganar tana mai lunshe eyes balls dinta,saboda ita har ga Allah tana San Hasan duk da har yanzu ta kasa gano wani irin kalar so yake yimata,Dan lokutta da dama sai ya rinka yimata maganganu marassa dadin ji,ta fada mai ya daina kunyar shi take ji,Amma sai ya ce ai ZAMANINMU NE yanzu a wannan zamanin idan baka haka kaida budurwa ka ai ba soyayya kuke yi ba,ko Baki ganin yanda kawayen ki su Bilkisu Abbas suke yi?ita dai duk time din da ya ce mata haka sai dai tayi dariya ta ce ni bana ra'ayin yin irin wannan wayar.maganar shi ta dawo da ita.
"Kina jina ko"?

"Eh Ina jinka" cewar Farrah

"Anjima ki samu ki fito mu hadu kin ji"?
"Kayi hakuri ni wallahi bazan iya fita ba Amma bari na kira Bilkisu Abban sai kuzo tare tunda motan ka tintek ne,idan kun zo ita saita shigo sai mu fito tare da ita ko?.

"Tau shikenan zan Kara Kiran ki anjima"
"Tau shikenan" ta katse call din tana mai juyawa bayan ta dan tana jin kamar motsin mutum a cikin dakin.

Wayar take kokarin boyewa saboda mutanan da ta gani tsaye a kanta shiya saka duk tayi saurin rudewa,tana karkarwa ta sanya hannun ta cikin riga ta a kokarin ta na San ganin ta boye karamar wayar.Dady ne a tsaye gefen shi Kuma Yasir ne yayi folding din hannuwan shi a kirjin shi ,gaba daya eyes balls din shi ya zuba mata su. Dady Bishir baiyi mamakin irin wayar da ya ji tana yi ba,shi yasa har suka shigo bata san sun shigo ba.

Cikin fushi Dady ya ce "Ashe duk abinda ake fadaman a kanki Yana nema ya tabbata ko? ,duk kokarin da muke yi a kanki bakya gani ko idan wani abu ya faru da ke mahaifin ki laifin mu zai gani saboda daman baya San zaman ki anan Yana yimana Kara ne kawai".
"To wallahi baki isa ba tunda Baki jin magana zamuyi maganin abin tun kafin kawayen zamani da samari su Kai ga yin nasara a kanki, Dan haka zakiga hukuncin da zai biyo baya,"Yana mai nuna ta da yatsa.kallan shi ya mayar gun Yasir da yayi sororo ya ce "Kai Kuma bababban banza da baka iya tsawatar wa ba,kaima naka hukuncin Yana nan zuwa tunda duk kun raina mu. Har ya Kai bakin kofar fita daga dakin ya juyo ya ce "ka tabbatar ka anshe wanan wayar kafin ka kawomin ita yanzun nan".Yana gama fadar haka ya fice da ga dakin cikin fushi.

Yafi minti biyu yana Kare mata kallo,kafin ya fara takawa a hankali Yana nufar inda take,  Tana jin alamun takun shi a zuciyar ta ta ce "na shiga ukku ya zanyi idan na bari ya kamani tsaf  yake iya saka wannan hannun nashi a inda na sanya wayar ya ciro ta,Dan Yana kallo na saka ta a breaziya".kafin ta ankare ta ji bugawar numfashin shi gab da ita da sauri tayi kokarin ta shi ya yi saurin damko ta da karfi ta furta wayyo Allah !!!!!!!!!!!!!.

To fa freind Farra tana neman agaji ku zo ku taimaka mata. 🤦🤣🤣😭🤭🤭.

🅿️9
     📝A kalla ta dau tsawon 5 minute tana tinanin zuwan ta dakin shi dan bata manta irin yanda suka rinka kwashe wa da shi lokacin suna Abuja.
A karshe dai dole ta yanke shawarar zuwa saboda bata so Momy ta zargi wani abu.
Doguwar hijaf dinta ta saka,fuskar ta tayi fayau duk da ba makeup tayi ba .Babban faranti ta gani an jera komai akai,Bata ga Momy ba a ranta ta ce "hmmAllah sarki Momy gajiya tayi da jira na".

Tana zuwa bakin kofar tayi tsaye ta rasa me zata yi,ta yj niyyar yin knocking ta fasa dan tasan halin shi yana sai ya kyale ta.murdawa kawai tayi ta kunna kanta cikin falon dakin,Duk da hijaf din da ta saka,Amma sosai ta ji sanyi ya ratsa bargon jikin ta tare da wani daddadan kanshi mai ratsa zuciya.
A hankali ta sauke ajiyar zuciya,murya kasa-kasa tayi sallama Wanda ba lallai Wanda ke nesa ya ji ba.

Gani tayi falon wayam ba   kowa sai tv a kunne.Bisa wurin dinning din falon ta nufa ta dora abincin a kai.A ranta ta ce Allah ya taimake ni bayanan may be ma ya fita sarkin iyayi abincin ma sai an kawo masa wanna matar shi ta bani dan bazai barta t huta ba.Tana karaso wa bakin kofar taga mutum a tsaye ya harde hannuwan shi a kirji ya zuba mata narkakkun eyes balls din shi (inji Farrah ta ce irin na mage 🤣🤣😜).sosai ta tsorata kirjinta ta ji yana bugawa a duk second din  bugawar numfashin ta.kara sadda kanta tayi kasa tana Jan numfashi.

A hankali take kokarin motsa bakinta, Amma ji tayi labbanta sunyi nauyi.


A hankali yake matsota,tana jin alamar tana kusanto ta ta fara ja da baya,sai da yaga sun kure da wurin dinning ya tsaya cak Yana Kare mata kallo.

Murya can kasa ya furta "Beby meyasa kika raina ni haka,kina kallona kaman sa'anki ko"?
Iska mai zafi ya furzar daga bakin shi Yana mai Kara kusanto ta har tana iya jiyo irin bugawar da zuciyar shi take yi da sauri.Da azama ta Kara yin baya,jikin dauriya da jarumta ta furta "Please ni ban rainaka ba,ka budeman in fita.

"Idan anki fa" ya bata amsa Kai tsaye cikin wani irin amo kamar na mai shirin yin kuka.
Da sauri ta kalle shi ganin yadda jijiyoyin kanshi suka fito rudu-rudu.sosai ta tsorata,Amma dole ta nuna mai hakurin ta ya fara gazawa akan irin takura ta da yake yi kaman shi ne ubanta.

Da sauri tayi zumbur ta mike tsaye ganin yana Shirin zama kusa da ita ta nufi jikin kofa.
Cikin zafin nama ya ruko hijaf dinta,hakan yasa tayi baya gaba daya ta fada faffadan kirjinsa tsam ya rungume ta gaba dayanta a tare suka sauke ajiyar zuciya.A hankali yakai bakin shi saitin kunnen ta ya furta "waye ya turo maki text a wayar ki"?.
Da karfi ta ji zuciyar ta ta bada sautin dam_dam💔.gaba daya ta tattaro sauran karfin ta ta turashi baya da duka hannuwan ta.baya yayi Yana dafe kujera,tana mayar da numfashi cikin dauriya da dakewa ta furta"Au tambaya ta kake yi?to Wanda zan aura ne ko ka dauka zanyi ta zama kana cin zalina ta karasa fada tana murguda baki alamar tsiwa😏.Amma duk da haka bata yarda sun hada ido ba,domin tasan tana bari suka hada ido ta San ta yau ta kade.

Sosai ya tsura mata ido yana kallan yadda take tsiwa,sai ya ga ta Kara kyau a haka.a ranshi ya ce lallai Beby samarin zamaninmu sun fara canza mata dabi'u.
Cikin daure fuska ya ce "Beby ni kike furtama irin wadannan kalaman,?"hmmm banyi mamaki ba jeki nagode,Dan harga Allah ranshi ya baci.
Stel tana tsaye bata dago kanta ba,ji tayi jikin ta yayi sanyi ta ji  babu dadi a ranta.A hankali ta furta "sorry ".

Ba tare da ya Kara furta komai ba,ya wuce bedroom dinshi ya barta nan tsaye sororo.A hankali ita ma ta bude kofar tayi waje a ranta tana cewa "Mugu ya harda wani rungume ni,duk ya gama rungumar wannan kucakan yanmatan nashi shi ne Nima ya taba ni dan ya gogaman kazantar su,mtssss ta ja tsaki Allah yasa da hijaf a jikina (daga baya kenan Farrah 😂🤣🤣).

Tana shiga main falon Momy ta gani a zaune ba tare da ta ce komai ba ta wuce bedroom dinta tana turo dan karamin bakinta.

Momy ta bita da kallo tana girgiza kanta a fili ta furta "Dole ne na hana ki zuwa dakinsa".

      
          ---------------------------
A (G R A) kuwa Jabir ne na gani da Ummahn su Yana magana da ita kasa-kasa alamar dai rada yake yimata,murmushi ta yi tare da kaima shi duka da sauri ya  kau ce tana dariya ya ce Momy na tafi saina dawo zan ce kina gaishe ta.
"Hmmm Allah shirya ka Jabir,oh ni Maryama Allah ya kawomu wani zamani da yara basa jin kunyar yima iyayen su maganar aure tana rike habar ta🤔.

Gaba yayi Yana dariya,Abba autan su ya ce Ummah ai shi Yaya Jabir ba ruwan shi zai iya fadi a ko Ina ne,Amma Yaya Yasir ban taba ji ance ga budurwar shi ba.

"Hmmm kaima ka fadi,shi wannan wa yake ta shi har mun gaji da yima shi maganar ya fitar da mata,Ai Abban ku ya ce Yana nan yana jiran shi wannan satin sai ya bata masa rai akan maganar fitar da mata.

"Ummah Ina ga sai ya zama tazuru kamin zaiyi aure".

"Hmmm Allah dai ya shirya mana,muna ta yimashi addu'a".



Jabir ko cikin farin ciki ya ja motar shi mai gadi ya budemai ya nufi Barhim.yana karasawa yaga mota Yasir a wurin parking ya ce "A su bro an shigo kenan,wallahi shiyasa Ummah take cewa kaman ya aje mata duk sati Yana hanya.

Cikin gidan ha wuce,Momy ce kadai a falon tana kallan labarai.sallama yayi tare da karasa wa ciki.

"A Jabir ne shigo Mana"cewar Momy da take zaune.

Kasan kafet din ya dauna tare da tonkwashe kafafunshi,cikin girmamawa yake gaishe ta.ta amsa tana cewa "Ya su Ummah taku"
"Tana lafiya lau,ta ce na gaishe ku"

"To Masha Allah muna amsawa".

Ido yake ta wurga wa yaga ta Ina mutuniyar zata bullo,Amma shiru ba alamar ta.Lura da hakan Momy tayi sai ta tashi tana ce wa "Ina mutuniyar ka ko ta kwanta amma bari na yimata magana.sosai ya ji dadi ya kama shi dan harga Allah yana jin kunyar tambayar Momy.

Bisa kujerar ya koma,ita ko Momy ta wuce dakin Farrah.

(Wai Ina Yasir🤔🤣)
Yasir bawan Allah tunda ya shige bedroom dinshi bisa tamfatsetsan gadon shi ya fada,Yana mayar da numfashi sosai ya ji zuciyar shi tayi nauyi,danma shi jarumi ne da ba abinda zai hana shi zubar da hawaye ko ya ji sanyin abina ya tsaye mai a kirjin shi.
Ya dau tsawon lokaci a tsaye kafin ya mike v  wuce toilet Yana kokarin kintsawa,few minutes ya fito shirye cikin kananan kaya masu kau da tsada,sun anshi kalar fatar shi sai yayi Shar gwanin kyau 😂 

A daninng ya yada zango,kadan ya dan tsakuri abincin duk da yanda abincin yayi dadi Amma haka ya hakura ya tashi, Bedroom din ya koma ya dauko wayan shi yayi waje.a zuciyar shi Yana ce wa "gwamma kawai na fadawa Momy duk ma abinda zai faro sai dai ya faru,ko kuwa ace Beby zata tonamin asiri.

( *_Kai_* *_Kai_* *_Kai_* *_cab_* *_akwai_* *_fa_* *_cakwakiya_* 🤔, *_amma_* 
 *_dai_* *_bari_* *_nidai_* *_na_* *yi* *shiru* 🤭 *_kar_* *_aji_* *_mutuwar_* *_sarki_* *a* *bakina* 🤣🤣🤣)

Ina yan team Yasir da alama fa akwai abinda Yasir ya aika shiya sa duk ya rude irin haka🤔

🅿️13
      📝Yasir da dukkanin karfin shi ya rike ta,kokarin kwacewa take yi amma ta kada,saboda karfin mace da namiji ba daya ne ba.daure fuskar shi yayi dan kar ta kawo mai raini,Eyes ball din shi ya zubama ta.sosai take kokarin sadda kanta ga karkarwa da jikin ta ke yi.cikin sarkewar harshe ta fara magana."pl...ea...s..Ya...ya..zan...ba...ka..wa..Yan...ka..sake...ni.."dariya ta kusan kufe mai yanda yaga cikin lokaci kalilan duk ta bi ta rude.hannun ta daya ya saki alamar da shi zata ciro wayar.

Tana kokacin saka hannun ta ta ciro ba tare da ta ankare ba ta ji ya tura hannun shi cikin gaban rigar ta.sosai ta Kara rudewa a ranta ta ce "wato shi dai kullin iskancin shi karuwa yake yi".ji tayi abin nashi ba mai karewa ne ba,cikin zama ta tattaro sauran karfin da ya ragemata ta tura shi yayi baya tagal tagal kaman zai fadi da sauri ya dabe werdrop din wurin da kyar yake mayar da numfashi saboda yanda jijiyoyin jikin shi suka yi sanyi.

Tana ganin tayi nasarar kwatar kanta cikin sauri ta ciro wayar da ga gaban rigar ta ta wurgata a kasa.ta nufi cikin toilet din da gudu ta saka key.anan jikin kofar ta sulale kasa tana sakin kuka mai cin rai,a ranta tana mamakin wannan wane irin zamani ne Allah ya kawo mu da mafi yawan samari haka suke,Kai ita kam taga ta kanta dole ta bar gidannan kawai ta koma Abuja gidan Aunty Husna,Sam ba zata jure wannan abin ba.ga shi daman Bilkisu Abbas tana so ta dorata a wata hanya da bata gane ta ba.Bata so wata rana zuciyar ta ta rinjaye ta.ya Allah ka kubutar da ni.sosai take kuka kaman ranta zai fita.


Shiko goganka Yasir dakyar ya dauki wayar yana hada hanya yabar dakin cikin sanyin jiki da dinbun damuwa.da kyar ya iso part din shi,Dan har wani duhu yake gani yana kokarin rufe mai idanu.saman bed ya zube Yana mai jujjuya kanshi ya damke lebenshi daya na kasa yana taunawa tun karfin shi Amma shi kadai yasan abinda yake ji.Dan shi harga Allah idan Yana kusa da wannan yarinyar some time sai ya kasa control din kanshi bai san meye yake damunshi ba.Duk da a dayan bangaran na zuciyar shi ya kasa yarda da cewa yarinyar tana bin samarin banza duk da irin San da yake yimata Yana ji a ranshi bazai iya auran sauran wasu ba.

Ya dade a cikin wannan yanayi kamin ya fada toilet ya sakanma kanshi ruwa,ko yaji saukin abinda yake damun gangar jikin shi da zuciya.




Dady Bishir kuwa Yana fitowa derect off-site ya haura zuwa babban falon  shi anan ya tarar da Momy tana jiran karasowar shi.Ba tare da ya ce mata komai ba ya wuce bedroom.cikin sauri ta bi bayan shi dan ganin yadda mai gidan nata ya shigo rai a bace.


A bakin bed din ta tadda shi yayi tagumi,Babu kalmar da take fitowa daga bakin shi sai Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraju'un Allahumma ajirni fi musibati wa'akirni kairan minha.ita kadai yake ta maimaitawa.Momy fridge ta nufa,gorar ruwa mai sanyi ta ciro tare da cup ciyayawa tayi ta Mika mai,ba tare da ta ce uffan ba ya amsa bimilla yayi ya kafa bakin shi saida ya shanye tas kafin ta anshi cup din tana duban shi sasai.

Cikin taushin murya ta fara magana "waye ta tabomin mai gidana?waye ya Bata maka rai? Waye yake so yasa hakurin mai gidana ya gaza?nasan mai gidana jarumi ne,Kuma adali ne,mai gidana yana da hakuri da juriyar bacin rai,da irin wannan kalaman tayi nasarar ya sauko.


"Wallahi wannan diyar tamu ce zata bataman rai daga dirowa ta kasar"


"Kana so kaceman Farrah"?

"Kwarai kuwa,domin muna shigowa nida Magaji nake tambayar shi tana ina,sai yake ceman kila tana dakinta hakan yasa muka shiga tare da shi,wallahi maganganun da naji suna fitowa daga bakin ta sun firgita ni.Dukda Dan zamani baka shedar shi amma nasan halayyar Farrah, nasan abinda zata iya aikatawa da Wanda bazata aikata ba.Amma da kunne na na ji tana magana da saurayi har suna agender inda zasu hadu,Kai duniya Ina zaki damu?wannan wane irin zamani ne Allah ya kawo mu?.
Ya dora da cewa"Amma ba komai tunda na dawo dole zamu San abin yi,bana so a samu matsala mahaifin ta zai zarge mu,Yana ganin mutincin mu,Amma ba komai insha Allah komai yazo karshe"ya karasa fada Yana kallan matar shi dan jin me zata ce.


A hankali ya nisa cikin nutsuwa ta fara ba shi hakuri tare da nusar da shi illar saurin yanke hukunci cikin fushi.ta ci gaba da cewa"wallahi Alhaji bansan Farrah tana da saurayi ba domin kuwa bata taba yin waya da wani namiji a Kan idona ba,sannan ba Wanda ya taba zauwa nanan gidan da sunan yazo zanje wurin ta,saboda nasan ka hana.


"Kuma a gefe daya akwai wata rigima da take neman kunnowa tsakanin Yasir da Jabir duk akan ita Farra,Dan haka gwamma tun yanzu kuyiwa tufkar hanci kafin lamarin ya tana zuminci".


"Kina so ki ceman dukkansu santa suke yi"?

"Kusan hakane dan yanzu haka Yasir ya hana Jabir zauwa nanan gidan tun randa suka samu sabani,Amma ni a ganina ku aurama ta Jabir saboda sunfi shakuwa da juna,Dan Yasir tsoran shi take yi sosai wallahi da zaran ya zo weekend Bata da sauran sukuni har sai ya tafi zakaga walwalar ta,Amma duk yanda kukayi yayi".



"Tau shi wannan saurayin da suke waya a makaranta kenan suka hadu"?

"Wallahi bansani ba Alahji saboda bai taba zuwa nan gidan ba".

"Tau kyaleta zanyi maganin ta,makarantar ma ta daina zuwa da mga yau,bayan an kaita dakin mijinta idan ya barta ta karasa idan kuma bai yarda ba dole ta hakura".



"To da yafi Alhaji Dan wallahi karkaga yadda nake zama inyi ta yimata nasiha akan hatsarin wannan Zamanin da mike ciki,kawaye ba abin yarda ne ba,suma samarin idan ka basu Amana zasu iya cutar da kai,sai dai addu'a kawai da fatan Allah ya shiryamana zuri'ar MUSULMI".


"Ba damuwa Allah ya shige Mana gaba"cewar Alhaji Bishir Yana mikewa Dan kimtsa jikin shi.

Bayan Momy ta taimaka mai ya shirya,abinci ta gabatar mai ya ci har wurin motar shi ta raka shi ya tafi,Yana cewa "idan yasir ya futo ki cemashi ya jirani na dawo daga GRA".

Da haka ya fita tana yimai  fatan Allah ya tsare hanya.ya amsa da Ameeen.



 _Tofa _freind ko ya_ za'a_ _Kare_ ?

 _Tsakanin Yasir da Jabir_ _ko wa zaiyi nasarar yin_ _ciki da Farrah_ ?

 *_Koya Farra zata ji,idan ta_ _samu labarin_ _katsemata karatu da_ _za'ayi harga aure ana_ _Shirin yimata_ ?

🅿️11
       📝A washe garin ranar asabar Yasir yana dawo wa daga masallacin sallar asuba yaga kiran Abban su ya shigo wayar shi,cikin azama yayi picking a ranshi yana tunanin ko lafiya".
Cikin girmama wa ya gaishe shi Dady bayan ya amsa ya dora da "Magaji yau ka zo Ina san magana da Kai"
"To Abba insha Allah ganinan shigowa". Katse Kiran yayi yana sauke numfashi domin kuwa yasan da ya ji irin wannan Kiran yasan fada ne zai sha,da wannan tinanin ya shige part din shi.

Dan Farrah kuwa tunda tayi sallah ta koma ta yi kwanciyar ta.

Karfe 10:00 daidai ya fito cikin Shirin shi na yadin cufta brown color ya dora hular shi takalmin shi black,Yana daura agogo a tsintsiyar hannun shi ya nufi part din Momy da sallama dauke a bakin shi.

Ba kowa a falon sai sanyin iskar A/C da yake kadawa.zama yayi saboda ya ji kaman motsin mutum a kiching.Momy ce ta fito tana kokarin jera break a Kan dinning,bayan ta gama karasowa falon tayi,kasa ya sauko Yana ce wa "Momy Ina kwana".
Ba yabo ba fallasa ta amsa mai,hakan yasa ya sha jinin jikin shi.cikin sanyin jiki ya mike,"Momy bari inje GRA Abbah yake kirana."ka yi break Mana kafin ka tafi"?
"Naga lokaci ya kure bari kawai na je a can zanyi".

"Tau ka gaishe su" cewar Momy a zuciyar ta tana ce wa ni bazan kara yima magana ba zan ga iya gudun ruwan ka.

Cikin sanyin jiki ya nufi gidan iyayen nashi,a hankali yake tukin har ya karasa gidan.

Mai gadi ya bude mashi ya yi parking din motar shi wurin da aka tana da dan adana motoci,Yana fitowa ya ga Jabir Yana fitowa da ga site din su ya nufo cikin gidan.Ba tare da ya kula shi ba ya shiga cikin shima ya wuce cikin gidan.A falon ya yadda su gaba dayan su suna break,Dan haka cikin sallama ya karasa Sallamar kawai suka amsa dan haka shima ya ja kujera ya zauna,Dan yasan dokar gidan ce idan ana cin abinci ba'ayin magana.

Bayan sun kammala break din Dady ya fara mikewa shima yabi bayan sa zuwa babban falon nasa,Bisa kafet din da yayima falon kawanya ya yima kansa masauki tare da tonkwashe kafafuwan sa.gyaran murya Dady yayi tare da Kiran sunan sa "Magaji nasan kasan dalilin da ya saka nayi Kiran ka ko?, Ba sai na Kara maimaita wa ba dan haka maganar da mukayi da Kai Ina ta kwana?.

Shiru yayi yana Kara sadda kanshi kasa.
"Da Kai nake magana kayiman shiru?.
"Am ehmm Dady Dan Allah ka Karaman wani dan lokaci insha Allah zan kawo".

"Ai na saba jin wanan kalmar da ga bakin ka ba tun yau ba,to wallahi ka saurare ni da kyau wannan shi ne magana ta karshe da zan yima akan lamarin kayi aure,yanzu kai ko kunya baka ji shekarar ka kusan nawa, Amma kana zaune ,Kuma idan ka koma aiki gobe na Kara ganin keyar ka a garinnan wallahi zaka gamu da fishi na,kafi son kayi ta yawo yau kana nan gobe kana abuja ko?.
Ya dora da,"sannan the last one jiya duk rashin mutumcin da kayiwa Yan uwan ka ya dawo Kan kunne na,Dan haka ka kyauta".

Shi dai har zuwa wannan lokacin bai ce kala ba.Dady kuwa ta inda ya shiga batanan yake fita ba,sai da yayi mai tas sosai ta inda ya shiga ba tanan yake fita ba.A karshe ya dora da Kuma ka saurari hukuncin da zamu yanke da dan uwana ya dawo,ta shi ka bani wuri.

"A hankali ya furta Allah ya huci zuciyar ka"Yana mikewa,wurin motar shi ya nufa ko sallama bai yiwa Ummah ba ya figi motar shi da mugun gudu ya fita da ga gidan.

      --------------------------
A bangaran Farrah tunda ta fito ta yi break ta koma  bedroom dinta,dan haka Momy ta kyale ta bata ce mata komai ba saboda tasan tana bukatar hutu da dogon nazari.

Sosai Jabir ya ji dadi,Dan yana shigowa Auta umar (Abba)ya labarta mai zuwan Yaya Yasir amma besan fadan me akayi mai ba yaga dai ya fita daga gidan ranshi a bace."Hmm Allah yasa idan ya tafi yayi shekara bai zo garinnan ba ni nasan kafin nan insha Allah anyi bikina da matar ka ga shikenan,Dan ni wallahi ban yarda da shi ba Yana takura mana, Auta umar yayi ta dariya.


          *************
Yasir Yana fitowa da gudu darect wayar abokin shi ya Kira Bishir ya tambaye shi su hadu a wurin wani shakata wa.Haka ko aka yi Yana zuwa shima ya karaso.

Yasir shiru yayi ba tare da ya ce komai ba sai huci da yake faman yi kaman Wanda yayi gudun tsere.Da Bishir yaga shirun yayi yawa ya ce "wallahi kaifa dan iska ne yaushe zaka Kira ni Kuma ka saka ni a gaba kana kallo na,tell me me yake faruwa"?.
Sai a sannan ya samu damar magana "Dan Allah ka bani shawara me ya kamata inyi,wallah i so suke su raba ni da ita ,duk cikin kwanakin nan Ina cikin tashin hankali".

"Hmmm Yasir kenan wallahi ka bani mamaki,Ai dole zaka rasa nutuwar ka,kaida kanka kasan dole zata rinka tosran ka hmmm sai ta tona maka asiri a wurin su Dady a sannan zaka gane kuranka",haba haba  ka dawo cikin hayyacin ka Mana".

Sosai ya nutsu Yana kallan abokin nashi.

"Amma Bishir kasan  nayi ma bayanin ba laifina bane sharrin zuciya ne,to wallahi yanzu kwata-kwata tsorona take yi,Amma abu daya yasa nake jin karfin gyuwa ina hango soyaya ta a cikin kwayar idanun ta duk da ita kuruciya ta sanya ta kasa fahimtar haka".

"Ok ita ma ai duk a cikin kuruciyar ne ta kasa manta yanda ka rinka mammatse ta," ya karasa fada Yana nuna shi da hannu,cikin dariya ya Kara ce wa "wallahi freind Kai dan rainin hankali ne ,idan na fahimce ka shiyasa kake tunanin tana tare da wasu samarin ko? To Ina mai baka shawara da ka kyauta ta zato gare ta ka daina zarginta,Duk da muna cikin wani zamani mai wuyar sha'ani amma akwai yanmatan da ba ruwan su harga Allah Dan baza'a taru a zama daya ba.m".

Yasir ya kule sosai cikin jin haushi ya ce "ban San wulakanci Bishir ban ce ka tasa ni a gaba ba kana yiman dariya,shawara nake bukata ko nayi tafiya t".

"Hmmmm to sorry man ba ni na kashe zomon ba rataya aka bani".

"Please kana ji na farko dai saika cire zargin da kake yimata idan ba haka ba duk randa ta gano ana zargin ta to wallahi duk randa Allah ya kaddar auran ku zata gwara ka sosai,na biyu dole sai ka rage wannan shegen kishin naka marar amfani ,sannan saika daina takurama ta.idan kabi wannan insha Allah Ina ganin zakayi nasara".


"Wallahi matsala ta kishi,tabbas kishin ta yake sanya ni yin wasu abubuwan,Amma Insha Allah zan yi kokarin ganin na rinka control din kaina ".

"Ba matsala bari na koma kasuwa zamuyi magana ta waya".
"Ok Alright nagode"

Da ga haka suka rabu ya wuce gida.

🅿️14
      📝Yasir tunda ya fito daga toilet Kan bed dinshi ya kwanta da towel daure a kugun shi.ba bacci yakeyi ba eyes balls dinshi a rufe suke,sosai ya lula duniyar tunani a wani bangaran na zuciyar Shi Yana Addu'ar Allah yasa Beby ta zama mallakin shi.Yana cikin wannan tinanin ya ji Kiran sallar la'asar,Mikewa yayi ya dauro Arwalla tare da shiryawa sky blue na shadda ya saka dinkin yayi mai kyau saboda riga karama ce daidai guywar shi hannun mai link n,kanshi ba hula sau mai santsi da ya taje ta kwanta luf gwanin sha'awa.cikin azama ya wuce masallaci danya sauke farali.


            -----------------------

Dady Bishir Yana isowa GRA derect gidan Dan uwan shi ya nufa,mai gadi ya bude mai ya shiga bayan sun gaisa cikin girmamawa,ya nufi hanyar da zata sada shi da falon gidan.A daidai kofar kofar sukayi kicibis,Abbah shida Jabir sun fito zasu je masallaci."A a Dan uwa Kaine tafe da yammacin nan,yanzu muke maganar da Jabir cewar "da mun fito daga sallah zai kaini gidan naka"Ashe kana tafe".cewar Abbah Yana kallan dan uwan nashi cikin so da kaunar juna.


"Wallahi kasa daurewa nayi kawai sai na fito,Amma mu je masallacin mu dawo sai mu tattana".

A haka suka wuce cikin nutsuwa da San junan su Jabir kuwa Yana gefen su.




         **************
Farrah baiwar Allah ta dade a cikin toilet tana sharbar kuka,saida ta gaji saboda yanda ta ji kanta Yana saramata da karfi,kamin ta nufi jikin sink fara wanke fuskarta tayi kafin ta gyara jikin ta dan tana fashin sallah.sai a sannan ta tina ko abinci bata ci ba sakamakon kugin da ta ji cikin ta nayi alamar Yana bukatar abinci.Doguwar riga kadai ta zira ta dora dan siririn mayafin saman kanta.



A hankali ta nufi kiching din,cikin sanda dan kar kowa ya ganta tayi sa'a kuwa dan har ta shige ba Wanda ya ganta.Dambun shinkafa ne ta gani da miyar kabeji,Dan kadan ta sanya cikin plate ta dora nama akai bata tsaya daukar lemu ba dan tana da shi a fridge dinta.Cikin sanda ta samu ta shige dakinta,key ta murza a jikin kofar ta fara cin abincin ta cikin nutsuwa kamar wadda bata da damuwar komai a ranta.dan ita har ga Allah damuwa bata hanata ba cikinta hakkin sa.(kamar ni kike Farrah idan ma baka ci ba,to fa damuwar duniya dai kullun karuwa take yi😂😜).




Momy ko bayan tafiyar mai gidan nata,sosai ta lula duniyar tinani Dan ita  a nata bangaran tafison a hada Jabir da Farrah, saboda tafi sabawa da shi Kuma zata fi samun kulawa da nasuwa sabanin Yasir da Basu Shan inuwa daya.Da wannan tinanin ta mike dan Kara gyara dakin mai gidan nata.
