🅿️16
      📝Duk wainar da ake toyawa a wannan lokacin Farrah bata sa ni ba har zuwa dare har zuwa dare bata fito ba Kuma ba Wanda ya bi ta kanta.



Shima gogan naka Yasir tunda ya dawo daga masallaci bai Kara fitowa ba har zuwa dare.sosai naga fuskar shi ta dan fada eyes balls din shi sun Kara fitowa waje a cikin yini daya duk ya fara susucewa,Damuwar shi daya kada su Dady su bada Farrah ga Jabir ko saurayin da suka ji tana waya da shi,Idan kuwa haka ta kasance shikam kila ajalin shi ne ya taho.A bangare daya na zuciyar shi tana umartar shi daya fito ya sanar da Farrah  hakikanin abinda ke cikin zuciyar,kada ya bari garin kallan ruwa kwado yayimashi kafa.



Da karfi ya daki dan karamin tebur din dake gaban shi gaba daya ya tarwatse kayan da ke Kai suka yi dai-dai a tsakar daki.Dandanan hannun ya fara zubar da jini sakamakon yayyankewa da yayi.Zirga-zirga ya fara yi a tsakar dakin Yana cewa "Ina imporsible bazan iya ba,daga lokacin dana furta ma ta zata Kara raina ni,gaskiya bazan iya ba idan ko har ban sameta ba dole na bar kasar nan gaba daya".


Sosai Yasir ya rude kuka take yi kaman Wanda akayi  ma mutuwa a karshe jiri ya dauke shi anan ya zube saman kafet din a sume.



Jabir ne gaban Ummah Yana ta damunta akan maganar Farrah,Duk yabi ya isheta.
Cikin jin haushi ta ce "Wai Dan Allah Jabir meyasa kake so ka mayar da ni abokiyar wasanka,Baka zuwa ka samu iyayen ka sai ni da ka raina ko? To ka baceman da gani tun kafin Raina ya baci".


Cikin sanyin jiki ya mike Yana cewa" Allah ya huci zuciyar ki"ya fita a cikin zuciyar shi Yana mamakin wannan sauyi.






      _________________

Misalin karfe 8:30 na dare Momy tana zubawa Dady abinci jefi-jefi suna dan taba hira.Bayan ya kammala ya Kai duban ta gare shi ya ce "Wai ni Ina Magaji"?


"Wallahi tun dazu ban Kara ganin ya shigo ba,Amma bari na leko inga ko lafiya nasan halin shi da zuciya"ta karasa fada tana mike wa ta nufi site din Yasir.
Dady ba tare da ya ce ma ta komai ba ya mayar da hankalin shi Kan jaridar da yake dubawa.


Tana shiga ta taras baya falo saita karasa bed room din shi tana sallama shiru ta ji ba a amsa ba hakan yasa tayi tinanin ko Yana toilet.
Tana bude cotean din ta hango shi yashe saman kafet,Da sauri ta karasa tana Kiran sunan shi "Magaji! Magaji? Lafiya meya sameka?tana bubbuga shi amma shiru bai motsa ba,hannun shi ta daga taga  baya ko alamar motsi.da sauri ta koma part din su tana kwalawa Alhaji kira,tasowa yayi Yana cewa "Lafiya Hajiya?meya faru?.


"Cikin tsoro da sarkewar murya ta ce "Alhaji Ma..ga.ji.. tana nuna mai part din cikin azama yayi gaba tana biye da shi a baya.Yana zuwa Dady yaga Magaji yashe a kasa baya ko motsi,sosai ya shi ma ya rikice Amma da yake namiji ne dole ya dauko dauriya ya yafawa ranshi.


"Hajiya bari na janyo mota sai inyiwa Baba Auwal magana ya taimaka Mana mu sanya shi a mota gwamma muje asibiti" fita yayi jimawa kadan suka shigo da Baba Auwal mai gadi.cikin ikon Allah sukayi nasarar sanya shi a cikin mota Momy tana kokarin shiga Dady ya kalleta "Ina hijaf dinki"?

Sai a sannan ta tuna da ko hijaf bata dauko ba,ciki ta koma da sauri ta dauko suka ja motar da sauri suka nufi Alheri cleanik .


Cikin ikon Allah tafiyar minti 10 ta kaisu asibitin,da sauri likitocin suka fara ba shi taimakon gaggawa.

A wurin gaban dakin aka dakatar da su Momy,nan suka zauna suna addu"ar Allah ya bashi lafiya.


Jimawa kadan daya daga cikin likitocin da suke duba shi ta fito,Da sauri suka tareta suna tambayar ya jikin nashi.

"Ku kwantar da hankalin ku Alhamdlillah munyi nasarar ceto ranshi,Amma yanzu munyi mai allurar bacci dan haka Yana bukatar hutu sai nan da two hours zai farka, sakamakon shork din daya same shi".

"Tau Allah ya taimaka ke mu ya tashi" cewar Dady.Dan sai a sannan ya tina da ko wayar da zai Kira yayan shi dan ya sanar mai ya manta bai dauko ba.



 *_To fns koya za'a Kare_ _ga* *dai Yasir a gadon_* *_asibiti_* ?

🅿️18
     📝Suna cikin tafiya Dady ya dauko wayar shi daya aje a gefen shi tun shigowar shi motar.


Kokarin danna callii din nomber yayan shi yake yi sai ya ga Kiran shi ya shigo.

A hankali yake rage gudun motar tare da yin picking call din ya saka ta a handfree yana mai yiwa yayan nashi sallama.

Bayan ya amsa ya ke ce mai "kanina Ina ka aje wayar ka Ina ta Kira baka dauka ba"?.cewar Abbah


"Wallahi yaya bana kusa da wayar dan yanzu nake Shirin Kiran ka sai naga naka Kiran ya shigo"


"Ok ba matsala ya iyali? Da fatan duk kuna lafiya   ko"?.


"Da sauki dai Yaya wallahi daman Magaji ne  muka ganshi ya fadi a cikin dakin shi to shi ne muka kawo shi asibiti,Amma ka kwantar da hankalin ka ya farka tun dazu.Sun ce da sun cire ma shi Karin ruwan zuwa gobe zasu yi discharge din mu insha Allah".


Ajiyar zuciya Abba ya sauke Yana ce wa "Subahanallahi Yanzu haka kuna asibitin ne ko kuwa"?


"Akwai maman su Nusaiba tare da shi,Amma ni yanzu gida na je dauko kayan da za'a bukata dan yanzu haka mun kusa karasa wa clinic din nida Farrah".


"To ba damuwa bari nasa Jabir ya kawo mu nida Umman su yanzu".


"A a. Yaya ai da kun bari tunda naga kaman dare ya  yi sosai,da sai mu barwa safiya".


"Karka damu kanina nasan zaka ba Magaji kulawa fiye da yadda mu zamu ba shi, saboda Umman su kar ta ga ba'a yi ma ta adalci ba ko"?


"To Allah dai ya ba shi lafiya,ba komai Yaya sai kun Kara so" cewar Dady yana katse wayar dai_dai lokacin da suka yi sallama. Yana mayar da hankalin shi bisa titi.


"A hankali Farrah da ke sit din baya ta sauke wata boyayyar ajiyar zuciya a hankali tana lunshe idanun ta tare da jingina bayan ta jikin kujerar a  hankali ta ji jikin ta yayi sanyi ta fara yunani a ranta may be ko abinda tayi mai ne ya saka shi a damuwa dan tasan shi da bakar zuciya
Wani bangaran na zuciyata ta ji ya bata amsa da "ke meye laifin ki a cikin wannan lamarin ?Ai ke ya kamata ki yi fushi da shi saboda ya dade Yana musguna ma ki da salan wulakancin shi".

Amma duk da haka sai ta ji babu dadi a ranta dan ko ba komai Yaya Yasir dan uwanta ne na jini Akwai alaka ta yan'uwan taka a tsakanin su.tana cikin wanna tinanin ta ji motar ta tsaya alamun su iso kenan.

Cikin sanyin ta ta bude kofar motar ta fita dai-dai lokacin har Dady ya bude burt.

Tana daukar basket din suka nufi cikin hosbital din tsayawa tayi a Bayan shi saboda door din kamar irin ta bank ne ba'a wucewa mutum biyu har sai tayi charging din mutum kafin wa wuce ciki.



Suna shiga darect make word suka wuce a dai-dai lokacin doctor shima yake kokarin komawa dan ganin an siyo magungunan.

A bakin  kofar suka hadu anan Dady yake ba shi hakuri akan bata time da suka yi .


"A bakomai Alhaji kuyi sauri yanzu ku amso zamu Kara duba shi"cewar doctor suna karasa shiga dakin dukan su.


Ita dai Farrah tana biye da su kamar jela🤣 sai rabe-rabe take yi kamar wadda take jin tsoron wani ya kama ta.


Yasir ba bacci yake ba Amma idon shi a rufe yake,Koda ya ji maganar su Dady baisa ya bude su ba.


Daidai sun Karaso jikin gadon Momy ta mike tana cewa "Har kun karaso"?

"Eh mun samu karasowa" daidai  yana zama akan kujerar da ke kusa da gadon.


Hannun shi ya ruko yana Kiran sunan shi Magaji,Magaji!.

A hankali yake bude idanun shi har yayi nasarar bude su gaba daya akan Dady.

"Sannu Magaji ya kake jin jikin naka"? Yana mai taba wuyan shi jin ba zafi .

"Akwai abinda yake yima ka ciwo "?


Hannun shi na dama ya da ga ya dora bisa goshin shi, alamar nan yake yi ma shi ciwo.


"Tau sannu kaga ga doctor dinnan ya dawo,bari ya cire maka Karin ruwan sai kayi burosh a baka abinci ko"?

A daidai nan doctor ya iso wurin,Karin ruwan ya cire ma shi,tare da cewa ga magungunan ku ba shi ko ruwan tea ne ya sha sai mu ba shi druks din.

"Ok ba matsala doctor mu hado mai abinci ke Farrah yi sauri hado mai yanzu".


Sai a sannan gaba dayan su suka juya zuwa wurin da suka ga Dady yakai duban shi.


A raku be suka ganta wuri guda kaman mai Shirin rugawa,sai a sannan suka ankare da ita a wurin.

Saurin daukar basket din tayi zuwa bakin gadon.
Cikin sanyin murya ta ce Ummah ya jikin na shi?tana kokarin zuba tea a cikin cop.

Momy tsayawa tayi tana kallanta ba tare da ta ce komai ba .

Hakane yasa Farrah jikin ta ya Kara yin sanyi sosai itama ba tare da ta Kara cewa komai ba harta gama zubawa.


Akan dan karamin table din ta dora ta koma sit daya ta zauna tana wasa da zoben hannun ta.

Yasir kuwa duk abinda take yi Yana kallanta ta kasan eyes din shi har zuwa lokacin da ta zauna.shi kadai yasan irin zafin da yake ji a zuciyar shi.


Bude eyes din yayi a kanta sosai yake duban ta duk da hijaf ce a jikin shi amma tayi ma shi kyau.

Momy ta dube shi tana cewa "Magaji ta shi ka yi brosh sai ka sha tea din"tana kokarin tayar da shi.Dady ya kama suka karasa tayar da shi.

A hankali yake saukowa daga bisa gadon harya mike tsaye.

Momy ta kalli Farrah "ta shi ki hada ma shi ruwa yayi brosh.

Sosai ta ji gabanta ya fadi,Amma cikin dauriya ta mike zuwa tailet din dake cikin dakin.

Bayan ta fito tana cewa "Momy na hada ruwan akwai saban brosh a ciki.

Da kanshi ya karasa toilet din.


Daidai ya fito ya tarda  Abban shi sun Kara so.

Sosai Ummahn shi ta rude ganin yadda dan nata ya zabge a cikin yini daya.

Da sauri ta nufi dan nata tana riko hannun shi.
"Magaji Kai ne ka rame haka?meya ke damunka ? .

Shi dai bai ce komai ba har ya zauna bakin gadon.

Umman shi Bata rai ta yi sosai dan harga Allah ta fara tunanin akwai hannun wannan yarinyar, A da tana San Farrah,Amma a yanzu gaskiya ta fara fita daga ranta saboda ta fahimci so take ta kashe mata yara har guda biyu.

🅿️17
      📝Su Dady duk tsawon lokacin nan suna wurin a zaune basu matsa ko Ina ba.


Cikin ikon Allah Yasir karfe   10:25 pm dai-dai lokacin ya fara kokarin  motsa hannun shi a hankali,Hakan yasa nursing din da take kokarin shigowa dakin dan Kara dubashi tayi saurin karasowa tana cewa "Sannu ka farka,bari na Kira iyayen ka daman suna ta jiran farkawar ka"ta fita da sauri.

Momy tana ganin tahowar nursing din ta tareta cikin sauri tana tambayar ta ya jikin dannasu.Dady ma ya taso suna hada baki wurin tambayar ta.


"Kuna ji? Ku kwantar da hankalin ku ya farka yanzu,Amma yana bukatar hutu sannan kada ayima shi hayaniya a Kan shi,Yanzu zamu Kara duba shi Idan ruwan ya Kare muna sa ran zuwa gobe insha Allah zamu yi discharge din shi".tana juyawa zuwa dakin,suka bi bayanta a zukatan su suna masu tasbihi ga Allah daya tashi kafadun yaron nasu.



Daidai sun shiga Yana kokarin tashi zaune,cikin sauri suka taimaka mai ya zauna filo aka sanya ma shi a baya ya jingina da jikin bangon suna yi mai sannu.

Shi dai bai ko uffan bai ce ba,Amma Yana jin me suke fada.


Nursing din ce ta Kara shigowa tare da doctor.Cikin kulawa suka Kara duba shi tare da gyara ma shi Karin ruwan ,wata allurar ya Kara yima shi a cikin ruwan da aka jona mai,sannan ya mikowa Dady wata takarda daya rubuta magungunan da ake bukata.


"Ga shi Alhaji ku samo wadannan magungunan yanzu za'a ba shi kafin muga zuwa anjima".


Cikin sanyin jiki Dady yasa hannun shi ya amsa Yana cewa "Wallahi doctor saboda rudewa da muka yi kaga ko walet din da waya ban dauko ba,Amma bari na je yanzu insha Allah zan daw".


"To ba matsala Alhaji muna jiran ku " cewar doctor Yana kokarin futa da ga room din nursing din tana biye da shi a baya.



Dady ya mayar da duban shi wurin da Momy take zaune ta yi tagumi ba tare da ta ce komai ba ya ce "Bari na je ki kula da shi Yanzu zan dawo insha Allah,idan na dauko wayar dai a sanar da su Yaya ko"?


"To Alhaji Allah ya kiyaye,ka ce ma Farrah ta hado ma shi kayan tea saboda idan ya sun cire Karin ruwan a ba shi ko ya ji dan karfin jikin shi,Dan Ina tinanin bata San mun fito ba".


"Tau ba matsala Allah dai ya ba shi lafiya"cewar Alhaji Yana fita daga dakin.

Yana fita Momy ta matsa kusa da gadon ganin yadda ya Kara rufe idanun shi kamar mai bacci.A hankali ta ke Kiran sunan shi "Magaji! Magaji! Magaji!".



Sai a sannan ya bude idanun shi kadan saboda dishi-dishi da yake gani .


Ruko dayan hannun shi ta yi Wanda ba kanula a jiki tana mai yima shi kallan tausa yi ganin yadda lokaci daya fuskar shi ta fada tayi wani fayau alamar Yana jin jiki.


"Sannu Magaji Allah dai ya baka lafiya".


Shi dai bai ce komai ba sai idanu da ya tsura ma ta Yana kallan ta.

       

        **************

Dady yana isowa Barhim Baba Auwal ya bude mai gate ya shigo Yana tambayar shi ya mai jikin,ya amsa "Da sauki Baba".


"To Allah ya ba shi lafiya"cewar Baba Auwal.


Ya amsa da "Amin ya Allah" yana yin parking ko marfin motar bai tsaya rufewa ba ya nufi cikin gidan da sauri 


Darect dakin Farrah ya nufa ya fara kwankwasa wa.


Cikin baccin da ya fara alamar daukar ta ta ji kamar ana bugun kofa.

A hankali ta mike da addu'a a bakinta ta nufi jikin kofar a ranta tana tinanin kila Momy ce.


Turus ta yi lokacin da ta bude kofar ta ga Dady a tsaye.

"Dady ina yini?

Ba tare da ya bi ta Kan gaisuwar da take yimai ba ya fara cewa "kiyi sauri yanzu ki hada kayan tea zamu je asibiti yayan ku ne baya da lafiya,ki hamzar ta bana so mu bata lokaci saboda sai na je za'a ba shi magunguna" ya juya zuwa off-site ba tare da ya Kira amsar ta ba.


Gaban ta ta ji ya bada wani sautin *rassssss!!!!-rasssss* !!!!! da karfi har sai da ta dafe saitin zuciyarta da hannu.


A hankali ta koma dakin,plant shues dinta ta sako a hankali ta nufi hanyar kiching kamar wadda bata san tafiyar.




Cikin sanyi ta fara kokarin hada abubuwan da ake bukata da sauri ta fere arish ta fada kwai ta kada shi da magi ta yanka albasa zuwa wannan lokacin ruwan tea din da ta dora ya dahu ta juye shi a plas tare da fara soya wainar da arish din da ta hada.


Cikin ikon Allah a cikin five minutes ta gama hada komai a muhallin shi wani basket ta dauko duk ta zuzu ba kulas din ta nufi falo da shi.


A zaune ta tadda Dady yana jiranta.

Tana ajewa a Kan dinning ta yi saurin zuwa ta dauko dogon hijaf din ta ta saka tare da daukar basket din tabi bayan Dady da tuni ya nufi wurin motar shi.


Burt ya bude ta aje abincin bayan ya rufe ya shiga mazaunin drever ita Kuma tayiwa kanta masauki a sit din baya.Ya tashi motar da gudu Baba Auwal ya bude ma su gate suka nufi hanyar Alheri cleanic.





 *To fns wasa farin girki* 🤣🤣 *yanzuma fa za'a* *hadu ga Yasir ga Farrah* *koya zata kasan* *ce* ?

🅿️20
      📝Jabir daidai sun kawo wurin round din kwado a lokacin karfe 10:55 tayi sai jefi jefi motoci ke giftawa suma ba na haya ne ba irin na masu kudi ne .

Kiran sunan shi tayi "Jabir"

"Na'am Ummah"Yana kokarin rage gudun motar dan jin me zata ce.


"Kana ji na wallahi da Kai da Magaji ku kiyayi wannan yarinyar dan na fara fahimtar makira ce duk wannan rawar kafar da kuke a kanta shiyasa take neman rainaku,kamar ku karamar yarinya ta rinka juyaku to wallahi ku kiyaye ni daga ku har ita idan ba haka ba kunsan hali na (Daman Ummahn su Jabir akwai ta da fada dukda tana da fara'a ga San jama'a,Amma bata daukar raini wannan dalilin yasa yaranta suke shakkarta fiye da mahaifin su ALHAJI Usman).


Sosai take yimai fada ta inda ta shiga batanan take fita ba,har suka shiga harabar gidan tana abu daya.

Shi dai Jabir bawan Allah bai ce ma ta komai ba sai hakuri da yake bata.

Tana fita ta nufi cikin gidan shi Kuma ya juya Kan motar zuwa clinic din ya dauko Abbahn nasu.


Yana isowa bakin kofar asibitin yaga iyayen nasu sun fito waje suna tattaunawa akan matsalar da doctor yayi masu baya ni agame da Yasir.


Karasawa yayi wurin su Yana gaishe da Dady tare da tambayar ya jikin Yayan shi,Dady amsawa yayi ai da sauki sosai.


"To Allah ya ba shi lafiya" cewar Jabir ya nufi cikin hosbital din Dan ganin ya jikin dan uwan nashi".


Dady Bishir ya ce "Ba damuya Yaya Allah ya shigemana gaba,insha Allah zan Kara yiwa mahaifin ta bayani gobe idan Allah ya kaimu dan duk abinda ya ce shi za'a bi".


"To Allah ya kaimu" cewar Abbah.

Cikin suka koma suna masu amsawa da Ameen.



Jabir yamay shiga yaga brother din shi a kwance ga dukkan alamu bacci yake yi dan ko bai ji ya amsa sallama ta.

A kujerar dake fuskantar gadon ya zauna Yana nazarin shi da Kuma irin kalaman da ya ji mahaifiyar su na fada kafin ya sauketa a gida.


A ranshi ya ce "Tabbas akwai wani boyayyyan al'amari a cikin wannan abin,Amma Allah ka zabama na abinda yafi alkhairi  a rayuwar mu da addinin mu".

Yana cikin wannan zancen zucin su Dady suka turo kofar dakin, karasowa sukayi suna tambayar har yanzu baccin yake yi.


"Eh bai farka ba har yanzu" cewar Jabir.


"To taso mu wuce da safe sai mu dawo,Dan doctor sunce akwai masu kula da shi ba sai mun bar kowa ba".


Daga haka suka fice Dady Bishir shi kadai ya nufi Barhim Abbah da Jabir suka wuce GRA kowannen  zuciyar  shi  da abinda take saka mai.



      ******************
            *WASHEGARI* 
Washe gari tunda asuba Momy ta kwankwasa room din Farrah ta sanar da ita ta zo su kammala kayan break da wuri zasu je asibitin.


Cikin ikon Allah zuwa karfe 8:00 am sun kammala komai Wanda za'a tafi da shi sun zuba a cikin basket. Suka nufi kimtsa jikin su Dan Dady har ya fito.



A bangaran su Ummah ma hakan take suna ta shiri dan zuwa ganin dan nasu zuciyar ta cike da sake saken rashin mutumcin da zata yiwa Farrah.



Duk kusan a tare suka iso bakin asibitin,motar su Jabir tana karasowa sai ga ta su Dady ta shigo.


Bayan sun firfito aka fara gaggaisawa da Juna,Ita dai Farrah tana like jikin Momy ta gaishe Da Abbah da Jabir ta ce "Ummah Ina kwana".

Yi tayi kamar Bata jita ba tayi gaba Autah Umar ya take mata baya.

Sosai Farrah jikinta ya Kara yin sanyi ganin irin yadda tayi mata.A hankali suma suka kara sa shiga ciki.Dan shi kanshi Jabir bai ji dadin irin yadda Umman tasu ta nuna ba dan shi harga Allah baiga laifin ta anan ba.


Suna shiga room din daidai lokacin Yasir ya fito da ga wanka kayan shi Momy ta ciromashi Wanda ta sako cikin jakar kayan su.

Toilet din ya koma bayan ya canza ya dawo bakin gadon dukan su suna yimashi ya jiki Yana amsawa cikin sanyi murya.


A hankali Farrah ta gaishe shi kauda kanshi yayi Yana yiwa Autah Umar magana.


Hakan yasa Farrah ta koma gefe a ranta tana cewa "Dan rainin hankali ni ya kamata na ji haushi saboda kwana nayi yatsuna suna yiman zafi damma nasha magani.



A wannan karon Umman su ta hada mai abinci ta fara ba shi.

Ita dai Farrah bata Kara cewa uffan ba har doctor ya shigo bayan ya Kara duba shi yayi discharging din su tare da jaddada masu a kula da ba shi magani,Kuma ya rage yawan tunani da sanya damuwa a ranshi.



Kayan su suka fara tattarawa dan tafiya gida bayan sunyi bill din komai.


Farrah ta dauki wasu kayan ta yi waje Daman ta gaji so take su tafi gida ga yunwa ta fara kwakularta.


Jabir Yana ganni ta fita shima ya dauki sauran kayan ya bi bayanta.


A jikin motar ya risketa kamar zata yi kuka Dan ya fahimci bata ji dadin abinda Ummahn su tayi mata ba.


Kusa da ita ya jingina Yana kallan fuskarta cikin kulawa ya ce "Matar tau kuma ba magana"?


Murmuy take kokarin kakalowa Amma yaki zuwa,cikin dakiya ta ce "Sorry Yaya kaina ne ya fara yiman ciwo shiyasa"


"Ayya sorry yanzu zamu tafi ai sai ki sha magani idan kin je ko".


Kai ta gyada ma shi saboda yadda take jin zuciyar ta nayi mata zafi kuka Yana iya kufemata ba tare da ta ankare ba.



Fitowar su Dady yasa Jabir barin gurin ya nufi jikin motar su.


Gani tayi Momy ta bata rai tana magana kamar tana fada.

Ita dai tana nan tana hango duk abinda ke faruwa.sai ta ga Abbah ya janyo hannun Yasir ya nufo motar su da shi.


Mamaki take yi ganin kowanne ranshi a bace,a ranta ta ce "may be bayan na fito wani abin ya faru".


Suna karasowa inda suke itama Ummahn su Jabir ta biyo su day sauri tana cewa "wallahi Alhaji bakuyiman adalci ba wato mayar da shi zaka yi wanna mai fuskar irin ta munafikai ta kasara kashe min shi ko"? Ta fada cikin fushi tana nuna Farrah da yatsarta.


Sosai Farrah ta ji faduwar gaba,cikin in Ina ta ce "Umm..mah..me..na.yi."?tana kwabe fuska kamar zata yi kuka .


Cikin fushi Abbah ya daka masu tsawa da cewar kowa ya shiga mota.

Kowa a motar da ya zo ya shige Abbah ya Saka Yasir motar su Farrah shima ya koma tasu suka dunguma zuwa Barhim.



Tofa fns Wai meke Shirin faruwa ne?






     ✍️✍️✍️✍️✍️✍️

🅿️21
     📝Isuwar su Barhim keda wuya Farrah ta dauki wasu kayan da aka saka Burt din motar ta wuce cikin gidan ba tare da ta tsaya sauraren wani abuba tana ajewa ta nufi bedroom dinta da sauri ta saka key,ko hijaf dinta bata tsaya cirewa ba ta fada bisa gado tana kuwa ita ta rasa me yake damun zuciyar ta,Dan ta lura Ummah tana yimata wulakanci ne saboda zalimu ai da ta San yanda bana kaunar shi da ta samamin lafiya.
To ni mema zanyi da shi ?Ai ko kaddara bana fatan tasa na aure shi.mtsssss ta ja dogon tsaki tare da Kara mirginawa daga kwanciyar da take.



Suna karasa firfitowa Dady ya ce wa "Jabir kama Dan uwanka ka kaishi part din shi ya huta kafin mu gama tattaunawa"Yana nufar kofar da zata sada su da babban falon gidan duk suka bishi.


A hankali su Yasir suke takawa ba Wanda ya yiwa dan uwanshi magana har suka karasa cikin falon nashi.

Anan bisa kujera Jabir ya kwanta Yana jin gajiya tare da shi,ga shi yau an hanashi zuwa wurin aiki.


Yasir kuwa bedroom ya wuce cire kayan jikin shi ya daura towel a kugunshi ya fada tailet Dan Kara yin wanka.



Su Dady a babban falon suka zauna,inda har yanzu Ummah bata Kara cewa komai ba dan har yanzu haushin su take ji.


Gyaran murya Abbah yayi tare da fara ba kanin nashi hakuri akan abinda matar shi ta nuna,sannan ya Kara jaddada ma su bai yarda ta amshe Magaji daga nan gidan ba.Tunda ba ita ta kwoshi ba shi ya kawo kanshi.Kuma da kike maganar Za'a kashe maki yaro oh da bakiyi wannan tinanin ba sai yanzu ko,to wallahi zan mugun sabamaki,the last one ko ki so Farrah,ko ki Kira ya rage naki idan Allah yasa zata zama matar daya daga cikin yaran ki to mu Umarnin ubangiji kawai zamu cika .Dan haka ya rage naki cuta kuka Allah ne ya nufeshi da yinta.yana kaiwanan ya dubi Kanan nashi ya ce " ni na gama magana idan kana da abinda zaka ce bismillah,idan babu mu zamu wuce dan inaga sai naje Abujar da kaina wurin mahaifinta insha Allah.



"Bakomai Yaya duk abinda ka ce shi zamuyi,Amma inaga tare ya kamata muje Dan inaganin zaifi".


"To ba matsala karka damu insha Allah komai zai wuce"cewar Abbah Yana kokarin mikewa.


Kusan a tare suka fito Dan Ummah ko sallama bata yimasu ba ta shigewar ta mota.


Da haka suka rabu Abbah ya Kira wayar Jabir,Jim kadan sai ga shi ya fito sukayi sallama da kanin na shi ya fara tuka su suka dauki hanyar GRA.



          -----------------------
Farrah ko batasan sun Jabir sun tafi ba.da kyar ta fito ta nufi kiching hannun ta dafe saman goshinta saboda tsabar yunwa da take ji,Dan lokacin har karfe goma da rabi tayi bata yi break ba.


Kular da suka dawo da ita ta buda yanda ta aje sauran arish din da waina haka ta tadda shi.plate ta dauko ta zuba tare da hada tea ta koma dakinta.



Yasir tunda ya kwanta yake aikin sake-sake a zuciyar shi har bacci yayi gaba da shi ba tare da ya sani ba.


Sosai Dady ya damu da wulakanci da Matar yayan nashi tayi mai,danma Momy na tausar shi tana Kara ba shi hakuri tunda ba dan uwan shi ya nuna hakan ba kawai ya kyaleta kanta tayiwa.



Karfe 11:30 Farrah ta nufi kiching dan dora masu girkin rana.A ciki ta taras da Momy ta kama suka ci gaba jefi jefi suna Dan taba hira.


A hankali Momy ta yi mata wanann tambayar "Wai ni Farrah a tsakanin  Magaji da Jabir wanne kika fi so a cikin su kike jin idan aka aura maki shi zaki iya zama da "?


Sosai ta ji faduwar gaba dan batayi tinanin abinda zata tambayeta ba kenan .


A zuciyar ta tafi son Ya jabir,Amma shi wancan zalimun yanzu Yana bata tausayi musamman wanann rashin lafiyar da yayi,Dukda idan ta tuna abubuwan da ya rinka yimata shekarun baya da suna Abuja sai duk ta ji tana jin haushin shi.


Jin hannun Momy tayi tana girgizata.
 firgigit tayi tana na'am Momy.


Tsira mata ido Momy tayi tana Kara nazarin ta.


"Me kike tunanin "?


"Ba komai" ta bata amsa a gajarce tana mayar da hankalin ta Kan gyara tafannuwa da take yi.


Momy ba tare da ta Kara ce mata komai ba itama taci gaba da aikin.

Suna kammalawa farrah ta wuce dakin ta dan ta Kara kimtsa jikin ta.


Momy ma site din Dady ta haura kafin time din yin lunch yayi su fito.

🅿️24
       📝Tana shiga cikin gidan darect bedroom ta shige zuciyar ta cike da tunani iri-iri akan shawarar da Bilkisu ta bata,Dan haryanzu zuciyarta bata gama aminta ba.


Shigewarta keda wuya na hango Yasir ta jikin windown ko site din shi Yana girgiza Kai eyes din shi kyam a Kan Farrah har saida yaga shigewar ta cikin falon kafin ya saki labulen windown Yana sauke ajiyar zuciya kamar Wanda yayi gudun tsere.

Da lalube ya karasa zama bisa kujerar falon Yana juya wayar dake hannunshi.

Wata nomber yake Kira tare da karawa a kunnen shi.Yana jin anyi picking cikin mutuwar jiki yayi sallama tare da cewa "Momy Ina jin yunwa Kuma bansha magani ba"Yana yamutsa fuska kamar Wanda zaiyi kuka.

A dayan bangaran banji me aka ceba naga ya katse wayar Yana shafa sumar kanshi da tayi luf gwanin kyau a hankali yake rufe idanunshi tare da motsa labban shi ya ce "Beby dole na rabaki da wannan gogaggiyar yarinya,Ba tsarar ki ba ce ta fiki wayo daman nasan ba laifinki ne ba".
Ya karasa Yana Kara mike kafarshi akan doguwar kujera.


Momy ce tayi sallama.Bude idanun shi yayi Yana Amsawa cikin sanyi ya tareta zai amshi tiren kayan abinci ta tsayar da shi da cewa "a a Magaji yi zamanka,wallahi Nusaiba duk ta shantakar da ni shiyasa ban kawoma abinci da wuri ba"ta aje tiren Kan dan table din tana zuba mai abincin.Yana ci suna Dan taba hira har ya karasa ta ba shi magani ya sha.Sai a lokacin ta dube shi tana cewa "Badai wata matsalar ko? .

"Eh ba komai Momy,Amma wannan yarinyar da naga ta zo gurin Beby dan Allah ku raba ta da ita,wallahi Momy daga ganinta ba yarinyar bata da tarbiya Sam bata da ce da su zama freind ba" Yana Kara Kara bata fuska kamar wani mai gida 🤣.


"Yaushe Kuma?wallahi ban San wata yarinya ta shigo nan gidan ba,Amma Bari na tambayeta dan kawarta dai Bilkisu ce na sani kila ita ce"tayi waje ba tare data ji abinda zai ce ba.


A hankali ya mayar da kanshi jikin kujerar ya lumshe idanunshi Yana firzar da iska mai zafi daga bakishi.


Momy dakin Farrah ta kwankwasa.tana budewa taga Momy ce dandanan ta dabirce "ehmm Momy ai na ci abincin tun dazu "tana sosa keya ganin yadda Momy ta tsareta da kallo.


"Wacece ta shigo gidannan ba tare da na saniba".


Kamar saukar arasu haka taji tambayar ta doki dodan kunnenta.

Cikin rawar murya ta ce "Mo..my...ba.fa..wanda..ya shigo nan.".


"Hmmm Farrah kenan wato ba zaki kiyaye duk abinda ake fadamaki ba ko?kullun so kike ki batawa iyayenki da yan'uwan ki rai ko?wallahi Ina jiyemaki wannan halin da kika tsiro,Baki gudin wannan *ZAMANIN* mai cike da kalubale ko ?to nidai nayi iya bakin kokarina ganin na zauna da ke lafiya.Amma Bari Dadyn ku ya dawo Daman gobe zasu je Abuja kawai sai su tafi da ke Nima ko nayi bacci cikin kwanciyar hankali". Tana gama fadin haka ta juya zuwa benen da zai sadata da site din Dady.


Farrah ajiyar zuciya ta sauke a zuciyata tana cewa "na fahimta wato ni kowa ya fara tsanata,na rasa me nayi masu da bazasu barni Nima na ji dadin rayuwata ba?haba Wai meye aibuna?Kullin ni ce mai laifi saika ce ni kadai ce a gidan.


Anan bisa gadon ta kwanta tana cigaba da saka da warwara a cikin zuciyar ta.


Har zuwa dare a cikin gidan ban Kara jin motsin kowa  ba.


A bangaran Hajiya Safiyya bayan ta Kira Momy sun tattauna anan take shaida mata ai Magaji baya jin dadi dazu aka maido shi daga asibiti.

"Ayya wallahi ban saniba ,Amma bari na kirashi yanzu sai na duba shi"cewar Hajiya Safiyya tana katse kiran.


Yana bisa abin sallah ya ji Karan wayar shi hannunshi ya zura ya dauko Yana duba screen dim,ganin Sunan Aunty yasa yayi picking da sauri a zuciyar shi Yana cewa "Diyar ki ta kusan hallaka ni dole nabar garin nan a gobe idan Allah ya kaimu".


Sallamar ta ta ankarar da shi da sauri ya amsa Yana gaisheta.


Bayan sun gama gaisawa tayi mashi ya jiki da tambayar shi yaushe zai dawo.

Ya amsa da "Gobe nake sa ran zan dawo insha Allah".

"To Allah ya kaimu"tana katse kiran.

Da haka shima yayi Shirin bacci tare da duba wayar shi ganin irin Kiran da abokan shi sukayi mai yasa ya kashe wayar gaba daya da tunanin gobe da safe ya Kira su kafin ya wuce.



     *****************

Washe garin lahadi tun karfe 8:00 am na safe Abbah ya iso Barhim dan tafiya Abuja.


Cikin Shiri suka fito tare da Dady suna nufar wurin parking a nan suka hango Yasir ya fito kafadar shi rataye da kayan shi hannun shi rike da wayoyinshi da sauran tarkace.


Yana hango su cikin sauri ya karasa ga iyayen nashi cikin girmamawa ya fara gaishe su suna amsawa suka Kara yimashi ya jiki ya ce "Ai jiki ya warware dan yanzu nake San wucewa Abuja insha Allah".


"Ok muma can zamu tafi yanzu"cewar Dady Bishir.shiko Abbah har ya shige mota dan ji yayi nan take tausayin dan nashi ya lullubeshi.


"Tau saina taho Dady Allah ya kiyaye hanya".


"Amin ya Allah" inji Dady Yana karasawa jikin Motar.

Shima Yasir cikin gidan ya nufa a zuciyar shi Yana tinanin kome zasu je yi,shi dai Allah yasa Auran Beby zasu Nemo ma shi tabbas da yafi kowa farin ciki.


Suko su Dady tuni sun ba motar su wuta tare da Addu'ar Allah ya sauke su lafiya.



Jin falon yayi shiru ba kowa dan haka ya wuce dinning ya fara cin abinci cikin nutsuwa.


 *To fns mu hadu a Abuja* *dan jin yadda abin* *zai kasan* *ce* .

 *Shin ko Iyayen Farrah* *zasu yarda da tsarin da* *su Dady suka je da shi* ?.

🅿️22
     📝 Tunda suka tafi hanya Ummah bata ce uffan ba,haka shima Jabir bai yi magana ba ya ci gaba da tukin shi har suka karasa gida.



Bayan ya budewa iyayen nashi sun shige site din su,shima ya nufi bangaran su zuciyar shi fal da tunanin abinda ke Shirin kunne masu Kai a family din su.



Farrah ce ta fito falo da cakulet a hannunta tana sha   a daya daga cikin kujerun wurin ta zauna,ta mayar da hankalin ta Kan tv.


Waya ta hango an jona ta a chaji tana haske alamar Kira ake yi.A hankali ta mike zuwa wurin riga da siket ne na light blue din atamfa dinkin ya zauna a jikinta das gwanin sha'awa ba daura dankwalin tayi ba tayi follow yin dinshi ne.


Hannu ta saka tana duba wayan Nomber Auntyn ta ta gani akan call din.batasan sanda murmushi ya kufce mata ba dan farinciki Daman tana bukatar jin muryar Auntyn ta ta.


Picking tayi tare da karawa a kunnenta tana yin sallama.

A dayan barin Hajiya Safiyya tana shingide bisa kafet din   babban falonta tana jin muryar Diyar tata tayi saurin mikewa zaune tana cewa  "Farrah meya same ki naji muryar ki tayi wani iri"?.ta jefomata tambayar tana Kara gyara rikon wayar a kunnenta.


"Aunty me kika gani? Ba komai wallahi bacci nayi shiyasa".


"Ok Ina Maman taku"?


"Tana off-site ta saka wayan chage ne anan".

"Ok ya gidan?Baki da wata matsala dai ko?idan kina da matsala ga yayanki nan ki sanar mai kin ji "


"Ehm Aunty ba komai, bana da matsalar komai.idan akwai zan fadama Momy ai".


"To yaushe hutun ku yake karewa"?


"Sai cikin wannan week din ranar Monday zamu koma insha Allah"


"To Allah ya bada sa'a ki dage da karatu kin ji diyata shi ne burin mu Allah yayi maki Albarka"


"Amin" cewar Farrah a ranta tana jin dadin yadda Mamanta take sanya mata albarka.


"Wai baki Dora layin akan wayar ba" cewar Hajiya Safiyya.


"Am ehhm Haryanzu bai kawo min wayar ba,Amma Dady ya ce ya Bari shi zai kawoman". Cewar Farrah tana goge zufar da ta karyomata a goshinta saboda dakyar ta harhada kalmomin bakin ta Dan Bata so Auntyn ta fahimci laifin da ta aikata aka amshe wayoyin nata.


Muryar Auntyn ta maidota daga tunanin da ta lula.

"Tau ba matsala zan Kira Magajin sai muyi magana".


"Tau Aunty  nagode" tana katse kiran tare da sauke ajiyar zuciya.



Tana gama wayar wata dubara ta fadomata a zuciyar ta sai ta nufi bedroom dinta da wayar.



Wata jorter ta dauko da nombers a rubuce,wata nomber ta sanya tana danna call tare da karawa a kunnenta.


Anayin picking ta ce "kawata please helf me wallahi Ina cikin wani yanayi na fara fuskantar matsala Dan Allah ki zo muyi magana".



"Ok ganinan zuwa karki damu insha Allah zamu samu mafita".

Cewar kawar tata tana katse kiran.



Saurin goge nomber tayi ta mayar da wayar inda ta daukota ta zuba abinci a plate ta koma bed room dinta.


Kawar tata tana katse kiran ta gagggabe da dariya tana cewa "Kin kusa zauwa hannunan Dan nayima kaina alkawari sai kinbi layin da nabi,Dan bazan Bata ni kadai ba(wa'iyazubillah kun ji irin sharrin kawaye ko,wallahi irin haka ta sha faruwa a maimakon su yi fatan suma Allah ya shirya su a a kullin burin su suka sun hallakar da wasu daga cikin kawayen su.Allah ka bamu Abokan hulda na gsakiya ba Wanda zasu cutar da mu ba).


Saurayin ta dake kusa da ita Yana wasa da gashin kanta ya kalle ta Yana cewa "My shuger keda wacece kuke waya haka"?.


"Hmmm Bari Kai dai My nida wata shegiyar kawata ce,Wai ita ustaziya bata iya aikata komai kamar ita tafi kowa tsoran Allah,Ai nayima kaina alkawari dole sai na dilmiyar da ita dan bazai yiwu na Bata ni kadai ba.hmmm abin dariya Wai kasan me take ceman?


"A a my shuger sai kin fada Yana Kara Dora kanshi bisa kafadar ta Yana wani lunshe ido kamar Wanda ya sha wani abu".


"Hhhhh shegiyar Wai ita sai takai mutincin ta gidan mijinta saboda ya mutunta ta, Ai wallahi yanda bazan Kai nawa mutincin ba itama sai dai ta Kai ma shi saura,indai boka rusau nanan to sai na cika wannan burin"ta karasa fada tana Kara gaggaba dariya kamar wata sakarai.mikewa tayi ta fara hada kayanta dan zuwa gidan su Farrah.

Ya ce "yanzu Bilkisu tafiya zakiyi ki barni" Yana Kara tsareta da idanunshi irin na Yan maye.

(Sai a lokacin na gane Ashe Bilkisu Abbas ce babbar kawar farrah,kunji fa irin sahrrin kawaye ka yarda da mutum shi Kuma burinshi ya cutar da kai Allah ka karemu daga sharrin kawayen banza).



A bangaran Farrah tana cin abinci Tana kara rayawa a zuciyar ta "indai Bilkisu ta zo tasan zata samar mata mafita dan gaskiya ta gaji danta fahimci Ummah ta fara tsanar ta ita bazata iya jurewa ba.

🅿️23
       ✍️Farrah tana kammala cin abincin ta mayar da plate din kiching ta dawo bisa gadonta tana jiran karasowar freind din ta.



Bilkisu a tare suka fito da saurayinta daga cikin dakin hotel din suka nufi motar shi suna firarsu da dariya.


Suna karasawa bakin gate din Barhim ta gaisa da masu gadin gidan saboda daman sun santa dan suna yawan shigowa da Farrah.ba tare da bata lokaci ba suka budemasu gate suka kutsa hancin motarsu cikin estate din sun danyi tafiya sosai kafin ta kawo su kofar gidan Basu shiga ba anan bakin gate din gidan daga waje ya tsaya.Saida suka Kara holewar su da tsotsar bakin juna kafin suka rabu ta kwankwasa kofar gate din.Baba Auwal lekowa ya fara yi Yana ganin ita ce ya hade Fuska gaisuwar ta ma a ciki ya amsa saboda shi harga Allah bai yarda da tarbiyar yarinyar ba dan daga ganinta suffar munafikai gareta .


Cikin jin haushi ya ce "yarinya lafiya dai ko ?Yana tsareta da ido.


"Ehmm daman na zo wurin Farrah ne,Ai tasan da zuwana ma"ta karashi fadi tana Kara yin kasa da kanta.dan ta fara jin haushin wannan tsohon da alama Yana iya kawomata cikas a plane dinta.

Maganar shi ta ji Yana cewa "Tau ba matsala shiga .


Cikin sanyi ta nufi hanyar da zata sadata da babban falon gidan.


Duk abinda sukeyi Farrah tana hangensu ta window din falon,Duk da akwai tazara Amma kana iya hangowa.tana ganin ya barta ta shigo tayi saurin fitowa daga falon da gudu ta tari kawar tata tana Kara jin farincikin ganinta.Dan tunda sukayi hutu sau uku kadai ta fita( daya ta je GRA ita da Momyn su sai na biyu da aka kaita salun a fita ta ukku ne tace zata je gidan su kawarta ta shi ne ta nufi hotel dan ta hadu da suarayin ta aka samu akasi Yasir ya kirata ta dawo gida ba tare da sun hadu ba).


Sosai itama ta kadandameta tana murnar ganinta hannunta ta ja suka wuce bedroom dinta ta saka ki a jikin kofar dan karma ayi rashin sa'a wani ya shigo ya ji abinda suke fada.


Fridge ta nufa drinks da swan water masu sanyi ta kawomata bisa dan karamin table din wurin ta aje tana zama kusa da ita .


Kallanta tayi tana cewa "kawata kinga yadda kika Kara kyau kinyi wani fresh da ke kaman amarya? Tana Kara taba fuskarta.


"Hmmm kedai ki zaunan nan kullum kina gida kamar bokanya,Baki da lokacin da zaki fita kidan shakata ke Bari ki ji ai ni yanzu nafi amarya saboda yanda nake shanawa ko amarya bata samun haka,ga kudi suna shigoman tako Ina bani da matsala ko nawa nake da bukata mutanena zasu cikamin jakata da cash kinga ko me zai dame ni"tana Kara girgiza jikin ta.


Hmmm a hankali Farrah ta sauke ajiyar zuciya tana Kara duban kawarta ta ta ce"hmm ke mazaki gane ba wallahi kinsan yanzinma wata matsalar ce tasa nace kizo muji yadda za'ayi danni gaskiya na ga ji. Wannan zalimun Kara matsaman yake yi ga Umman su harta fara tsana ta Ina dalili Kuma ko ni wallahi ba cewa nayi Ina San danta ba".Ta karasa fada fuskar ta cike da damuwa.


Bilkisu Kara matsowa tayi ta dafa kafadarta tana kallan idanunta dan so takeyi shawarar da zata bata tayi tasiri a zuciyar ta da gangar jikinta.
Cikin salo irin na gogaggun Yan bariki ta ce "Ai kawata naga kamar har yanzu Baki gama yarda da ni ba,saboda duk shawarar da nake baki bakya amfani da ita shiyasa ko abubuwa suke Kara tabarbare maki ko,idan kinyi dubi dani kinga yanzu bani da wata damuwa harkokin gabana kawai nake yi,Kuma shi aure da kike magana idan lokaci yayi duk zamiyi,Amma yanzu abinda nake so da ke ki kwantar da hankalin ki sannan duk shawarar dana baki idan har kin bita to bake ba sauran damuwa kina jina ko?ta karashe karshen maganar tana kashe murya.


Ita ko Farrah kamar kurma haka ta koma sai Kai ta gyadamata alamar tana jinta.


"Da farko ko aure suka ce zasu yimaki ki nuna kefa ba yanzu ba sai kin kammala karatun ki kin fahimta?


"Eh na fahimta am........


Da sauri ta katseta da cewa "Amma me ?ke matsalata da ke kenan gardama idan Kuma baki shirya amsar shawarata ba shikenan kinga tafiyata"ta dauki Jakarta tana nufar bakin kofar dakin.


Da sauri Farrah ta tarota tana "Haba kawata kar muyi haka dake mana dawo dan Allah a wanan karon nayi maki alkawari zan dauki shawarar ki kin ji"tana Kara rike jakarta.


Sosai ta tsuramata idanun dan ta gaskata abinda ta ji ta fada .Dan haka ta Kara kashe murya irin ta tausayi ta ce "A a Farrah kawai ki bari tunda har yanzu baki daukeni matsayin yar uwa ba ba komai ke kadai matsalar zata dama danni hankali na a kwance yake".


"Sorry my Friend "cewar Farrah tana janta zuwa bakin gadon.


Bayan sun zauna Bilkisu ta kalli Farrah ta ce yanzu kin shirya amsar shawata ko.

Da sauri ta gyada mata Kai alamar ta amimce.


"To idan na fara magana bana San gardama cewar Bilkisu".


"Karki damu kawata ai nasan ba zaki bani shawara banza ba" cewar Farrah da murmushi saman fuskarta.


Hakan yasa Bilkisu yin wani murmushi da ita kadai tasan ma"ma'anar shi.ta ce gaba da cewa "Da farko dai sai kin tsefare ma'ana sai kin daina tsoran kowa kin cire duk wani tsoro ko shakku da kike ji a cikin zuciyar ki,Kuma meye a ciki ke Bari ki ji in fadamaki da kin saba kin zama yar hannu da kanki zaki rinka nema.kuma meye a ciki dan munje gidan miji fanko ba abinda zai faru,Kuma yanzu zamanin nan da mutun yasha maganin mata shikenan miji bazai taba ganewa ba (ka ji wani batan basira da yammatan wannan zamanin ke yi).

Ta ci gaba da cewa "sannan batun yadda zaki rinka fita wanan karki damu ni nasan yadda zan bullowa lamarin,yanzu ai kince ya amshe wayar ki ko?


"Eh wlh tunda ya amshe bai mayar min ba har yanzu" cewar Farrah tana kwal kwal da ido kamar zata yi kuka.


"To karki wani damu komai zakiyi kiyi kokari a barki ki je school ranar Monday Nima insha Allah zan je to duk sauran bayani zamu karasa tattaunawa kin ji" tana kallan Idanun Farrah da itama ita kadai take kallo.


Cikin sanyi ta gyadamata Kai dan ita harga Allah har yanzu zuciyarta nayimata wasi wasi akan wannan shawarar Amma bata so tayi musu Dan kar kawarta ta tayi fushi da ita.

Cikin sanyinta ta ce "To ba matsala kawata insha Allah zanyima Momy magana ta ansoman key din mota na sai na je mu hadu ko?

"Yauwa ko kefa" cewar Bilkisu a zuciyar ta tana murna hakarta ta kusa cimma ruwa,Dan ita a kullun da wannan burin take kwana tasan ta Kara samun hanyar da zata rinka samun makudan kudade dan duk Alhaji da ta hada shi da Farrah to tasan ba makawa sai yayimata kyauta mai tsoka,Daman tana jin haushin yadda ta fita kyan sura da kyakkyawar fuska.


Ji tayi Farrah ta dafa kafadar ta "kawata me kike tinani"?


"Eh ahm ba komai kawata"tayi saurin wayancewa dan karta fahimci akwai wani abu.


Daga haka suka ci gaba da tattaunawa tana Kara bata shawarwarin banza.ita ko tana Kara dauka.


Lemun ta zuba mata a cup "kawata zaki tafi ko ruwa baki sha ba"

"No karki damu kawata kinsan tun jiya Ina tare da my shuger dan haka bana bukatar komai" ta karasa fada tana Kara kashe idonta daya.


Cikin mamaki Farrah ta ce "Kuma yanzu nan idan kika je gida baza'ayi maki fada ba?

"Hmmmm Farrah kenan ke har yanzu kina abu kamar baki San yadda duniyar take ba,Ai ko su Yan gidanmu ban barsu hakanan ba saida na tabbatar na rufe masu baki kafin na fara wannan harkar "


"Kamar ya"cewar Farrah tana Kara mamaki a zuciyar ta.

"Hmmm kinga mubar wannan maganar sai mun hadu a school duk zan yimaki bayani kin ji"ta fada tana murmushin mugunta.


"To ba damuwa kawata Allah ya kaimu" cewar Farrah.


Mikewa Bilkisu tayi ta dauki Jakarta tare da wayarta suka fito sakamakon Kiran da saurayinta yayi mata cewar Yana jiranta a kofar gidan.


Har bakin gate Farrah ta rakota suna ta labari kafin suka rabu ta koma cikin gida ita Kuma Bilkisu ta shige mota suka fita da Barhim.

🅿️26
      📝A kujerun da sukayiwa faffadan falon kawanya suka yi  masauki,saboda sun sauke faralinsu tun akan hanyar kaduna.


Mai aikin gidan tahau hidimar jera masu abinci kala kala tare da abin sha.

Bayan Alhaji Ubah ya shigo sun gaisa cikin mitinta juna,Domin Yana girmama Yan uwar matar shi saboda yadda suka dauke shi kamar dan uwan su kasancewar shima mahaifan shi sun rasu sai Yan'uwan uba gareshi dan haka da farko yasha gwagwarmayar rayuwa daga karshe har Allah ya taimake shi ya zama wani abu.Dan har a kwanan gobe Yan uwan shi basu kaunar shi musamman da sukaga irin daukakar da Allah yayi mai.hakan yasa yafi shiga cikin dangin mahaifiyar shi wadda yar asalin wani kauye ce anan yankin Garin na Abuja.



Bayan sun gama gaggaisawa Aunty Safiyya tayimasu jagora zuwa dinning ta zuzzuba masu abinci cikin farinciki.Saboda yayyun nata sune jigonta idan tana ganinsu takan ji kewar iyayen nata ta ragu a zuciyar ta.


Tana gama suba masu sama ta haura dakin mai gidan nata ta basu wuri dan su samu damar cin abincin.


Sunci abincin sosai ba tare da sunyi karaba dukda gidan kanwar su ne,Tunda a ganinsu yanzu an zama daya.


Aunty safiya ce suke saukowa falon ita da mai gidan nata,Dukan su a falon suka hadu bayan an Kara gaggaisawa tare da tambayar iyali sai Alhaji Usman yayi gyaran murya tare da fara bayanin dalilin zuwansu kakar haka:To alhmdlillah kamar yadda muka sanar daku zamu zo,Allah cikin ikon shi ga shi mun samu damar zuwa. 

Ya ci gaba da cewa "A gaskiyar al'amari dalilin da yasa muka zo akan maganar yarinyar mu ne Aminatu wato Farrah (taci sunan mahaifiyar Alhaji Ubah ne shiyasa ake kiranta da Farrah).Saboda irin matsalolin da suke neman kunno Kai,Shi ne mukaga ya kamata ace mun sanar da ku don gudun abinda zai je ya zo.Dukda Ina ganin akwai hanyar da ya kamata mubi dan samo mafita Amma dole duk hukuncin da zamu yanke saida saninku saboda kunfi mu hakki a kanta nasan duk yadda mukayi da Farrah to Ina da yakinin ba zaku watsa mana kasa a ido ba.Amma kunji duk abinda ke faruwa ya fara basu labari kamar yadda ya ji daga bakin Momy........har zuwa wayar da Dady ya ritseta tana yi a daki na Shirin haduwa da saurayinta ba tare da sun saniba.

Daga nan ya dora da cewa "kunga kenan a Koda wani lokaci komai na iya faruwa,Dukda ba wayonmu yake tsare Mana iyali ha Allah si yake Kare manasu da Kuma addu'ar da muke yi".

Sannan a Ina ganin tunda Allah ya horemana abinda zamu aurar da yaranmu ta a wannan ZAMANIN idan Allah yaba yaranmu mata miji to mu aurar da su ko ya kuka ce". Ya karasa fadi yana duban Alhaji Ubah.


A hankali Alhaji Ubah ya sauke ajiyar zuciya tare da cewar "Ai Yaya dan wannan Ina gani ba matsala ba ne,kasan wannnan *ZAMANIN* namu wani shike bata tarbiyar wani shiyasa banyi mamaki da jin wannan batu ba,Saboda lokacin tana secondary school bata da wannan rashin jin maganar Amma kasan yau da gobe ga Kuma kuruciya ta yarinta,Amma wallahi ni Yaya kamar yadda kuka dauke ni tamkar Dan'uwan ki to na barmaku Farrah duk hukuncin da kuka yanke a kanta yayi,Dan nima bazan so ace diyata ce take kokarin bijirewa irin haka na,Amma dan Allah Ina neman wata alfarma guda daya"


"Haba Ubaidullah ai a tsakanin mu da Kai  kafi karfin neman alfarma,Dan haka ka fadi koma meye insha Allah zamuyi maka"Cewar Dady da sai yanzu ya saka baki a al'amarin.


" Yaya ai ba wani babban abune ba kawai dai Ina so duk Wanda zaku auramat Dan Allah ya barta ta karasa karatunta ,Dan shi ne babban burina naga ta samu ingattaccen ilimi Koda kuwa ba zata yi aikin gwamnati ba".


"Karka damu Ubaidullah indan wannan ne insha Allah ba matsala dan kasan muma da bokon nan  muka tashi insha Allah ba abinda zai taba karatunta"cewar Alhaji Usman.


Kanwar tasu ya duba cikin so da kauna irin ta yan'uwan taka ya ce "kanwata muna ta tattaunawa ke har yanzu baki ce komai ba"? Da murmushi a saman fuskar shi.


"Tau Yaya ni kam ba abinda zance dan kune iyayen Farrah dan haka ni a nawa bangaren wallahi duk hukuncin da kuka yanke mata yayi,Tunda ita bata jin magana tanason biyewa rudin *ZAMANIN* yimata auren shi ne kawai mafita,Dan haka sai dai nace Allah ya Kara karemana iyali"


Dukan su suka amsa da Amin.


"To amma Yaya kana ganin tana da Wanda take so ko kuwa"? Cewar Alhaji Ubah.


Dady ne yayi saurin cewa "gaskiya ni bana barinta tana kula samari dan ko shi yaron da naji tana waya da shi wallahi ban san Inda ta samo shi ba,Kuma mu bazamu iya hadata da shi ba,Dan haka a ganina tunda ga Magaji me zai hana mu hada su tunda daman shima ana ta faman ya fitar da mata abin ya gagara kaga shikenan basai munba juna wahala ba".


"To amma Yaya kana ganin Yana santa dan kasan shi namiji ba'a yima shi auren dole"cewar Baban Farrah Yana duban yan'uwan matar tashi dan jin amsar da zasu ba shi.


Abbah ya ba shi amsa da "Ai dukan su bama bukatar amimcewar su tunda shi kanshi da yake babba Yana yimana wasa da hankali da an tambaye shi sai ya ce a Kara mai lokaci zai kawo,To wannan shi ne kawai abinda ya kamata ayi"(Kaico Jabir dinmu ba Wanda ya tina da kai,shin Wai su Dady ko sun manta kaima kana santa?).


Da haka dai suka ci gaba da tattaunawa inda hankalin Hajiya Safiyya ya kasa kwanciya a gameda 'al'amuran tilon diyarta ta har a karshe ta nemi Alfarmar kafin su tafi Dan Allah a daura wannan auren ko hankalin ta yafi kwanciya (kunsan tsakanin 'da' da mahaifiya sai Allah musamman idan aka ce diya mace ce to hankali yafi ta shi).


Cikin ikon Allah yayyun nata suka amince da bukatar ta inda sukayi zasu kwana Kara kwana zuwa gobe idan an daura aure sai su wuce katsina kafin zu yanke shawarar lokacin daya kamata ayi hidimar biki.



A bangaran Yasir tunda ya shige site din shi kokarin yin wanka yayi tare da yin sallar azahar da aka biyo shi saboda tsabar gajiya da yayi anan bisa abin sallah bacci ya dauke shi,shi ne bai Farka ba sai da yaji Kiran sallar la'asar ta cikin masallacin unguwar.

Hakan yasa ya tashi da Addu'a a bakinshi.
Yana Kara dauro Arwalla ya fito,Daidai su Dady suma sun fito daga cikin gidan dan zuwa masallaci anan ya bisu suka wuce a tare.A zuciyar shi Yana tinanin da ya dawo yaje ya ci abinci saboda irin kugin da cikin shi yake yi.




 ```Kai fns akwai fa rigima``` ```dan indai aka``` ```hana Jabir din mu``` ```Auren Farrah``` ```to sai anyi``` ```yakin basasa fa``` 🤣🤣🤣

 *_Aunty Safiyya karfa_* *_kuyi tinanin kunyi* *wenner_ _eheee dan* *akwai_ _rigima_* 😏😏😏.

🅿️25
        📝Yana gaf da karasa cin abinci Momy ta fito ganin jakarshi akan kujera yasa tayi turus a wurin tana kallanshi.


"Momy Ina kwana "ya mike da teshu a hannun shi.

"Lafiya Lau badai har tafiya zakayi ba"


"Wallahi ko Momy gobe Ina da aiki a office dukda ansanar masu,To sai nake ganin tunda da sauki gwamma kawai na tafi" Daidai ya rataya jakar sa a kafada.



"To ni Magaji sai dai na ce Allah ya kiyaye,sannan ka tafi da magungunan ka"?


"Na dauko su insha Allah zan rinka sha"Yana kokarin daura agogo a tsintsiyar hannunshi.


"Hmmm Ai bari kaji idan har bazaka rage wannan shegen tunanin ba to kullin kana hanyar ganin likita,Kuma ka dage da addu'a domin neman zabin Allah insha Allah komai zai wuce".tana Kai karshen maganar Farrah ta fito daga dakinta tana kokarin daura karamin dankwali a kanta.



Bata san da mutane a falon ba Dan haka  ta wucewarta kiching.


Da  manyan eyes din shi ya bita da kallo harta shige.


Sosai yaba Momy Tausayi,Duk da bata San asalin abinda ya hadasu ba Amma tana tabbatar kanta ba karamin so yake yiwa Farrah ba.


Kallanta ta maido gareshi ganin har yanzu hankalinshi baya wurin yasa tayi gyaran murya.Amma da yake Yasir namijin duniya ne sai yayi saurin wayancewa Yana sosa keya alamar jin kunya.


Momy murmushi tayi tana cewa "Wallahi kada ka bari wannan tinanin ya hanaka yin aikinka idan ba haka ba zan yima Jabir campen"ta karasa fada tana yimashi dariyar zolaya.


Murmushi yayi Yana Kara shafa Dan madaidaicin gemunshi"A a Momyna ai nasan bazaki yiman haka ba idan ko hakane to daga Abuja sai dai acemaku na mutu kawai.


Har a lokwcin Farrah Bata fito ba.

Ita kuwa sai da ta zo fitowa bayan ta dauko kanifarin da zatayi amfani da shi ta ji maganar shi Hakan yasa tayi saurin komawa saboda yadda taji gabanta ya fadi,  a ciki  ranta tana addu'ar Allah yasa gidan zai bari gaba daya ita kam yau da taji dadinta.



Har suka fita waje da Momy duk tana jin su irin hirar da suke yi a ranta ta ce "Ji shi Dan lambo kamar jiya ba shi ne aka kwantar a asibiti ba?Amma a fuska idan ka ganshi simi-simi kamar baya magana nan ko tsabar miskilanci ne cikin ranshi.Buntsiro Baki ta fice daga kiching din.



Momy har bakin mota ta raka Yasir tana kara yimashi nasiha tare da nunamashi mahimmancin addu'a da hakuri,Kafin tayi mai sallama.
Har ya tayar da motar Yana daga mata hannu.

Bayan yayima Baba Auwal alheri na kudi ya doshi fita daga estate din bakin shi dauke da addu'a Yana jin wani saban kuzari Yana kara sauko mai saboda irin shawararin da Momy ta Kara ba shi.shiyasa har gobe yake jin dadin zama da ita sabanin Ummahn su da take da fada,Ga shi Bata Basu lokacin ta ballantana ta fahimci damuwar shi.


Da wannan tinanin ya fara fita daga garin katsina ya Kara gudun motar shi dan so yake ya taras da su Dady a can kafin su juyo.



A bangaran Farrah kuwa tana jin fitar motar shi ta hau tsalle saboda tsabar  farincikin daya lullubeta.


Tana cikin murna har bata san Momy ta shigo ba.

Hannuwanta ta harde a kirji tana kallan ikon Allah a ranta tana saka yadda Yasir zai sha dabir da wannan yarinyar dan ta fahimci rashin jituwar dake tsakanin su ita ta hana Farrah fahimtar irin San da yake yimata.


Tana cikin tsallanta kamar ance ta kalli bakin kofar taga Momy a tsaye tana kallanta.

Sosai taji kunya ta kamata Dan tasan Momy ta fahimci abinda take yiwa murna.


Cikin daure fuska Momy tace mata "Wallahi kinji kunya Farrah saboda dan'uwan ki ya tafi shiyasa kike wannan murnan ko?ke ko kunya bakya ji?shiko bawan Allah duk abinda kika ce kina so Yana rawar jiki yake siyamaki,Amma saboda Baki tsoran Allah baki da wanda kika tsana nan duniyar irin shi,to wallahi kibi a hankali dan akwai ranar kin dillanci.ke sakarai har yanzu baki San ciwon kanki ba hmmm Allah dai ya shirya ki.Tana kaiwa karshen nan ta wuce site dinta tabar Farrah anan da duk kunyar duniya ta gama rufeta.


Tana ganin ta shige ta ce "Ai kema dan bakisan irin cutar da yayiman ba shiyasa,Ai mugun nasan alhakina ne yake bibiyar sa shiyasa duk ya zama abin tausayi,Niko hankali na kwance Allah yasa daga can ya wuce zan"tana Kara murguda Baki kamar Yana gabanta.(ihu bayan hari kenan tunda Baki iya yi a gaban shi 🤣🤣) .Ta shige bedroom dinta zuciyarta cike da farinciki.





               _____________________


Su Dady Karfe 12:20am daidai ta kawosu birnin tarayya Abuja daret unguwa su Aunty Safiyya suka nufa kasancewar unguwar masu kudi ce hakan yasa ba wata hayaniya kowa yana cikin gidan shi.

Daidai wani tamfatsetsen Ash din gate naga sunyi horn mai gadi ne ya bude masu daman Hajiya Safiyya ta sanar da shi zuwan yan'uwan nata,bayan sun gaisa Yana yimasu barka da zuwa ,dan haka ba tare da bata lokaci ba suka kutsa Kan motar su harabar gidan.




Akwai ta zara daga bakin gate din zuwa cikin gidan dan haka inda aka tana dan ajiye motoci anan suka yiwa tasu masauki.


Suna firfitowa kanwar tasu ta karaso cikin murna dan ganin isowar Yan uwan nata.


"Alhamdlillah Allah na godema daka kawomin Yan"uwana lafiya"cewar Hajiya Safiyya tana karasowa gare su.


Farin cikin fuskarsu ya karu ganin yar kanwar tasu cikin kwanciyar hankali.


"Sannunku da zuwa Yaya"


"Yauwa kanwarmu kinganmu sai yanzu ko"


"Wallahi yaya kunyi sauri ma,ku taho mu shiga ciki"


Suna kokarin juyawa suka hango mai gadi ya bude gate alamar akwai Wanda zai shigo,a tare suka juya dan ganin waye.


Motar Yasir sukaga ta shigo,Dan haka basuyi mamaki ba dan sunsan shi Idan ya kama sitiyarin ba wasa.


Jiranshi sukayi ya Kara so aunty tana cewa"yanzu soon saida ka taho saboda rigima ko?

Shidai baice komai ba saboda gajiyar da yayi ga shi Yana so ya watsa ruwa a jikinshi ya sauke faralin dake kanshi.


Gaba dayansu suka dunguma zuwa cikin gidan,shi Kuma Yasir ya wuce site din da yake sauka.

🅿️27
     📝Bayan sun dawo daga masallaci gaba dayansu suka koma cikin gidan inda Yasir yasa mai aikin gidan ta kawomai abinci su ko su Dady anan suka ci gaba da hirarsu ba tare da sun sanar da Yasir abinda yake faruwa  ba.


A nata bangaran Farrah  yinin wannan ranar tayishi ne cikin jin dadi dan ji take yi kamar karya dawo,wani bangare na zuciyarta Kuma baya yimata dadi ta rasa kome yasa data tuna cizon da yayi mata a yatsunta biyu sai duk ta ji gaba daya cikar jikinta tana tashi lokaci guda sai ta ji duk jikinta ya dau sanyi kamar wadda zata yi masassara.Da haka dai takai har zuwa dare ba tare data sanarwa da Momy ba,Dan ta fahimci kamarma fishi take yi da ita yau dan tun fadan data yimata da safe bata Kara shiga harkarta ba.



Jabir bawan Allah a wannan ranar kasa jurewa yayi har karfe takwas na dare Yana tinanin Farrah dan haka ko fadawa Ummahn su bai yiba ya tayar da motar shi ya dauki hanyar da zata sada shi da Barhim.



       _________________


Bayan sallar isha'i su Dady bayan sun dawo daga masallaci nan suka ci abinci tare da Alhaji Ubah ya raka su zuwa masaukin su na wani bangare a cikin gidan aka gyara masu,zuwa wannan lokacin Alhaji Ubah ya sanar da Yan uwan baban shi duk da auren ne ya zo a Kai tsaye shiyasa ya basu hakuri akan ba yanzu za'ayi taron bikin ba sai ta karasa karatunta,Dan haka basu wani damuba sun amsa da zasu turo mutum biyu su wakilce su.sosai ya ji dadi dan bayyi tinanin zasu zo ba.




Yasir ne ya shigo gidan a cikin motar shi saboda tunda ya karasa cin abinci ya fita cikin gari sai yanzu da dare yake dawowa.


Cikin gidan ya nufa ba kowa a falon dan haka yayi nufin juyawa ya ji aunty ta tsayar da shi daga off-site take tahowa dan haka ya karaso cikin falon.


"Yo Ina kaje na jika shiru nayi tinanin ko kana part dinka"tana kokarin zama bisa kujera.


"A a nadan fitane tun dazu shiyasa".


"Ok kunyi waya da yan gidan ko"?

"A a ban Kira su ba,Amma zuwa anjima na Kira Momy insha Allah,ko wani abu ya faru"?


"A a ba komai wallahi".
Nan dai suka ci gaba da taba hira har wurin karfe tara da yan mintina kafin yayi mata sallama ya nufi makwancin shi itama ta wuce wurin mai gidan nata.


Jabir Yana shiga gidan bayan sun gaisa da Baba Auwal ya wuce falon,Ba kowa Dan haka ya nufi dakin Farrah.

Yana turawa yaji a rufe dan haka yayi knowking ya ji shiru ya Kara yi.

A hankali ta bude kofar tana kokarin bude idanta da suka yi nauyi saboda baccin daya fara daukarta.


"A ya Jabir Kai ne yanzu"tana Kara bude kofar.

"Eh ko na koma idan ba"a so na zo ba"?


"Sorry sir ni na isa na ce banso ka zo ba,Ai wallahi nafi kowa farin cikin ganinka,Bari kaga na dauko hijaf dina musha hira dan daman na gaji da zaman kadai ci shiyasa bacci ya fara dauka ta" juyawa taji cikin dakin dan dauko hijaf din.shiko kaman sakarai yabi bayanta da kallo yanda hips dinta yake juyawa kamar na babbar mace.da sauri ya dauke kanshi Yana juyawa zuwa falo dan ya jirata.


Tana daukowa taga baya bakin kofa itama saita nufo falon.

Tana dariya ta zauna a kujerar da ya zauna tana dariya ta ce "To ni meye abin bata rai kuma,?kamar Wanda aka yiwa laifi eh?

"Haushi naji shiyasa Kuma idan kina yiman dariya yanzu zan koma inda na fito 😔"

Sosai dariya ta kamata Amma dole ta gimtseta saboda tasan halinshi Yana iya tafiyar shi kamar yadda ya fada .

Kasan kafe ta sauko kusa da kafafun shi ta rike kunnuwanta tana "Baba na tuba Baba,Baba bazan Kara ba"

Sosai ya tuntsire da dariya dan lokutta da dama idan yaga matar na wani shirmen ya kanyi mamaki kamar yarinya take komawa,shiyasa take Kara shiga ranshi saboda yadda take sakin jiki suyi ta hira.


Yana cikin dariya ya ce "Tau matar na yafe maki kin ji"Yana kallan yadda take murmushi.


Daga haka suka ci gaba da hirarsu gwanin sha'awa kafin yayi mata sallama ya tafi.Dan haka ranar cikin farinciki tayi bacci.



    __________________


Washe garin ranar talata da karfe 10:00 daidai aka daura auren Yasir Usman Hashim da Amina Ubaidullah (Farrah) akan sadaki naira dubu dari lakadan ba ajalan ba.
Inda yan uwan Alhaji Ubah suka zo har su hudu yaji dadin zuwan su kwarai dan ko ba komai sun fitar da shi kunya.


Suko su Abbah da Dady sun buga waya dutsanma Dan sanar da su abinda ya faru ba tare da bata lokaci ba su kawu Yakubu suka amince tare da yimasu uziri da fatan alkhairi.


A daidai wannan lokacin Yasir yana office zaune ya gagara yin aiki saboda yadda yaji kirjin shi Yana haurawa da sauri da sauri kaman zai fashe, addu'a yayi tayi Allah yasa alkhairi ne a bangare daya Kuma Yana tuna abar kaunar shi wato Farrah ko kadan baya san yana yin nesa da ita dan ba yadda zai yi ne ga shi tunda ya taho hankalin shi ya kasa kwanciya saboda gani yakeyi tunda bayanan zata yi yadda taga dama,Dukda yana kokarin cire wannan zargin daga zuciyar shi Amma har kullin ya kasa,Amma a karshe ya yanke shawarar zai je gidan aunty Husna su tattauna akan matsalar.


Su Dady ana kammala daurin aure suka yima kanwar tasu sallama suka dauki hanyar dawowa garin katsina.


Farrah ce da Momy suke girki a kichinga dan taryar su Dady da sukayi waya da Momy cewar gasunan sun taho tare da sanar da ita duk yadda abubuwan suka faru.

Momy bata yi mamaki ba ,Amma tana hango yadda za'a sha dagi da Farrah kafin ta amince da. Wannan auren dan ta fahimci har yanzu bata San tana San Yasir ba saboda shakuwar da suka yi da Jabir.

Aiki sukeyi cikin kwarewa Momy ta mayar da kallanta gare ta "Farrah na fahimci sai anyi maki aure kafin zaki fara yin hankali"

Da sauri ta kalli Momy har tana sakin wukar da take yanka kabej.

"Haba Momy ai ni saina karasa makaranta kafin ayiman aure,Kuma ma ni Ya Jabir nake so dan shi wancan Hasan din naga kaman ba da gaske yake sona ba,Hmm ai ni yanzu bama zan yarda ayiman wani aure ba a kasheni tun Ina da kuruciya ta,a a nasan ma bazai yiwuba ".


"Hmmm shima Jabir din kin rabu da shi"Momy a zuciyarta take wannan maganar.
Amma a fili sai ta ce mata "kedai ki nemi zabin Allah,Dan lokutta da dama abinda muke so ba shine alkhairin muba,Amma idan muka roki Allah sai ya bamu abinda zai zame Mana alkahairi har karshen rayuwar mu".


"Hmm to Momy Allah dai yasa mu dace "

"Amin ya Allah".

Daga haka suka ci gaba da aikin su.





     ✍️✍️✍️✍️✍️✍️

Su Dady ana kammala daurin aure suka yima kanwar tasu sallama suka dauki hanyar dawowa garin katsina.


Farrah ce da Momy suke girki a kichinga dan taryar su Dady da sukayi waya da Momy cewar gasunan sun taho tare da sanar da ita duk yadda abubuwan suka faru.

Momy bata yi mamaki ba ,Amma tana hango yadda za'a sha dagi da Farrah kafin ta amince da. Wannan auren dan ta fahimci har yanzu bata San tana San Yasir ba saboda shakuwar da suka yi da Jabir.

Aiki sukeyi cikin kwarewa Momy ta mayar da kallanta gare ta "Farrah na fahimci sai anyi maki aure kafin zaki fara yin hankali"

Da sauri ta kalli Momy har tana sakin wukar da take yanka kabej.

"Haba Momy ai ni saina karasa makaranta kafin ayiman aure,Kuma ma ni Ya Jabir nake so dan shi wancan Hasan din naga kaman ba da gaske yake sona ba,Hmm ai ni yanzu bama zan yarda ayiman wani aure ba a kasheni tun Ina da kuruciya ta,a a nasan ma bazai yiwuba ".


"Hmmm shima Jabir din kin rabu da shi"Momy a zuciyarta take wannan maganar.
Amma a fili sai ta ce mata "kedai ki nemi zabin Allah,Dan lokutta da dama abinda muke so ba shine alkhairin muba,Amma idan muka roki Allah sai ya bamu abinda zai zame Mana alkahairi har karshen rayuwar mu".


"Hmm to Momy Allah dai yasa mu dace "

"Amin ya Allah".

Daga haka suka ci gaba da aikin su.





     ✍️✍️✍️✍️✍️✍️

🅿️29
      📝Cikin ikon Allah yana Kiran wayar yaji ta shiga,Bayan ya daga ba tare daya saurari gaisuwar da yake yimasa ba ya fara cewa "please Doctor ka taimaka Mana kar yarinyar mutane ta rasa ranta,Dan Allah kayi sauri ka zo " Yana goge zufar take tsatstsafowa daga goshin shi dukda sanyin A/C da yake kadawa dan gaskiya lamarin ya fara ba shi tsoro.


"Ok ranka dade ganinan zuwa insha Allah nan da 10 minutes zan karaso".

Katse wayar yayi Yana kaiwa da komowa a tsakiyar falon.Inda Momy ke rungume da Farrah a Kan cinyarta.


Suna a haka sukaga ji karar bude gate da sauri Dady yayi hanyar waje dan ganin ko Doctor Umar din ne ya karaso.

Daidai ya fito harabar gidan yaga Motar tana kokarin shigowa dan haka ya tsaya har yayi parking Yana fitowa ya nufeshi Yana "Dan Allah ka taimaka Mana wallahi kanwata ita kadai ta haifa idan ta rasata muma zamu iya rasa kanwar mu"

"Karka damu insha Allah zata rayu addu'ar mu take bukata"daidai sun karaso falon.
Cikin kwarewa Doctor Umar ya fito da kayan aikin shi ya fara Bata taimakon gaggawa.

Yana hada allura ya ce a miko mai ruwa a hannun shi yasa Yana shafa mata a fuska Amma shiru kake ji dukda Yana da tabbacin bata mutu ba doguwar sumace tayi saboda jijiyar hannunta tana motsawa.

Yadau tsawon 2 minutes Yana shafa mata ruwan Kanan taja wani dogon numfashi tana fisge-fisge .
Da sauri Momy ta Kara rike ta sosai tana Kiran sunan ta tana jin maganar Momy saita fashe da kuka.

"Kara riketa sosai karta fisge "cewar Doctor Umar Yana Kara hada allura a siringi.


Cikin dabara ya samu ya cikaramata allurar Yana zare siringin ta karasa zubewa a jikin Momy kamar wadda ta mutu.

"Allurar bacci ce insha Allah idan ta farka ba matsala, akwai abinda ya tsorata ta ne shiyasa tayi doguwar suma ya rubuta wasu magungunan a karamar takadda tare da mikawa Dady Yana hade kayanshi wuri guda.

Sai a sannan Dady ya nisa dan tunda suka shigo bai Kara cewa komai ba.

"To Doctor kana ganin ba matsala dai ko"

"Eh insha Allah munasa ran ba matsala dan sai nan da 2 hours zata farka,Amma a fara bata abinci kafin a bata magunguna".


"To Doctor mun gode ba damuwa insha Allah,zamuyi magana da Kai daga baya"cewar Dady suna fitowa daga cikin gidan.kowa motar shi ya hau


Motar Doctor na gaba ta Dady na biye suna fitowa shi Doctor yabi titin mani dan komawa gidan shi,Shi Kuma Dady ya nufi titin kwado Dan zuwa *FARIDA CEMIST* siyan magunguna.


Suna fita Momy ta Kara gyara mata kwanciyar ta tare da sanya mata filon kujera a kanta,Ta zauna tana nazarin wannan al'amari.



  

           **************

A Abuja kuwa ta bangaran Yasir Yana kammala cin abincin ya wuce part din shi dan kimtsa jikin shi,Saboda duk ji yake Yana yimashi ciwo.

Shi ne bai fito ba sai wuraren karfe 8 na dare part din Aunty ya Kara komawa dan amsa Kiran da Mijin Auntyn tashi yake mai Wanda yake addu'ar nan gaba ya zama sirikin shi In Allah ya yarda.


A main falo ya taddasu dan haka da sallama ya karasa ciki tare da durkusawa saman kafet din kafafun shi a tonkwashe Yana Kara sadda kanshi kasa alamar akwai nutsuwa tattare da shi.

Bayan ya Kara gaishe su ya yi shiru Dan jin me Sirikin na shi zan ce.


Cikin dattijanta ka Alhaji Ubah yayi gyaran murya tare da fara yimashi nasiha mai ratsa jiki,Har Yasa Yasir ya fara zargin ko wani ya Mutu a cikin familyn nasu,Amma kafin ya karasa tunanin yaji Alhaji Yana cewa "Kayi hakuri Magaji mun yanke wani hukunci ba tare da shawarar ka ba,Dan ni harga Allah Kai nafi ji ba Farrah ba,Dan namiji ba'ayimai auren dole,Amma da farko ina fara baka hakuri a jiya aka dauramaka aure da Farrah.

Cikin razana Yasir ya dago kanshi yana kallan Alhaji ubah,Fuskar shi ba yabo ba fallasa dan bazaka iya tantance yanayin da yake ciki ba.


"To Amma dan Allah karka dauka munyi haka ne dan mu takurama sai dan Kai na gida ne munsan ba zaka bamu kunya ba"cewar Alhaji Ubah yana duban Yasir cikin tsanaki.
Sai a sannan Aunty sis tayi magana "Kayi shiru Magaji ko hadin baiyimaka ba"?


"Am ehm ba komai wallahi Allah ya tabbatar Mana da alkhairinsa"Yana Kara sadda kanshi kasa.


"To to.. ba komai Magaji sauran bayani Kuma kaji daga wurin iyayenka,tashi kaje Allah yayi maku albarka"

"Mikewa yayi jikin shi duk yayi sanyi Yana amsawa da "Amin".


Har yakai ga bakin kofar part dinshi Yana tinani a ranshi Anya kuwa anyima shi adalci dukda Yana San Farrah , yana da burin ya rayu da ita har karshen rayuwar shi Amma akwai matsala dan shi yasan tunda akayi haka to akwai abinda ake rufemai,Daman tun fil'azal bai yarda da yarinyar ba yanda yaga daga baya duk ta canza dabi'unta ta koyi rashin kunya da fitsara .
A fili ya firta "Kai gaskiya da sake wallahi bazai yiwu ba"yayi kwafa da hannun shi Yana karasa shiga falon nashi da yaji kayan alatu.




 *_Taufa ana wata ga_ _wata me kake nufi_ _Yasir_* ?🤣😂🤣🤣😂

🅿️30
      📝Sai wuraren karfe 11 kafin Farrah ta fara kokarin bude idanun ta a lokacin Momy ce kusa da ita dan Dady tunda ya dawo ya wuce dakin shi.

Momy ruko hannunta tayi tana kokarin tayar da ita zaune.Ba tare da ta furta komai ba sai kallo da take bin ta da shi.
Momy sannu tayi mata tana Kara gyara fulon da ta jingina bayan ta.

Tea ta kawomata tana janyo ledar magunguna da Dady ya aje .

A hankali ta ce "Momy kaini dakina"tana kokarin mikewa tsaye.

Hannunta ta kamo suka sauko down zuwa dakinta hijaf ta cire ta nufi toilet, Momy komawa tayi tana dauko sauran kayan da suka baro,tana dawowa ta tadda harta fito dan haka ta mikamata cup din tare da ballo mata maganin da zata sha.

Bayan ta shanye maganin komawa tayi bisa gadon tayi lamo tare da rungume filo a kirjinta tana sauke ajiyar zuciya.

Ganin haka yasa Momy ta Kara rufamata bargo ba tare data cemata komai ba ta fita tana rufemata dakin.




     *****************
A bangaran Yasir kuwa zan iya cewa har wurin tsakiyar dare babu alamun bacci a idanun shi saboda yadda yayi zurfi a duniyar tunani har baisan dare yayi tsawo haka ba.sai dakyar bacci barawo yayi awan gaba da shi ba tare da ya sani ba zuciyar shi cike fal da tunani iri-iri.


         *WASHE GARI*


A washe gari haka Farrah ta tashi duk jikinta ba kuzari,Kuma tayima kanta alkawarin bazata taba tambayar waye aka aura mata ba Bata da bukatar taji Kuma tayi alkawarin zatayi hakuri tunda so sukeyi suga zuciyarta ta buga gaba daya.itakam ta godewa Allah a haka kaddarar ta ta zo auren ma ba za'a Bata dama ta auri Wanda take so ba,A fili ta furta "Kaico sweety na shikenan rabamu za'ayi bani da yadda zanyi.Da haka dai yinin ranar tayi shi ba Wanda ya Kara yimata maganar auren a wannan ranar.


Jabir ne da yamma a dakinshi system ce a gabanshi.Pics din Farrah naga ya kurawa eyes yana kallo dan ko kyaftawa ba yayi .Ya dade a haka kafin naga ya tashi da murmushi akan fuskarsa.
Ba tare da bata lokaci ba ya fito cikin Shirin shi na kananun kaya yana sauri ya nufi parking lot,A bayan shi ya ji ana kiran shi yana juyawa yaga Abbah ne yake Kiran,Dan haka ya karasa wurin shi Yana gaishe shi.


"Ina zaka je naga kana ta sauri haka"cewar Abbah Yana kallan yadda yake ta fara'a .


"Ehm wallahi zan danje Barhim ne "


"Tau kana jina ko Jabir "?


"Eh Abbah Ina jinka "Yana Kara dan duka wa alamar girmamawa.


"Daga yau ina sanarma ba ruwanka da Amina idan ba zuminci ba kaji na fadama"Yana Kara jinjinawa alamar tabbatar mai da abinda yake fada ba tantama a cikinsa.



"Amma Abbah..........."

"Dakata kaji banasan musu idan Kuma ban isa da kaiba to sai ingani" cewar Abbah Yana juyawa zuwa cikin gidan.

Jabir sororo yayi a wurin kaman an dasa shi.sosai jikin shi yayi sanyi.

A fili ya furta "Hakan Yana nufin akwai matsala kenan,to bazan iya zuwa ba gsky"Dan haka ya juya zuwa part din shi zuciyar shi cike da tunanin wannan al'amarin.


Yasir ne a Mota Yana ta uban gudu ya dauki hanyar zuwa gidan Aunty Husna sosai yake gudu yaji wayar shi ta dauki Kara hannu yasa Yana daukowa tare da duba mai Kiran sunan Ummah ya gani akan screen din Yana yawo,Cikin fargaba ya fara rage gudun da yake yi har saida ya gangara gefen titi kafin ya katse call din sai ya kirata.Yana jin ta daga yayi sallama tare da gaisheta.



"Bana bukatar gaisuwar ka Magaji ka rike abinka, tinda kaida ubanka Baku daukeni a matsayin komai ba,harku yanke hukunci ba tare da kun sanar da ni ba sai dai inji a wani wurin ko, to wallahi saika saketa inhar ni na haifeka,Yarinyar da bata sanka kiri-kiri take nunama kiyayya Amma dayake baka San ciwan kanka ba saida kabi aka kwakubama ita ko? To ko mai kakeyi ka zo wannan weakend din Ina san ganinka"t


"Momy dan Allah kiyi haku.......

Ba tare data jira me yake Shirin fada ba tayi saurin katse wayar tana ruwan bala'i ita kadai.

Abbah da yake tsaye jikin kofa Yana jin duk abinda take fada ba tare da ya kalli inda take ba ya wuce cikin bed room dinshi ya barta tana cigaba da kunfar Baki.

🅿️31
     📝Yasir ajiye wayar yayi ranshi duk Yana jin ba dadi Wai meyasa Umman su tafiye yawan fada?kwata-kwata ba halayyar su daya da Momy ba,Ya kamata ace ta tambaye shi abinda ya faru Amma taki ba shi dama ballantana yayi mata bayani.A fili ya firta "zanje insha Allah,Ya Allah ka fitar da ni daga wannan chakwakiyar"Yana mai Kara gudun motar shi dan ganin magariba  ta fara kasasowa.

Yana karasawa suka gaisa da mai gadin gidan daman sun saba da juna.A falo ya risketa tana yima Hibba kwalliya .

"A lallai kanina sai yau nake ganinka,duk ka biye ni nayi tinanin har yanzu kana katsina?

"Hmmm Aunty ai kinsan bazanki zuwa ba idan ina garin,yau kwana na ukku da dawowa duk abubuwa sun kara cunkusheman na rasa ya zanyi da rayuwa ta"Yana Kara lunshe eyes din shi kamar mai Shirin yin bacci.


"To mai yake faruwa kanina ?yiman bayani dan muji inda matsalar take".


"Aunty Beby"......sai Kuma yayi shiru Yana firzar da iskar bakin shi.

"Ehe Ina jinka Mana"


Shiru yayi harna wasu dakika kafin ya nisa a hankali Yana ci gaba da cewa "Aunty Wai aure aka dauramin da Beby ga Ummah ta kirani tana ta yiman fada,na rasa yadda zanyi duk sun Kara sakani a cikin damuwa".

"Hmmmm Yasir kenan na fahimceka nayi tinanin zakayi farinciki da faruwar hakan,Amma sai naga akasin haka saboda wani shirmen tinaninka Wanda baka da  hujja dan Ina da tabbacin baka da sheda akan hakan,kana abu kaman wani yaro eye?to wallahi ka nutsu kasan me ya kamata kayi duk yadda kake tunanin Farrah ba haka take ba,Kuma Bari kaji wallahi idan ka bari su Abbah suka fahimci abinda kake boyewa tsaf suke warware auren su ba Jabir na fadama dan haka ka farka daga wannan shirmen mafarkin da kake yi"ta Kara she maganar tana Kara hade Fuska daidai ta karasa shirya Hibba.
Itako yarinyar da gudu ta karasa gurin Yasir tana magana cikin gwarancinta .

Daukar ta yayi Yana cillata tana kyalkyala dariya .

Sosai Aunty Husnah take kallan shi duk yabi ya saka kanshi a damuwa a zuciyar ta ta ce"Ai sai nan gaba zaka fara gano kuskuran daka aikatawa Farrah daga ranar data fara gwaraka kamar kwallon mangwaro,duk da kana kanina hakan bazai hana na zigata ta rama abinda kayimata ba".


Sai a sannan ya dire Farrah suna kyalkyala dariya cikin farinci,Dan gaskiya Yana san yarinyar she is beautiful har yana addu'ar Allah yasa Beby ta haifamai kyakkyawa kamar Hibba saboda kamar su daya da Beby (kaji su malan Yasir harda zakewa irin haka🤣🤪).

Gyara zaman shi yayi Yana Kara fuskantar Antyn tashi Dan Hibba ta ruga dakin su.

"Wallahi Aunty na rasa meya kamata nayi ga Beby duk ta rainani yanzu bata ganin girmana ta daina respect dina"

"To ai dole ta rainaka tunda ka riga ka zubar da girmanka tun fil'azal a wurinta Kai a naka tinanin a lokacin ai karama ce kana dauka bata da wayo ko?to ai ga shi Yanzu ta kasa mantawa kaga wa gari ya waya ?Inaga kawai kayi hakuri ka lallabata kuyi zaman lafiya shi ne mafita kawai"


Dan danan ya hade ranshi Yana cewa "Dan Allah Aunty ki daina taso da abinda ya wuce ai ita kanta ya kamata tayiman uziri ta manta da abinda ya wuce"Yana sosa keyar shi.


"Hahaha wallahi kanina kai dan rainin wayo ne wato sai yanzu ka tina da haka ko?wallahi kabi ni a hankali idan ba haka ba na fadawa su Dady abinda kayi har yasa Farrah take tsoranka"ta karashe maganar cikin dariya.


"Double sorry my Aunty 🙉 Yana kama kunnuwan shi alamar ban hakuri"Dan yasan halinta tsaf take korawa Dady bayani ta Kara sanya shi a cikin wata chakwakiyar.


Sosai ya bata dariya ganin yadda lokaci kalilan daga tayar da maganar duk yabi ya rude.

Dariya tayi sosai kafin tace "To naji kanina ya wuce,Kuma insha Allah zanyi magana da Farrah 🤣 karka damu zata yafema,Amma fa saika daina zarginta ka ji ko"?


"Insha Allah zan yi kokarin ganin na cire wannan tinanin daga zuciya ta"


"Da yafiye maka dai,Ai ranar Friday duk zamu je katsinar saboda kasan Asabar ne bikin Diyar kawu Yakubu a Dutsinma "


"Oh my God wallahi na manta "cewar Yasir Yana dafe goshin shi.

'To ai dole ka manta Beby duk ta rudamana kai,Ai wallahi gwamma haka Allah ne yake saka mata ja'iri kawai"tana mikewa tsaye ta nufi kiching .


"Ajiyar zuciya ya sauke Yana Kara mayar da kanshi jikin fulon kujerar tare da lumshe idanunshi Yana murmushi kamar mai tinanin wani abu.


Abin tabawa ta kawo mai Yana ci suna fira dan raban da yaci abinci na kirki tun jiya da dare dan hankalin shi duk a tashe yake.


Sai da akayi Kiran sallar mangariba kafin yayi mata sallama har bakin motar shi ta rako shi suna ta fira irin ta yan'uwa dan ita kadai tasan   wannan sirrin nashi saifa uwar gayya wato Farrah.


Da haka sukayi sallama akan zasu hadu a katsina idan taje wurin biki.cikin gida ta koma shi Kuma ya kama hanyar komawa gidan Aunty Safiyya.

         ****************


Bilkisu ce a tsunne gaban malaminta ko nace boka Dan daka ga kayan daya tanada nayin aiki baiyi kama da malaman kwarai ba.
Daga mata hannu yayi alamar dakatarwa Yana mai janyo wata kwarya daga gefen hannun hagun shi wani abu na hango a cikin kwaryar shi ba jini ba Amma ya canza kala daga ruwa zuwa wani yallow-yallow da wani abu kamar ganye a ciki sosai ya Fara tsafe tsafan shi sai ga fuskar Farrah ta bayyana data Yasir.Wata muguwar dariya ya fara mai rikitarwa hahahahahaha saida ya gaji Dan kanshi ya dakata Yana ce wa "matso kiga wannan ba su ne ki ke magana akan su ba"? cikin muryar shi mai kamar ta katon zaki.


Cikin karkarwa ta Kara matsawa jikin kwaryar tana leka kanta "eh eh sune boka gagara"tana duban kawarta khairiyya dake kusa da ita.


"To a yanzu haka maganar da nake maki jiya aka daura auren su,Kuma duk Wanda ya ce zai raba wannan auren to zai iya rasa ranshi,Amma akwai mafita guda daya mahaifiyar shi Bata San wannan auren dan haka zamuyi amfani da wannan damar Dan ganin mun sanya rikici a tsakaini su sannan zamu dawo da soyayyar da yake yimata kanki,Amma kuma akwai sharadi duk ranar da ya kusanceta to daga wannan ranar asirinki zai tonu Kuma zaki fuskanci matsala kin amince ?


Tana kokarin yin magana kawarta tayi saurin cewa "Ta amince boka,mudai burinmu ya sota kuma mu samu manyan kudade daga gare shi"cewar kawar ta ta.


Daga haka ya Basu magani da yadda zasu yi amfani da shi a cikin sati daya hankalin shi zai dawo gareta.

Makudan kudade suka ajemai takalmansu a hannu su fito daga cikin kunkurimin dajin har zuwa inda suka aje direban daya kawo su kafin suka nufi hanyar da zata sadasu da garin na katsina.




 ```To fns Wai me yake``` ```Shirin faruwa da``` ```rayuwar Farrah``` ?


 ```Ko wane laifi Yasir ya``` ```yiwa Farrah``` ```Wanda yasa``` ```har take tsoran``` ```shi``` ?


 ```Shin ko Bilkisu zata``` yi ```nasarar shiga``` ```tsakanin Farrah``` ```da Yasir``` ?


 *Duk wannan amsoshin* *suna ga AUTAHR ku Dan* *haka kudai ku* *biyoni Dan jin yadda* *wannan lamari zai kasan* *ce* .
