🅿️36
       📝Zuwa da dare wajen karfe goma hannun Farrah ya kunbura suntum abinka da farar fata,ga taushin hannu gareta hakan yasa dandanan wurin yayi yayi ja sosai ga wani mugun zugi Yana yimata.ta kife kanta a Kan abin sallah tana sharbar kuka dan tunda ta idar da sallar isha'i bata tashi daga wurin ba.tana a haka Momy ta turo kofar ta shigo,sosai ta bata tausayi ganin yadda hannun yayi ga idanuwan ta duk sun kunbura saboda kuka.

"Bari na sanar da Dadyn ku,ya Kira doctor Umar ya zo dan ya duba maki hannun"cewar Momy tana ficewa daga dakin.
Ita dai kukanta ta ci gaba da sharba ba kakkautawa.


Momy a tare suka dawo da Dady da Aunty Husnah.kamata tayi tana kokarin dagata ita kuwa ta langabe a jikin Aunty Husnah tana Kara rushewa da wani saban kukan.

Dady Fada yayi ta yi,Yana kokarin Kiran nomber doctor.yana jin ya daga Bayan sun gaisa ya ce "Dan Allah doctor idan kana gari ina so ka zo zaka duba Farrah,rauni ne ta ji a hannunta".


"Ok ranka shi dade gani nan zuwa insha Allah"Yana katse kiran.

Fita yayi daga dakin ya nufi hanyar part din Yasir ranshi a bace.

Ga dukkan alamu Yasir zai sha fada 🤣🤣🤣




      ------------------------

"Wallahi Abbah na hakura da ita,Daman Allah ya riga ya kaddara ba rabona ce ba,Amma dan Allah ku barni mu rinka zuminci ni da ita,ku kanku ba zaku so zumincin mu ya samu rauni ba ta sanadiyyar faruwar wannan al'amarin,domin ni nasan wani baya auren matar wani"cewar Jabir da yake kasan kafet a zaune kusa da Mahaifin su.
Duk da Yana jin zafin rabuwa da Farrah,Amma dole ya danne zuciyar shi,ko domin kada zumincin su ya lalace.


Abbah nisawa yayi ya kalli dan nashi cike da jin dadin kalaman shi ya ce "Tabbas Ina alfahari da ku a matsayin yarana,Na ji dadin Yadda kayi tawakkali da hukuncin Ubangiji,Dan Ina tabbatar maka ko mu kanmu umarni ne muka cika,domin Allah ya riga ya rubuta shi ne mijinta,A lokacin shaf muka manta da shakuwar dake tsakanin ku,Amma ba komai kaima Allah ya baka mace ta gari wadda zata yima biyayya kamar yadda kayi Mana.

Sosai ya ji dadin addu'ar mahaifin na shi,da murmushi akan fuskarsa shi ya ce "Godiya nake Abbah Allah ya Kara girma da nisan kwana".

"Amen ya Allah Jabir,saboda yadda naga ka kontar da hankalinka nayima alkawarin insha Allah next week zan canza maka motor"


"Wooo alhamdlillah 🤲 ala kullu halin,Amma gaskiya na ji dadi Abban mu nagode Allah ya saka da alkhairi".


"Amen ya Allah"cewar Abbah Yana kokarin kunna tv.



"Ai dole kake canza mai motor,saboda an san shi ne mai hakuri"

Dukan su a tare suka Juya dan jin muryar Ummah a bakin kofar shigowa falon.

Ci gaba tayi da cewa "ita figaggiyar yarinyar da kuka dauki son duniya kuka Dora mata Ai ita bata San naku yaran,domin kuwa da tana San su,to da ta so Wanda kuka aura mata.yarinya karama sai wani lallabata ake yi,to wallahi inhar ni na haifi yarannan to sai Magaji ya saki wannan yarinyar tinda har ta iya nuna bata kaunar yarana to Nima bana kaunar ta daga ita har wannan munafikar uwar ta ta"sosai tahau ruwan masifa ba kakkautawa.
Shi dai Abbah ko kallonta baiyi ba,a karshe ya ce "Jabir jeka na sallame ka Allah ya albarka ce ku da sauran yaran musulmi"

Sai a sannan Jabir ya motsa dan duk fadan da Umman su take yi Kan shi kawai ya sadda Yana sauraren ta.Mikewa yayi Yana yiwa Abban su godiya ya fice daga part din Yana jin zuciyar shi ba dadi Dan kwata-kwata Jabir baya San hayaniya Dan shi ma'abocin raha ne Amma baya da fada ko kadan.
Da wannan tinanin ya shige part din su Yana Kara kokarin danne zuciyar shi.


Ummah kuwa saida ta gaji dan kanta kafin ta yi shiru da bakinta,Dan ganin Wanda take yin fadan domin ya tanka ko kallo bata isheshi ba Dan haka taja kafafunta fuuuuuuu ta nufi dakin ta tana huci kamar zakanya 🤣🙊.



    


       ************
A gidan su Farrah kuwa baiwar Allah kafin karasowar doctor ji tayi kamar hannunta zai fita saboda azabar zafi,har wata zufa take ketowa daga goshinta kamar wadda maciji ya ciza.


A tattare suka shigo da Dady sai Doctor na biye a bayan shi,sai Kuma Yasir da aka tattago shi Yana faman cira ido. Yana karasa shigowa ya hangota rungume jikin Aunty Husnah tana faman jijjuya kanta dan kukan yanzu ba hawaye saboda har sun kafe sai dai kukan zuci.
Wurin ta ya karasa da sauri Yana kokarin kamata tare da cewa "Subahallah Beby meya same ki haka?Daman baki da lafiya ne?ko wani ya taba min ke"?duk a lokaci daya ya jero mata wannan gustion din.

Ita kuwa Farrah abin tausayi tana jin muryar shi ta Kara kankame Aunty Husnah idonta ta Kara rufewa gam Dan yanzu ita ta tsani ganin shi,Dan gani take yi kamar cizon ta zai Kara yi.

Aunty Husnah cikin jin haushi ta ce "malam tashi ka bamu waje idan mun gama ka zo kayi jinyar ta"tana Kara  kauda kanta site guda.
Jiki ba kwari ya koma gefe guda ya tsaya, doctor ya fara duba hannun ganin yadda ya kunbura,Kuma ga dukkan alamu idan aka Bari ya kwana to zai yi ruwa dan haka  ya dauko box din kayan aiki wata fata da yaga ta Dan tashi ga dukkan alamu rufe wurin zata yi kawai ya yanketa tare da rubuta magunguna da allurai ya ce a siyo su yanzu yayi mata,kafin zuwa da safe kunbirin zai saki.
Dady ya amsa takardar Yana mikawa Yasir da duk kunyar da fargaba sun rufe shi,Dan ya fahimci yadda Dady ya dau abin da zafi to zai iya yanke ko wane hukunci.


Cikin sanyin jiki ya saka hannuwan shi biyu ya amsa ba tare da ya ce komai ba ya fice Dan baya bukatar Karin bayani.part din shi ya koma walet din kudin shi ya dauko da wayoyin shi ya zuba a cikin aljihu sai key daya riko.cikin sauri ya shiga Motar shi da gudu ya nufi gate Yana faman Danna horn tun kafin ya karaso gurin Baba Auwalu ya bude yana mamakin wannan sauri na uban gidan na shi,domin kuwa da ga gani ba lafiya a cikin gidan.su Dady da Doctor Falo suka dawo Dan jiran dawowar shi.inda ita kuwa Farrah kamar mai nakuda haka ta koma abin tausayi.


Suna cikin wannan halin ya shigo da ledoji a hannun shi,karba yayi doctor ya fara hada Allurar Yana cewa "Gaskiya Alhaji na kasa gano ko meye ya cijeta,domin kuwa wannan ba cizon zuma ne ba Dan wurin ya fashe ne shiyasa".


"Ai doctor ba zaka iya gano ko meye ba,Dan wannan cizon mutum ne ya kunbira mata hannu haka"


"Mutum dai"?

"Kwarai kuwa,ga garjejen katon daya cijeta nan a gaban ka"Yana nuna Yasir da yake jin kamar ya tashi ya ruga saboda kunyar da yaji ta rufe shi,Dan shi harga Allah kawai ji yayi Yana marmarin ya cijeta shiyasa,Amma baiyi tinanin lamarin zai kawo har haka ba.


Doctor murmushi kawai yayi Yana girgiza kai,ya nufi dakin Farrah suna binshi a baya.


Tana jin Aunty Husnah ta ce ta tashi ayi mata allaura,Ai ko ta Kara rudewa tana rike ta,Dan a rayuwar ta ta tsani allura gwamma ta sha magani da ayi mata allura.

Doctor yayi ta faman ta tsaya abin ya gagara Dan Aunty  bata iya riketa fisgewa take yi.

Cikin kosawa Dady ya ce "ke saketa ku fito falo dukan ku,Magaji ya riketa tinda nema take yi ta Bata Mana lokaci"Yana ficewa daga dakin suma duk fita suka yi aka barsu da Doctor sai Magaji da yayi shiru kanshi a kasa baya da alamar motsawa .ganin haka da doctor yayi yasa ya matsa kusa da ita Dan har sha daya takusa yi.zai riketa kenan Yasir yayi wata Zabura yana cewa ke "tashi na ce maki"
Zaune ta tashi tana girgiza kanta alamar bata so.


A hankali ya durkusa a gabanta Yana kokarin saka idanuwan shi cikin nata,Amma taki bashi damar yin haka ta hanyar Kara rufe idanuwan ta.hannuwanta ya hade guri daya ya cukukuyeta ya saka ta a faffadan kirjin shi,kusan a tare suka sauke ajiyar zuciya.
A saitin kunnenta ya rada mata "wallahi dakin kuskura kika yi  wani motsi anan zan Kara maki wani sabon cizon tunda kin rainani".


Baiwar Allah lamo tayi a jikin shi tana kokarin mayar da kukan da yake kokarin fitowa.A haka ya jaye Hijaf din jikinta gefe daya ya Dan daga rigar jikinta ta gefen da za'ayi allurar Yana cewa "Doctor yimata a haka"

Doctor da yayi shagara Yana kallan ikon Allah,sai a sannan ya samu damar matsawa ya fara cira mata siringin a hankali.

Tun tana daurewa harta kasa saboda zafin da taji Yana shigar ta, Ai tana jin ya gama saka siringin duka ta zabura zata mike da sauri Yasir ya Kara riketa yadda ba zata iya kwacewa ba.Ba yadda zata yi Dan haka kawai itama ta rike shi gam tana zubar da kolla har ya gama ya zare siringin tana a haka.

Sun Jima a haka har doctor ya fita bata sake shi ba,Dan ji tayi wani bacci Yana kokarin daukar ta.saida yaji motsi alamar tafiya kamar za'a shigo dakin kafin ya dauketa ya mayar da ita bisa gado Yana kokarin cire mata Hijaf.
Da sauri ta rike cikin sanyin muryarta ta ce "ka kyale ni hakanan bana so,Kuma ka sani saika sake ni na auri Wanda nake so tunda Kai ka tsane ni"tana mai juya mai baya.

Murmushi yayi Dukda ya ji ciwon maganar ta,Amma yayi mata uziri Dan auren su mutu ka raba ne Babu zancen saki.
Har ya bude Baki zai yi magana kawai sai ya juya ya fita. tana jin fitar shi ta Kara gyara konciyar ta tana addu'ar Allah ya saka mata.

A falon ya riske su doctor Yana nuna masu yadda za'a Bata maganin idan ta farka.wucewa yayi ya nufi part din shi Yana jin zuciyar shi na yima shi suya.


A wannan ranar duk  a haka suka konta ransu ba dadi,Dan shi Yasir haushin Farrah yake ji wato danta gama raina shi,shi take cewa ya saketa,hmmmm Beby kenan ai ki fara Shirin zama da ni,domin kuwa ni ne kaddarar ki.


Ita kuwa Bayan fitar shi bacci mai nauyi ne ya dauketa sakamakon allurar da aka yi mata,ga kuka da ta sha.

            *WASHE GARI*
Tun gab da asuba ta farka jin jinkinta alamar zazzabi ga kanta Yana faman Sara mata,wanka ta shiga tare da dauro Arwalla ta kabbara sallah tana Kai kukanta wurin zaljalali akan ya zaba mata abin da yafi alkhairi a rayuwar ta.saida ta ji an tayar da sallah kafin ta yi tata,bayan ta idar dakyar ta rarrafa zuwa Kan gadonta ta konta tana jin zafin zazzabin Yana karuwa.


Sai karfe Tara Momy ta shiga dakinta bayan ta kammala break,Dan ta tayar da ita.Gani tayi tana bacci,a hankali ta fara tayar da ita.
Dakyar ta bude idanunta saboda nauyin da kanta yayi."Momy kaina ciwo yake yi"tana dafe barin goshinta da hannu.

Jin jikinta tayi yayi zafi,"Subahanallahi Farrah zazzabin ne?tashi kiyi break sai kisha magunguna"kamata tayi ta kaita toilet ita Kuma ta nufi kiching.
Bayan ta fito bakin gadon ta zauna ta buga uban tagumi tana tinanin meke Shirin faruwa da rayuwar ta ne?ta kasa gane inda matsalar take.
Momy ce ta turo kofar dakin da tire a hunnunta ajewa tayi Kan kafet tana yimata sannu,tea ta hada mata mai kauri ta Mika mata bayan ta dan Sha ta ballo magungunan kamar yadda ta gani a rubuce ta Mika mata.tana Sha ta fara kokarin yin amai,Amma Allah ya taimake ta Bata yiba,Dan haka Momy ta Kara gyara mata filo ta konta.bargo ta rufeta da shi tana tattare kayan ta fita da su.ita ko rufe idanunta tayi tana jin haushin Ya Yasir,Dan tasan yau Aunty Husnah zata wuce Abuja shikenan ya ja mata Dan tasan Dady ba zai Bari a tafi da ita ba.



 

        ~~~~~~~~~~
"Gaskiya boka gagara ni bazan yarda ba ace na baka kudi amma aiki yaki ci,Dan wannnan shi ne burin da take raina naga ya rabu da ita hankalin shi ya dawo gare ni".


Cikin amon muryar shi marar dadin saurare ya ce "A wannan karon zamuyi nasara a kansu,Amma hanyar da zamu bi ita ce :yanzu haka tana konce Bata da lafiya zamuyi amfani da wannan damar ta hanyar tura ALJANI ya Kara rikita rashin lafiyar daganan sai a sanya tsanar shi a zuciyar ta ta birki ce mai har sai ya saketa,Dan shi Yana da yawan yin addu'o'i shiyasa yake neman gagarar mu,Amma ki kontar da hankalinki".


"Yauwa boka yanzu muka ji magana"cewar khairiyya da take gefenta.

Kudi suka Kara masu yawa suka ajemai suka bar wurin cikin farinciki.




         **************
Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraju'un shi ne kalmar da ta fito daga bakin shi lokacin da ya farka daga baccin da ya dauke shi tun bayan ya dawo daga sallar Asuba.
Sosai ya ji gaban shi ya fadi tinawa da yayi da mafarkin da yayi na ganin Farrah a cikin wani mawuyacin hali,Yana ta kokarin taimakon ta Amma sai yaga wata yarinya ta shiga tsakanin su.
Saukowa yayi ya shiga Wonka Yana gamawa ya dora arwallah tare da shiyawa,duk cikin sauri yake Shirin Dan burin shi ya je yaga ya lafiyar Beby take.

Part din ya shiga da sallama dauke a bakin shi.Su Momy suka amsa bayan ya zauna Yana gaishe su yake tambayar jikin Farrah,amsa mai tayi da sauki Dan ita Aunty Husnah sama-sama ta amsa saboda ta ji haushi ga shi nan ya jawowa yarinya cuta ba gaira daga cizon soyayyay ita ya zamar mata na wahala.
Ganin yadda suka yi biris da shi yasa ya nufi dakin ba tare da ya Kara ce masu komai ba...A rufe ya riketa Dan haka ya zauna kusa da ita Yana Dora hannun shi saman goshinta jin ba zafi sosai sai zufar da take yi.karfin fanka ya Kara mata,Yana janye bargon da aka rufeta.sosai ya ji tausayinta ya zarga zuciyar shi da bargon jikin shi.Dan karamin bakinta yake kallo ganin yadda take motsashi gwanin sha'awa.jiyayi gaban shi ya Kara faduwa Dan haka ya fara karanto addu'o'i Yana tofa mata cikin kwarewa da sanin haruffan larabci. A kalla ya dau tsawon 20 minutes kafin ya fita daga dakin bai tsaya a falon ba kawai ya wuce part din shi.



Wata muguwar dariya ya fara saida gaba dayan dajin ya amsa saboda karfin muryar shi,tsagaitawa yayi Yana wasu irin surutai masu kama da yare "Ai ko ya tabowa kansa tunda har ya konaka,sai na kaskantar da shi,idan ko har hakan Bata samu ba to sai dai ka koma jikin Bilkisu Dan tun farko na fada mata"Yana kaiwa nan ya kara kwashewa da wata mahaukaciyar dariya kafin naga a karo na biyu.



Kasancewar ranar lahadi ne ba aiki Dan haka yasa Jabir shiyawa cikin wata shadda kalar sky blue dinkin zamani,yayi kyau sosai bai saka hula ba sai kanshi da ya gyara.karfe hudu na yamma ya nufi Barhim dan ya gaisa da Farrah Dan ya Kira Momy take cemai ai Bata jin dadi.

   🅿️37
      📝         *WASHE GARI*
Tun gab da asuba ta farka jin jinkinta alamar zazzabi ga kanta Yana faman Sara mata,wanka ta shiga tare da dauro Arwalla ta kabbara sallah tana Kai kukanta wurin zaljalali akan ya zaba mata abin da yafi alkhairi a rayuwar ta.saida ta ji an tayar da sallah kafin ta yi tata,bayan ta idar dakyar ta rarrafa zuwa Kan gadonta ta konta tana jin zafin zazzabin Yana karuwa.


Sai karfe Tara Momy ta shiga dakinta bayan ta kammala break,Dan ta tayar da ita.Gani tayi tana bacci,a hankali ta fara tayar da ita.
Dakyar ta bude idanunta saboda nauyin da kanta yayi."Momy kaina ciwo yake yi"tana dafe barin goshinta da hannu.

Jin jikinta tayi yayi zafi,"Subahanallahi Farrah zazzabin ne?tashi kiyi break sai kisha magunguna"kamata tayi ta kaita toilet ita Kuma ta nufi kiching.
Bayan ta fito bakin gadon ta zauna ta buga uban tagumi tana tinanin meke Shirin faruwa da rayuwar ta ne?ta kasa gane inda matsalar take.
Momy ce ta turo kofar dakin da tire a hunnunta ajewa tayi Kan kafet tana yimata sannu,tea ta hada mata mai kauri ta Mika mata bayan ta dan Sha ta ballo magungunan kamar yadda ta gani a rubuce ta Mika mata.tana Sha ta fara kokarin yin amai,Amma Allah ya taimake ta Bata yiba,Dan haka Momy ta Kara gyara mata filo ta konta.bargo ta rufeta da shi tana tattare kayan ta fita da su.ita ko rufe idanunta tayi tana jin haushin Ya Yasir,Dan tasan yau Aunty Husnah zata wuce Abuja shikenan ya ja mata Dan tasan Dady ba zai Bari a tafi da ita ba.



 

        ~~~~~~~~~~
"Gaskiya boka gagara ni bazan yarda ba ace na baka kudi amma aiki yaki ci,Dan wannnan shi ne burin da take raina naga ya rabu da ita hankalin shi ya dawo gare ni".


Cikin amon muryar shi marar dadin saurare ya ce "A wannan karon zamuyi nasara a kansu,Amma hanyar da zamu bi ita ce :yanzu haka tana konce Bata da lafiya zamuyi amfani da wannan damar ta hanyar tura ALJANI ya Kara rikita rashin lafiyar daganan sai a sanya tsanar shi a zuciyar ta ta birki ce mai har sai ya saketa,Dan shi Yana da yawan yin addu'o'i shiyasa yake neman gagarar mu,Amma ki kontar da hankalinki".


"Yauwa boka yanzu muka ji magana"cewar khairiyya da take gefenta.

Kudi suka Kara masu yawa suka ajemai suka bar wurin cikin farinciki.




         **************
Innalillahi wa'inna ilaihirraju'un shi ne kalmar da ta fito daga bakin shi lokacin da ya farka daga baccin da ya dauke shi tun bayan ya dawo daga sallar Asuba.
Sosai ya ji gaban shi ya fadi tinawa da yayi da mafarkin da yayi na ganin Farrah a cikin wani mawuyacin hali,Yana ta kokarin taimakon ta Amma sai yaga wata yarinya ta shiga tsakanin su.
Saukowa yayi ya shiga Wonka Yana gamawa ya dora arwallah tare da shiyawa,duk cikin sauri yake Shirin Dan burin shi ya je yaga ya lafiyar Beby take.

Part din ya shiga da sallama dauke a bakin shi.Su Momy suka amsa bayan ya zauna Yana gaishe su yake tambayar jikin Farrah,amsa mai tayi da sauki Dan ita Aunty Husnah sama-sama ta amsa saboda ta ji haushi ga shi nan ya jawowa yarinya cuta ba gaira daga cizon soyayyay ita ya zamar mata na wahala.
Ganin yadda suka yi biris da shi yasa ya nufi dakin ba tare da ya Kara ce masu komai ba...A rufe ya riketa Dan haka ya zauna kusa da ita Yana Dora hannun shi saman goshinta jin ba zafi sosai sai zufar da take yi.karfin fanka ya Kara mata,Yana janye bargon da aka rufeta.sosai ya ji tausayinta ya zarga zuciyar shi da bargon jikin shi.Dan karamin bakinta yake kallo ganin yadda take motsashi gwanin sha'awa.jiyayi gaban shi ya Kara faduwa Dan haka ya fara karanto addu'o'i Yana tofa mata cikin kwarewa da sanin haruffan larabci. 🅿️38
     📝Yana fita daga gidan su ya dauki hanyar Barhim baka jin komai a cikin motar sai  kira'ar karatun kur'ani mai girma da yake sauraro,sai wani mayan kanshin turare da ya cika motar,sosai yake jin nutsuwa tana Kara sauke mai,Dan haka ba abinda yake Kara nanatawa a zuciyar shi sai tasbihi tare da godiya ga Allah da ya hallicce shi a cikin Al'ummar musulmi,ga ni'imomi da ya nufeshi da su masu tarin yawa,cikin ikon Allah da haka ya karasa duk da shi ba ma'abocin gudu ne ba kamar yasir🤣


Bayan ya wuce babban gate yana karasawa ciki Suka gaisa da Baba Auwalu, inda yayi parking fitowa yayi Yana taba Dan karamin sajen shi da ga Sha gyara.


Yasir ya hango Yana kokarin shiga part din shi,ganin ya Dan yi mai nisa Koda ya bishi kafin ya tadda shi ya riga ya shige,Dan haka kawai yadan daga murya Yana "Ya Yasir! Ya Yasir!"gani yayi bai waigo ba sai sai gani yayi ya shige ciki.

Shima Jabir kyale shi yayi ya nufi cikin gidan,A zuciyar shi Yana tinanin may be bai ji Kiran ba.


Ba kowa a Falon hakan yasa ya zauna Yana faman Danna wayar shi da alama chat yake yi ji nayi Yana cewa "Ai wallahi salisu ka Bari,shiyasa na gudu fa Dan nasan da kawu Yakubu ya farga da irin girman jikin da nayi to Yana iya dauramin aure a ranar shiyasa na biyo su Dady na fece gida,ni Kuma gaskiya tinda na rasa Farrah to Aure ba yanzu ba Dan Ina jin kewar ta har yanzu a kasan zuciya ta"ya karasa voice din muryar shi cikin rauni da alamar damuwa.


Daidai lokacin Farrah ta farka daga baccin wahalar ciwo da ya dauketa.jin kamar maganar mutum a Falo yasa ta leko Dan ji tayi muryar kamar ta namiji.


Kan kujera ta ganshi,bayan shi kadai take hangowa saboda shi Yana passing din tv ne ita ko kofarta na barin dama ta bayan kukerar,haka yasa shi bai ganta ba.


Kuri tayi da idonta tana hango sumar kanshi mai kyau da sheki.a zuciyarta tana jin yanzu da shi ne aka aura mata da suna nan suna kwasar soyayya,Amma an tashi an lika mata wancan mai irin ta Yan india.da sauri ta furta Astagfirullah tana mai komawa cikin dakin domin ta rama sallar da aka biyota.


Duk abinda take yi Jabir Yana hangota ta jikin tv saboda hasken gurin,Kuma a kashe take shiyasa,Ajiyar zuciya ya sauke tare da kokarin yakitar da tinanin da zuciyar shi take son bijiro mai da shi.


Ba tare data dau tsawon lokaci ba ta kammala har Wonka tayi kafin ta saka doguwar rigar atamfa ta dora hijaf akai,turare kawai ta fesa Dan ko mai Bata tsaya murzawa ba ta nufo falon.


Yana jin alamar takunta ta nufo inda yake,ya dago kanshi Yana dubanta gani yayi fuskarta tayi fayau kamar an jata,sai idanunta da suka fada alamar ta ji jiki.

Saida ya ji ta zauna a kurerar dake gefen shi kafin ya tina da magana yake yi a waya, wata yar office din su ce ta kirashi akan maganar wasu takaddu da za'a kawo mai a gobe litinin.

Katse kiran yayi Yana cewa "sorry I will call you letter "yana aje wayar jikin hannun kujerar.


Kallanta yayi sosai Yana Kara jin wani abu Yana yima shi yawo a kasan zuciyar shi.

"Sannu ya jikin naki"shi ne abinda ya iya furtawa Yana kallan fuskarta ganin yadda duk ta rame.


"Yauwa Ina yini "tana kallan shi ta kasan idonta.

"Lafiya Lau,ya jikin naki?"

"Alhmdl yayi sauki,kunbirin ya saki"tana juya hannun.

"Ok a hannu ne kenan,meye ta cije ki haka naga wurin yayi ja"?


Jim ta dan yi kafin ta ce "bigewa nayi ne jikin kofar toilet"a ranta tana ai bazan iya sanar ma cewar wancan katon ne ya cije ni ba,haba ai da kunya ma.


"Ok Allah ya kiyaye gaba"

"Amen ya Allah"tana sadda kanta kasa,Dan har yanzu kunyar shi take ji,tunda tasan zuwa yanzu yasan da maganar kaddararen auren da aka lika mata.


Shiru suka yi dukan su,kowa da abinda yake sakawa a cikin zuciyar shi.suna a wannan Yana yin Yasir ya turo kofar falon.
Gaba dayan su suka juya,tana ganin shi tsam ta mike ta wuce  dakinta.shiko Jabir cewa yayi "A Yaya ka fito?lokacin da na shigo Ina ta magana magana baka jini ba ka shiga part dinka".


"Ok" ya ce Yana zama bisa kujera.

"Jiya Momy take fadaman Auntyn tamu Bata da lafiya,shiyasa na zo dubata,Amma nasa da sauki"


Yasir shiru yayi Dan wani haushin kanen nashi ya ji Yana Kara kama shi,Dan rainin hankali ya zo ya tsare  matar shi  Yana kalle mai ita,yanzu Kuma Dan yagan shi ya bige da wani dubata ya zo yi.

"Ok ai daka Bari shikenan ,Dan taji sauki"Yana danna remote control a hannun shi.


Jabir tsayawa yayi Yana kallan shi a zuciyar shi mamaki yake yi,A maimakon ace shi ne yake nuna kishi akan fin karfin da aka yimai,Amma sai ga shi Wanda yayi nasara yake nuna haka.

Shima forget din shi yayi Yana ci gaba da chat din shi a zuciyar shi Yana Kara jin jina san Kai irin na mutanan wannan *ZAMANINI*,duk da ba abin mamaki ne ba Dan abinda yafi haka ma to zai iya gani.


Har tsawon wani Dan lokaci ba Wanda ya tankawa dan'uwan shi.

Momy tunda ta fara tako matakalar benen ta hango su kowa Yana faman Shan kamshi,a ranta ta ce akwai matsala.

Da sauri Jabir ya mike ganin ta Kara so inda suke Yana cewa "Momy barka da fitowa,da fatan na same ku lafiya?"


"Lafiya Lau Jabiru,ya su Ummah taku?"


Dan darawa yayi Dan yasan inhar ta Kira shi da wannan sunan to sai tayi ta Jan shi.
"Duk lafiya Lau muke"


"To Masha Allah,kaga Farrah ko"?

"Eh na ganta harna dubata ma,naga banga fitowar ki ba da tun dazu na wuce"


"Eh wallahi tunda Husnah ta tafi na koma ciki Dan itama Nusaiba tana islamiyya,Amma yanzu nasan sun kusa tasowa"


"Wayyo Aunty Husnah harta tafi kenan,shiyasa tun dazu na so na fito Dan musha hira da ita,Ashe dai na makara"


"Wallahi harta tafi Dan yanzu nasan sun kusa zuwa"cewar Momy tana mikewa tsaye 
"Bari a kawoma ko lemu ne ka sha"


"A a Momy na koshi,wucewa zanyi kar magriba  tayi min a hanya"Yana tashi daga kujera.


"To Allah ya kiyaye hanya,mun gode kaji ka gaisheman da Autah shi dai ya dade rabon shi da nan gidan"


"To Momy shi insha Allah,ai zanyi kokari na kawo shi Koda da dare ne saboda ba lokaci kusan any time yana school shiyasa"


"To nagode"cewar Momy tana kallan inda Yasir yake ganin bai tanka ba yasa Jabir na fita itama ta koma part din Dady ta barshi anan.



Yana ganin Fitar Jabir daga gidan, shima ya wuce masallaci Dan an kusa fara Kiran sallar mangariba.




     ***************
 _Wai Ina Farrah_? 🤣

Tinda ta shige dakinta ta bayan ta sha magungunan ta bata Kara fitowa ba,domin ita yanzu banda haushin Zalimu da take ji to shima Jabir ta fara jin haushin shi,Dan meye ba zaiyi wani abu aka ba,karshe ma ki zancan yaki yimata,ai dai yasan ita kadai ba zata iya banbare wannan auren ba sai da taimakon shi,Amma ba komai tunda ta ji da sauki anjima zata anshi wayar Momy ta Kira shi dan ya samo masu mafita tun kafin abin yayi nisa.

Da wannan tinanin ta kunna tv din dakinta ta kamo sunna tv tana kallo abinta har wuraren kafe takwas na dare.



Jabir har yaje gida Yana al'ajabin abinda brother ya nuna mai,Amma ya kudirta a zuciyar shi ba zai kara zuwa ba har sai Farrah tabar gidan,Koda kuwa an tambaye shi dalili zai ce kawai ba komai.

       ______________
Dan Allah Bilkisu kada ki cutar da wannan yarinyar ,yanzu fisabilillahi wane lefi ta aikata maki da zakiyi kokarin rabata da mijinta kwai saboda wani buri naki na hauka?"


"Dakata" malama,tana daga hannunta alamar tsawatar wa,bance maki Ina bukatar wa'azinki ba Dan haka ki adana kayanki may be wata rana zai maki amfani,ke kinsan yadda na Dade Ina shirya plan akan zuwan wannan lokacin to wallahi kar ki sake ki shiga wannan lamarin idan ko ba haka ba to ki sani du zanyi maganin ku daga ke har ita na fada maki"tana nunata da yatsa.

"Gaskiya Bilkisu ban taba sanin Ke dakikiya ce ba sai yau,a tinanina ko wani kikaga zai cutar da Farrah sai inda karfin ki ya Kare,Ashe ita Farrah bata sani ba tana tare da makiyiyar ta.lallai Bahaushe yayi gaskiya da ya ce "Ashe makashinka yana nan tare da Kai"idan ko hakane to ki sani wallahi saina tona asirin ki,muguwa mai mayar da alkhairi ya zama sharri,Kuma ki jira kiga karshen ki insha Allah sai kinyi dana sani tunda kin biyewa sharrin *ZAMANI* kina sharholiyar ki,idan anyi magana sai ku ce ai *ZAMANIN* ne ya zo da haka,ni nasan duk da *ZAMANINMU* ne ya zo da haka to wallahi harda San zuciyar ku,ke da su khairiyya idan har baku tuba kun koma ga Allah ba to zakuga yadda Allah zaiyi da ku,munafikan banza". Yarinyar tana gama fada mata ta juya   cikin takun ta nabirgewa bigewa tabar Bilkisu tana bin bayanta da kallo.ko Ni dai Banga fuskarta ba 🤣😜.

Sosai Bilkisu take cizon yatsa a fili ta furta "Duk zan yi maganin ku ta hanyar nuna maku true colors dina,munafikai ku kuna tutiyar iyayen ku suna da kudi ko?to ku saurari abinda zai biyo baya"tana hararar gurin tamkar har yanzu yarinyar ce a tsaye.kafin itama ta juya zuwa gurin inda tabar saurayin nata a cikin motar Yana jiran ta.

🅿️39
      📝Wuraren karfe tara na daren wannan lokacin farrah ra nufi dakin Momy,da sallama ta shiga.Momy ganin ita ce yasa ta mike daga kishingiden da tayi tana cewa "Kara so Mana"
Shigowa tayi tana zama kasan kafet din tsakar dakin.
"Momy dan Allah wayar ki nake so ki aro min zan Kira Aunty"

Momy kallanta tayi tana nazarin abinda taji ta fada,sannan ta nuna mata inda wayar take saman site drower.tana dauka ta fito waje,can ta nufa bayan gidan wurin wasu shuke-shuke na filawowi masu kyau wurin sai kanshi ruwa ke tashi,kamar lokacin damina.wata Yar bishiya ta samu ta jingina da gurin tana neman Nomber Jabir.

Tayi ta faman bincike contact din bata ga nomber ba,harta fitar da rai tana sakawa a ranta bari ta Kira Aunty Husnah dan taji sun je gida lafiya kwatsam  sai taga wata nomber an saka Son,da sauri ta danna call saboda tafi tunanin ita ce.tana fara ringing aka yi picking,tana yin sallama bata jira ya amsaba cikin raunin murya ta fara cewa "Yaya Jabir Kuma dazu shi ne kayi tafiyarka baka jira munyi magana ba,to nidai wallahi na gaji tunda ba zaka tambaye ni ya zamuyi ba,shi yasa na Kira ka don mu samu mafita,kadan Kai nake so,Kai ne muradin zuciyata,Dan Allah kayi wani abu akai a farke auren nan".
Shiru ta ji bai tanka ta ba.ta ce "Yaya Jabir ko baka jina ne?kayi shiru ehe?"tana kokarin tsugunnawa a gurin saboda yadda ta ji kafafunta sun Kara yin sanyi.a wannan karon ma bai ko tanka ba.kawai saita fashe da kuka,kuka take yi ba kakkautawa.
Shiko Yana saurarenta dukda Yana jin sautin kukan har cikin kokon ranshi,Amma bazai iya yin magana ba.
Ziro kafafuwan shi yayi kasa daga konciyar da yake ya nufi kofar fita daga part din nashi.yana shiga cikin gidan ya konkosa dakin Momy Yana raba wayar da kunnen shi,gani yayi bata katse kiran ba Kuma har zuwa lokacin kukan take yi.kashe wayar yayi bayan Momy ta ba shi izinin shiga.sallama yayi Yana cewa "Momy naga Kiran ki ne a wayana"


"Ni kuma"?a a ba ni ce ba sai dai ko Farrah Dan ta anshi wayana tun dazu zata Kira Mamanta".

"Ok" kawai ya ce Yana fita daga dakin,Dan yasan inda zai risketa derect can ya nufa.


Saboda yanayin gurin ba wadataccen haske shiyasa baya hangota sosai har saida ya karasa gaf da ita,kafin ya ganta a tsugunne ta saka kanta tsakanin cinyoyin ta a hankali ya tsuguna gabanta Yana kallan yadda take faman sheshsheka.ji yayi tausayin kanshi ya kama shi wato saboda an hadata da shi shiyasa ta kasa samun nutsuwa,sai ya fara zargin kanshi,shin Anya yayi mata adalci kuwa?ya fiye san kanshi da yawa,Amma ba yadda zaiyi zuciyar shi ce ta kasa jure hakuri da rashinta,ji tayi kamar numfashin mutum a kanta,tsoro ta ji ya fara kamata dan haka ta dago kanta da sauri.
Idonta ya sarke da nashi suka kurawa juna ido ba tare da sun san me suke yi ba,ita ta fara kawar da kanta tana yunkurin tashi tsaye ya ruko hannunta cikin nashi Yana mikar da ita.Bata hana shi ba ya fara tafiya tana biye da shi a baya kamar jela har zuwa dakinta.A bakin gado ya ajeta Yana goge mata hawayen da suke silalowa bisa fuskarta.a wannan karon ma bata hana shi na,domin yadda take jin zuciyar ta tana tafarfasa tasan dole tana bukatar a rarrashe ta.


Cikin raunin voice ya na duban fuskarta ya ce "kiyi hakuri Beby ni ne bakya so ko?,saboda an hadani da ke kike zubar da kolla ko?banyi mamaki ba saboda ni na jawa kaina,Amma ki sani bazan iya rayuwa babu ke ba,Dan haka ki saka a ranka auren mu Babu yaji ballentana rabuwa,har sai idan mutuwa ce ra rabamu"Yana kaiwa nan ya saki hannunta ya fice daga dakin Yana jin wani abu Yana Kara tasowa daga kasan zuciyar shi.

Ita ko baiwar Allah ba sautin kuka sai hawaye da suke silalowa cur-cur daga koyar idonta.

Anan ta kife tana hawaye har bacci ya dauketa da.


Tinda ya koma part din shi yake kaiwa da dawowa a tsakiyar falon na shi,Yana neman mafita Dan yasan idan har Abbah ya Fahimci Beby bata so to tabbas zai iya raba su,shiko idan aka yi haka to an saka shi cikin wani hali Wanda zai iya sanadin rusa rayuwar shi.da wannan tinanin ya kunna kira'ar kur'ani tare da Shirin konciya a lokacin har karfe 10:30 ta yi.

🅿️41
      📝Gudu yake sosai dan tunda suka taho basu tsaya a ko Ina ba,Farrah ko baccinta take ta yi hankali konce ita ce bata farka ba sai suna gaf da shiga garin Abuja.

A hankali ta fara motsa hannunta jin an Kara rike ta yasa tayi saurin bude idonta,sai yanzu ta tuna da inda take saboda ta ji dadin baccin.

Hannun ta take bi da kallo har zuwa Kan hannun shi ganin wasu gashi baki masu salki da sheki konce saman fatar shi yasa ta ji ya wurin ya birgeta,Kara kura idonta tayi tana kallon kekykyawar fatar shi.A hankali ta fara kokarin zare hannunta daga cikin nashi.ganin haka da yayi yada yadan sassauta rikon da yayi mata,zare hannunta tayi tana turo bakinta,Amma a ranta tana son ta shafa wannan gashin na hannun shi,Dan ita fa gaskiya ya birgeta.

Hanyar gidan Aunty Husnah ya nufa da ita.




Jabir kuwa tunda yaga tafiyar bro din nashi,ya mayar da kalla shi ga Bahijja.ita ko cikin kunya ta dan ja gefen mayafinta tana Kare fuskarta.

Cikin dariya ya ce "lallai kina da aiki,wato ni kike jin kunya ko? To gwamma ki yakice ta tun yanzu,idan ko ba haka ba to ni zan fara cire maki ita,Dan ga dukkan alamu wannan kunyar zata iya cuta ta eheh,na dai fada maki"Yana yin gaba ta bishi a baya.A tare suka shiga falon,sallama suka yiwa su Momy akan zai mayar da ita gida kafin ya wuce gurin aiki.

Sosai Momy ta nuna farin cikin ta akan wannan hadin,tayi masu addu'a da fatan alkhairi,har tana jan Bahijja tana cewa "shi dai wannan da kike gani yana da farin jinin Yan mata,Dan haka sai kin zage damtse kin kama kayan ki,idan ko ba haka ba to *YAN MATAN* *ZAMANI* su kwace shi".


Ita dai Bahijja sai dai tayi murmushi Dan Daman can bata fiye yawan magana ba.
Da haka suka yi mata sallama ya nufi goruba road Dan ya sauke Bahijja,shi Kuma ya wuce gurin aiki.





        **************

"Lallai zan tarwatsa farin cikin su,zan ruguza jin dadin su,Wai meyasa wannan lokacin yake zuya min baya?A lokuttan baya Ina ganin nafi kowa samun nasara,Amma yanzu meyasa burina yana gaf da cika sai watsu matsalar take kokarin dakatar da ni ne? why?why?"tana dafe goshinta.


Daga bayanta ta ji an dafa kafadar ta,hakan yasa ta juyo ganin khairiyya ya ce yasa ta ture hannunta gefe daya tana yimata mugun kallo.

Itama khairiyya tashi tsaye tayi tana cewa "Kinga me yayi zafi haka kike kokarin hucewa a khaina"itama tana hararar ta.


Bilkisu jiyowa tayi fuskar ta sharkaf da hawaye tana yimata kallan kin isa ,cikin kakkausar murya ta ce "kema ban yarda da ke ba,domin na fara tinanin akwai saka hannun ki a cikin wannan al'amarin,Dan haka ko ki fada man gaskiya ko na dauki mummunan mataki a kanki"tana matsota.



"Haba kawata ke kinsan bazan taba cin amanar ki ba,Kuma bazan yarda a haka baki da ni ba wurin hana cikar burin ki ba,Amma yanzu yi hakuri zauna ki sanar da ni matsalar da take faruwa dan musan yadda zamu shawo Kan lamarin"tana kokarin zaunar da ita a bakin katifar.


Hawayen fuskar ta take gogewa kafin ta mayar da dubanta ga kawar ta ta cikin rauni ta ce "na shiga cikin wani yanayi sakamakon mummunan labari daya riske ni a yanzu,Wai Yasir sun koma Abuja shida Farrah a matsayin ma'aurata ni Kuma sun barni anan da dawainiyar ciwon San da,Dan Allah ki fada min ya zanyi?wace hanya zan bi dan ganin na mallaki Yasir a rayuwa ta,domin shi ne farin cikina,Ina ji a jikina idan har na rasa shi bazan ci gaba da rayuwa ba"tana Kara rushewa da kuka tare da dora kanta bisa kafadar Khairiyya.



Khairiyya Wata munafikar harara ta bi bayan ta da shi kasancewar ta kontar da kanta a jikin ta,wani tinani ya zo mata a ranta Dan haka cikin sauri ta dagota tana goge mata hawayenta tare da kontar mata da hankali kafin ta ce "Ke matsala ta da ke kenan,yanzu saboda namiji kike zubar da hawayen ki a banza?wallahi har kin bani kunya,haba sai kace ba mace ba ya kamata kiyi amfani da wannan damar wajen ganin kin hanasu konciyar hankali,ki haddasa fitina a tsakanin su,ya zamana har sai Kinga bayan farin cikin su kafin ki rabu da su,Kinga daga nan cikin samarin ki kawai sai ki zabi daya ki aura,tunda suma da kudin su ko?tana kokarin ganin da wace fuska zata anshi wannan shawarar ta ta.



A hankali Bilkisu ta nisa tare da sauke ajiyar zuciya tana duban kawar ta ta ta ce "Wallahi shiyasa nake Kara sonki kawata,Dan ni kota-kota wannan tinanin bace min yayi sai yanzu naga dacewar hakan,hakan kawai za'ayi".tana gogen sauran hawayen da suka bushe a saman fuskar ta.


Da haka suka ci gaba da tattatuna yadda zasu tafiyar da al'amuran su.kafin sukayi sallama Khairiyya ta nufi gidan su itama zuciyar ta cike da sake-saken yadda zata tarwatsa rayuwar Bilkisu 🤔🤔Tau fa Ana wata ga wata.




  


      ----------------------------


Yasir Yana karya konar gidan Aunty Husnah ya fara Kiran wayar ta,ba tare da bata lokaci ba tayi picking .Bayan sun gaisa yake sanar mata suna gaf da shigowa gidan ta zai kawo Farrah.cikin farin ciki ta amsa tana kokarin hada masu abinci.


Bayan gate man ya bude masu gate din a wurin parking yayi masauki,shaya fara fitowa kafin ya zagayo ya bude site din da Farrah take.

A hankali ta sauko da kafarta daya,kafin ta karasa fitowa hannunta ya riko suka jera zuwa cikin falon,sadda kanta tayi tana turo bakinta a ranta tana cewa "Ina dalili mutum sai faman kwakube min hannu yake yi kaman na shi ne"


Kamar yasan tinanin da take yi ya ce "ba hannunki ne kadai nawa ba,yanzu gaba dayanki kin zama mallakima sai yadda naga dama zanyi da ke,Dan haka ki lallaba ni inba haka ba........hmmm"Yana sakin wani shu'umin murmushi,tare da kallanta ta kasan idon shi.


Da sauri ta dago kanta tana kallan shi,saboda tayi mamakin jin abinda ya ce.bata ce mai komai ba kawai ta kauda kanta suka ci gaba da tafiya.

Daidai bakin kofar falon suka tsaya,door bell ya Danna Yana sakin murmushi.

Aunty Husnah ce ta bude cikin farinciki ta ce "woooooo luck at beautiful couples,Masha Allah gaskiya kanne na kunyi kyau"tana dariya ganin yadda Farrah ta Kara hade fusu kamar hadari.


Hanya ta basu suka wuce cikin falon.zaunar da ita yayi kafin shima ya zauna kusa da ita.yana sauke ajiyar zuciya saboda yadda yake jin gajiya.


"A lallai irin wannan zuwan bazata,Ai ni jiya fishi nayi shiyasa na tahowata ganin Dady ya hana na taho da ita"cewar Aunty Husnah tana aje masu kayan abinci a Kan dan table din saman kafet.


"Hmmmm ai yanzu gamu mun zo da kanmu"cewar Yasir yana Jan table din gaban shi.


Cikin yin kasa da voice ya ce "Beby ki ciyar da ni yunwa nake ji"

Shiru tayi ganin Yadda Aunty Husnah take kallanta,sai tayi kamar bata ji ba.


Shima kyaleta yayi yaci gaba da magana shi da Aunty.


Sai can Aunty Husnah ta galla mata harara "ta ce wallahi Farrah yau so kike na fara bata maki rai anan gidan"


"Ehm to menayi Kuma,ni kullin nace mai laifi,komai zanyi banyi daidai ba"tana sunburo baki alamar shagwaba.


"Ban sani ba"cewar Aunty Husnah tana mikewa tsaye "Kuma ki  bari na dawo na tadda baki zuba ma shi abincin ba".tana wucewa dakin ta.



A hankali ta fara zuba mai abincin tana gunguni kasa-kasa.da haka ta gama tana turamai  gaban shi ta mike da sauri ta nufi cikin daki,Dan tasan Yana iya cewa sai ta bashi a baki.



Girgiza kanshi yayi Yana murmushi,Dan yasan abinda takewa gudu.da haka yaci gaba da cin abincin shi.


Saida ya kammala kafin Aunty Husnah ta fito,har wajen Motar ta rako shi suna Kara tattatunawa.



"Yanzu dai kasan dole kayimana alart ko, saboda saina Kara gyara yarinya ta kafin ka dauketa,Dan naga alamar  idonka a bude yake,Dan haka nake so Muje gurin *AISHA ALIYU* *GARKUWA* ta  gyara Mana ita tsaf yadda zaka rinka yimana kuka"tana kunshe dariyar da take neman kufce mata.



Murmushi yayi Yana cewa "Ai kyaleni da ita,wallahi ko zata gane kuranta,Dan kinsan cewa tayi ni raggo ne tau so nakeyi tsakanin nida ita a gane waye raggo?".


"Karfa kasa in hanaka ita,Dan nasan wannan tazurancin naka saika fanshe shi,to wallahi zan iya hada maka bom ya tashi da Kai"tana yimai dariya,ganin yadda danda nan ya marairaice fuska Yana bata hakuri.

"Na tuba Aunty da wasa nake yi,bazan yimata komai ba".


"Tau yanzu dai ka sallame mu kafin a gama gyaran gidan".


"Ba matsala ki turoman nomber account,zuwa anjima idan na zauna saina turo maki ko?".


"Tau mun gode Allah ya kara arziki,Kuma Dan Allah idan ka tafi karka isheni da zarya Dan so nake yi yarinyar ta taji gyara ciki da waje".


"A beg My Aunty 🙏Yana hade hannuwan shi guri guda"


Dariya ya bata sosai ganin yadda yake rudewa yanzu duk ya daina wannan Shan kanshin nan,lallai Farrah tayi dace,idan harta kontar da hankalinta to zata ji dadi a gurin Magaji.



Da haka ya shiga motar shi,yayi mata sallama tare da cewar gobe da yamma zai dawo,saboda yana da ayyuka a office.itama Aunty Husnah ta nufi cikin gida.

🅿️40
      📝 Murmushi yayi Dan jin wata dubara ta fado mai a ran shi.ya kudirta a zuciyar shi a gobe litinin da safe kafin ya tafi zai aiwatar da wannan plan din,Dan Yana jin karfin gyuywar insha Allah zaiyi nasara.da haka bacci ya dauke shi Yana Allah Allah safiya tayi.




A washe gari ne itama Farrah ta shirya nata plan din Wanda take ganin idan har tayi haka to zata iyayin nasarar rabuwa da wannan likakken auren, kodan ta auri zabin ranta.


Karfe takwas ya fito cikin Shirin shi sai tashin kanshi  yake yi,jakar wasu kayan shi ya saka cikin bort sai wayoyin shi da ya zuba cikin ajjihun rigar shadda.cikin gidan ya nufa Yana Addu'ar Allah yasa a yau yayi wenner dan gaskiya hakurin shi ya fara kasawa,gwamma a ba shi matar shi tun kafin wata matsalar ta taso.


A falon yayi sallama yana zama Kan kujera.Momy ce ta fito daga kiching da plates a hannunta.

"Magaji ba dai harka fito ba?"


"Eh Momy na fito,Ina so na shiga da wuri kasancewar yau Monday "

"Tau Bari na hada maka break kafin ka wuce"cewar Momy tana kokarin hada kayan a babban tire.


Bisa kafet din ya dauko Yana cewa "Dady ko ya fito?"

"A a yadan koma bacci ne dazu,Amma idan kanasan ganin shi sai a sanar mai"


"Tau bari muga na kammala break idan bai tashi ba sai a sanar mai"


"Ok Bari Nima na karasa aikinnan kafin lokacin ka kammala"tana aje mai kayan abincin a gaban shi.



Cikin nutsuwa yake cin abincin duk da akwai fargaba a tare da shi na kada Farrah ta tona mai asirin abinda ya dade da yimata,Dan ya fahimci rike abin da tayi a ranta shiyasa ta kasa yafe mai,Amma Insha Allah zaiyi iya kokarin shi Dan ganin ya wanke lefin shi a gurinta ta hanyar faranta ranta.
Dai dai ya kammala Dady ya sauko daga site din shi.gaishe shi yayi cikin girmamawa,sannan ya dora da "Am Daman Dady wucewa zanyi,Kuma inaga zan Dan samu tsawon lokaci ban zoba saboda wasu ayyuka da suka taso man,shi ne na ce Bari na sanar maku".


Tun da ya fara bayani Dady ya fahimci inda maganar ta shi ta dosa Dan haka ba tare da ya ce mai komai ba,yayi Kiran Momy.
Tana zuwa ya ce je ki tahoman da Farrah.juyawa tayi zuwa dakin,A bisa abin sallah ta ganta ta kudindine cikin hijaf.

A hankali ta fara tayar da ita.Farrah!Farrah! Tana daddabarta.

Cikin jin bacci ta fara motsawa,dakyar ta bude idonta jin yadda suka yi mata nauyi alamar baccin bai ishata ba.
"Ki tashi Dadyn ku yana Kiran ki"

Wartsakewa tayi jin ance Dady yake kiranta.mikewa tayi suka fita a tare,Momy na gaba tana biye da ita a baya.


Tsugunnawa tayi tana cewa "Dady Ina kwana?"


"Lafiya Lau,kina jina yanzu nake so kiyi sauri ki shirya duk wasu kayan ki da kikasan zaki bukata ki dauka minti ashirin na Baki".


Zata yi magana ya dakatar da ita ta hanyar cewa "umarni na Baki ba shawarar ki nake nema ba".

Daga bayan su suka ji ance "kibi zabin iyayen ki Dan  shi ne abinda zai zame maki alkhairi a rayuwar ki,ki sani wadda kika yarda da ita kike kokarin ganin kin sanar da ita sirrin ki,to ki sani tana gaf da tarwatsa rayuwar ki idan har Baki kiyaye ba"


Dukan su suka juya Dan ganin wacece wannan ta shigo masu gida Kai tsaye ba tare da izinin su ba.


Farrah cikin mamaki take kallan ta ganin tabbas ita ce,yasa ta taso da gudu suka rungume juna.

"Bahijja Ashe kice"tana Kara rushewa da kuka daga Momy,Dady har Yasir tsayawa suka yi suna kallan su.


"Tabbasa Maganar ki gaskiya ne,domin Farrah har yanzu ta kasa gane wane irin so Bro yake yimata"cewar Jabir da yanzu  yake shigowa falon.


Dagowa tayi tana duban sho,cikin karkarwar Baki ta ce "Da...man ...ba..ka so...na? meyasa...kaima.. kake....ko..Karin guduna...?"



  gyaran murya Dady yayi kafin suka ankare, umarnin ya basu suka da su zauna.

Cikin San sanin wacece ita ya fara tambayar ta wacece ita?Kuma daga Ina take?domin duk cikin kawayen Farrah Basu taba ganinta ba.



Ana ta sanar da su sunan ta Bahijja da course din su daya da Farrah,Asali sun fara kawance ganin su Bilkisu sun janyeta yasa ta kyaleta.to a shekaran jiya ne Bilkisu ta kirata tana neman taimakonta akan tana so ta zo wurin Farrah ta yaudareta domin ta fito da ita su hadu,akwai wani saurayinta da yake san ganinta,to shi ne na samu nayi mata plan harta sanar da ita kullin da take hadawa marar kyau akan ita Farrah.


"To a gaskiya shi ne naji bazan iya daurewa ba,ganin ba abinda Farrah tayi masu sai alkahiri,shi ne na naimi taimakon Ya Jabir yayi min jagora muka taho tare.



Kowa a gurin ya tsorata da jin wannan tashin hankali.anan take Yasir ya tuna da irin mafarkin da yayi marar kyau,tabbas Allah ne ya so su da ba'a San abinda zai faruba.



Dady nisawa yayi ya ce "Alhamdlillah Allah mun gode maka daka takaita faruwar wannan al'amarin,ba don wayin  mu ba sai Dan ikonka da hikimar ka,da Farko yarinya Ina yimaki godiya kwarai akan kokarin da kika yi na ganin kin taimaki Diyar mu,kema Ina yimaki addu'ar Allah ya Baki miji na gari.

Gaba dayan su suka amsa da "Amen"


"Bahijja ku je da Farrah ta shirya kayanta yanzu zata bi mijinta insha Allah ba tare da bata lokaci ba.yana mikawa Jabir key din gidan shi da yake a Abuja.


"Magaji wannan key din gida ne,ku fara zama kai da Farrah kafin nayi magana da Abban ku".


Hannu yasa Yana amsa zuciyar shi cike da farin ciki ganin yau zai tafi da muradin zuciyar shi ,zasu rayu a karkashin inuwa daya.cike da farin ciki yakewa kanin mahaifin nashi godiya tare da sanya albarka.


Bahijja kuwa kama hannun Farrah tayi suka wuce dakin ta.
Suna shiga ta rushe da kuka tana rungume Bahijja,ita sosai taji tausayin kawar ta ta,a ranta tana cewa "Daman rayuwa haka take wasu idan ka yarda da su Basu da amana,Amma mun gode Allah da al'amarin baiyi nisa ba.


Cikin kontar da hankalin ta fara rarrashin ta tare da fada mata kalamai masu kontar da hankali.da haka tayi nasarar saka ta shiryawa suka fito.Dady kadai suka tadda sau Jabir da yake zaune gefe daya ga dukkan alamu wata maganar suke yi,Dan naga Jabir dai murmushi yake yi Yana faman sosa keya alamar jin kunya.

Da haka dukansu suka fito har Momy data fito daga dakinta da wata leda a hannunta.


Har bakin motar da Yasir ke ciki Suka kaita inda  Momy take ta Kara yimata nasiha tare da kontar mata da hankali.Jabir ya saka kayanta a bort.


Dady ya matsa inda suke tasaye ya kamo Hannun Farrah ya nufi inda Yasir yake jingine Yana kallan su,hannun shi ya kamo ya hada da nata ya ruke Yana kallon Fuskar Jabir "ya ce Magaji kasan me nake san sanar da kai,Dan haka basai nayi maka bayani ba illa iyaka na ce kaji tsoron Allah".Yana gama fadi ya juya.

Shiko Yasir jikin shi ne ya ji yayi sanyi,Kara damke hannunta yayi har saida taji wani yar a jikinta saboda sanyin da ta ji.gaban motar ya bude ya sakata Yana mayar da murfin ya rufe.daga Bayan shi ya ji an dauki tafi raf-raf-raf juyawa yayi gani Dan kanin na shi da Bahijja yasa ya sakar masu murmushi tare da daga masu hannu alamar jinjina 👍.ya bude mazaunin drever ya zauna.


A hankali Jabir ya matso ta gefen da Farrah take ya dan duka daidai kanta cikin sanyi ya ce "kanwata abin alfahari na,idan har kika yiwa Babban Yaya biyayyay zan ji dadi, Amma ki sani duk sanda kika Bata mai rai to zuciya ta zata sanar min Kuma zanyi bakin ciki,sannan Ina bukatar Addu'ar ki Dan Nima na samu wadda zata zamu garkuwar rayuwa ta da farin ciki na"Yana kamo Hannun Bahijja.


Da sauri ta dago kanta tana San gaskata abinda kunnenta ya ke jiye mata.
Tabbas da gaske ne ganin yadda suke mata dariya.Haushi ta ji ya kamata,wato sun dauketa sakarai kenan.


Ganin haka yasa Yasir ya ce Kai bro kada fa ku rudamin mata eehe,ku je mun sallame ku sai munzo biki.yana kokarin rufe glass din motar.


"Wayyo big bro harda korar mu ake yi,Tau Allah ya kiyaye hanya sai mun zo suna"Yana daga masu hannu.


Kunya ta ji duk ta kamata ,sadda kanta tayi tana rufe fuskar ta da mayafinta a zuciyar ta tana cewa "wato su maza ba ruwan su, idan suka yi wani abin sai dai kai ka ji kunya.da haka Yasir ya ja Motar suna daga masu hannu har suka fita daga gate din gidan .


Suna fita daga babban gate ya ruko hannunta cikin nashi,a haka ya ci gaba da tukinshi Yana kara sauke ajiyar zuciya  Yana Kara gudun motar.
Duk abinda yake yi tana jin shi,tayi biris da shi zuciyar ta na halbawa da sauri,hakan yasa ta jingina da bayan kujera tana rufe idanunta jin yadda sanyin A/C yake ratsa bargon jikinta.ga wani kanshi mai sanyi daya cika motar .
Suna cikin tafiya da haka har bacci ya dauke ta.


Kallanta yayi ganin yadda baccin yayi mata kyau,hannunta ya Kara damkewa Yana jin wani dadi yana ratsa ranshi, murmushi yayi a fili ya furta "Yar rigimata kenan"Yana Kara gudun motar shi tare da yin addu'o'in neman tsari.

🅿️42
       📝A bedroom ta tadda Farrah ta konta,dayan hannunta ta saka tana damke cikinta ga wasu siraran hawaye suna silalowa ta gefen idonta."tashi muje kici abinci"cewar Aunty Husnah tana kokarin tayar da ita tsaye, 
A falon suka zauna,abinci ta zuba tana Dora mata bisa cinyar "maza ki cinye kafin na hadamaki ruwan wanka"anan ta barta  tana juya tsokali a hannun ta,Dan ita kadai tasan yadda zuciyar ta take yimata wata azababbiyar suya.hakanan ta daure tana tura dankalin ,Amma badomin tana jin dadin sa ba.


A haka Aunty Husnah ta kara dawowa ta tadda ita.idan baki ci tashi muje Dan ni Ina da aikin yi.tailet ta nufa da ita,anan ta nuna mata ruwan data hadamata da turarinka wanka a ciki.wasu kaya ta dauko mata Wanda zata saka,kafin ta koma falo tana ci gaba da ayukanta.


Bayan ta fito wanka anan bakin gadon ta zauna ta tallafe kumatunta da dukkan hannuwanta buyi.tinani take yi a zuciyar ta Wai Bilkisu ce take kokarin aikata haka a gareta duk kokarinta na san ganin ta kyautata mata,Amma a karshe zata saka mata da haka.ita kam tayi alkawarin daga yanzu ba ruwanta da kawayen wannan ZAMANIN,gwamma tayi rayuwar ta ita kadai.Alamar tafiya taji ana nufo dakin da take,da sauri ta mike tana shiryawa.






         ____________________

A katsina ko Bayan tafiyar su Yasir da Farrah, suma su Jabir suntafi Dady ya Kira Abbah Yana sanar mai tafiyar su Yasir da abinda ya faru .godiya yayiwa kanin na shi tare da Addu'ar Allah yasa Basu zaman lafiya da zuri'a dayyi ba.ya amsa da amen.nima na ce Amin.



Bilkisu ce take ta faman kaiwa da komowa a tsakiyar dakinta bayan tafiyar kawar ta khairiyya.sosai ta shiga rudani ganin komai na kara cakude mata,tinawa tayi da rigimar da sukayi da sukayi da Bahijja,anan taba kanta amsa lallai ba makawa itace ta fallasa sirrinta,da wannan tinanin ta danwa wata nomber call,dukda wani bangare na zuciyarta Yana sanar da ita ta hakura da Yasir domin kuwa yayi mata nisa.Amma ta kasa yarda da hakan.

"Hello barau ana jina ko"cewar Bilkisu tana zama bisa katifar tsakar dakin.

"Eh Ina jinki Hajjaju,wani aikin ne ya taso?"
Ya amsa cikin amon muryar shi mai kama data gardawan kauye.


"Eh barau idan harka anshi aikin nan to kada ka Bari a samu matsala,Dan a wannan karon bazan yadda da hakan ba,kawai anjima mu hadu da Kai wurin da muka Saba haduwa"tana gama fada mai ta kashe wayar tana saka ta cikin hand bag ta fita daga dakin.




     ________________

A yinin wannan rana har zuwa dare Farrah a haka suka wuni ita da Aunty Husnah tana Kara koyamata wasu abubuwan da suka shafi yanayin zaman takewar aure.Ita dai Farrah jinta kawai take yi Dan Tasha alwashin saita rama abinda yayi mata.





       _WASHE GARI_ 
A washe gari ne tunda yamma Yasir yake faman shirye-shirye Dan zuwa ganin abin kaunar tashi.wanka ya dauka sosai sai faman zuba kanshi yake kamar wani sabon ango.danyan boyel ne dark blue ya saka danki yamai shar,ya kara fito da samartakar shi,sai cover shueis days saka black sumar Tasha gyara gwanin sha'awa.
Yana daura agogon shi ya nufi site din Aunty safiyya.tana ganin ya shigo falon ta ce "Masha Allah son irin wannan cin gayu haka sai Ina?"tana murmushi.

Shima murmushin yayi Yana zama Kan kujera"Hmmmm Aunty gurin Beby zanje Dan zuwa jibi nake so idan ankammala gyaran gidan mu wuce"Yana dan sadda kanshi alamar kunya.


"Tau Allah ya bada zaman hakuri,sai kayi ta hakuri da Beby dan har yanzu kuruciya na damunta,zaka ga shirme kala-kala,Kai zaka dorata akan hanya"


"Tau insha Allah Aunty ngd,Bari naje saina dawo"Yana mikewa tsaye.


"Tau ka gaishe w Auntyn take"cewar Hajiya Safiyya tana wucewa dakin mai gidan nata.


Da haka ya tayar da motar shi,yayiwa gidan Aunty Husnah tsinke.


Wallahi Farrah idan baki yi sauri kin fito daga toilet din nan ba ranki zai baci,haba ke kwata-kwata kamar Baki da wayo ayita fama dake,to ki Bari har ya karaso kinanan kiga yadda zanyi da ke"tana fita daga dakin cikin jin haushi,saboda doorbell da taji ana dannawa.


Tana budewa Yasir ta gani a tsaye Yana murmushin shi mai tsada.

"Wayyooooo kanina kaga wani mayen kyau da kayi ko🤔?lallai idan kuka haihu zamuga damsels girls"tana matsawa alamar ya wuce.

Shidai baice komai ba illa wucewa da yayi Yana Kara jin wani farinciki a kalbin shi.


Waige-waige ya farayi,Dan ganin ta inda zumar tashi zata fito,Amma shiru.daga bayan shi yaji Aunty ta ce"to wallahi sai dai ka jira,dan tun dazu ta shige toilet taki fitowa".


Dan Bata rai yayi Yana kallanta yace "Tom ni Bari na fito da ita"da sauri ya nufi hanyar dakin.duk Kiran da Aunty Husnah ke yimashi bai tsayaba.


Yana shiga dakin kanshi tsaye ya tura kofar toilet din.

Daidai ta fito daga Bathroom tana kokarin daura towel,a hankali yayi sadaf-sadaf sai ji tayi an fige towel din daga hannunta.cikin tsoro ta jiyo da tsinkewar zuciya bakinta na kakkarwa ta fara kokarin Kare kirjinta da su kadai ya tsurawa mayun idanun shi Yana jin jinin jikin shi Yana neman tsayawa.

A hankali ya fara matsota tana yin baya har saida ta ji ta jingina da jikin mirron wurim,kafin ta fara kokarin tsugunnawa.da sauri ya tarota Yana jefar da towel din hannun shi.gaba daya ta fado cikin faffadan kirjin shi,wani yarrrrrrrr yaji lokacin da nashanunta suka sauka akan kirjin shi ajiyar zuciya ya sauke Yana Kara matseta, A hankali ya lalubi kunneta wata makirar iska ya fara huramata mai kamar da yanayin fito.da sauri yaji ta Kara rike shi gam tana juya kanta bisa kirjinshi ga wata matsitsiya da take shi,ji yayi kamar tana yimai tafiyar tsutsa Dan haka da sauri ya rabata da jikin shi steal Yana rike da ita,gani yayi gaba daya ta birkice tana lumshe idonta,kirjinta yake kallo Wanda suka gama tafiya dw sauran imaninshi.A hanakli ya fara daga hannunshi ya dire su akan..............

🅿️44
      📝Washe garin ranar ya kama juma'a ne Yasir da wani farinciki Wanda bazai iya misaltashi ba ya tashi,sosai yake jin wata sabuwar annashuwa tana lullube illahirin jikinshi.da haka ya shirya ya nufi CBN wurin aikin shi dukda ya Dan so ya makara.


Farrah tunda Aunty Husnah ta tayar da ita da asuba bata Kara samun damar Kara runtsawa ba da sunan bacci,domin irin tsoro da fargabar da suka makaye zuciyar ta,duk yinin ranar cikin sanyin jiki take yinshi,sai wasu magunguna da Aunty take faman duramata wasu idan taji da zaki ta shanye wasu ko saboda baurin su sai dai ta saka mata  kuka.yanzuma Bayan an sauko masallaci tana cikin kujera a konce tinani duk yayi mata yawa ga shi tana tsammanin yanzu luctures sun kankama school ta rasa wa zata tinkara da batun komawa makaranta.hannu biyu ta saka tayi tagumi tv take kallo Amma a badini tinani ne take yi.

Aunty Husnah da yanzu ta shigo falon da babban tray a hannunta tsayawa tayi tana kallanta ganin yadda tayi nisa a duniyar tunani.tary'n ta aje akan table din wurin kafin ta zauna kusa da ita,a hankali ta dafa kafadarta . firgigit tayi tana mazurai kamar marar gaskiya.

Aunty Husnah bata ce da ita komai ba illah wata kula data bude kaza ce guda a dafe da romonta sai tashin kanshi take yi.a karamin plate ta juye ta dora mata akan cinyoyin ta cikin tubune fuska ta ce "yi sauri ki cinye Ina jiranki "

Marairai fuska ta farayi,tana kwabe baki cikin zubda kolla ta dubi Auntynta ta tace "please Aunty wallahi cikina zai fashe,kiyi hakuri na koshi"tana hade hannuwanta alamar ban hakuri 🙏

Shiru tayi ta kyaleta tana ci gaba da Danna wayarta.
Itama Farrah shiru tayi tana goge kolla,Dan tunda taji tayi shiru to ba zata kulata na,sai kawai ta wanke hannunta ta fara cin kazar.

"Banda kashin zaki ci,ki cireshi tsokar kawai ake bukatar kici"haka taji muryar Auntyn Husnah tana fada mata.

Itama shiru tayi Bata Kara cewa komai ba har saida ta cinye tsokar tas ta shanye roman,duk da ta koshi turawa kawai take yi Dan ba yadda zata yi.

Gani tayi ta yanko mata kayan fruits ciki da wani Dan madaidaicin plate ta Kara Miko mata.da sauri ta zaro idonta tana kallan Auntyn tata.ita ko da ido tayi mata inkiya da cinye ki bani plate din.shiyasa cikin sanyi farrah ta ansa.A hankali take tura kayan fruits din har ta kusan shanyewa,da taji kamar zata yi amai sai kawai ta fashe da kuka.Aunty Husnah sororo tayi tana kallanta,wato ita dai abin kuka baya yimata wahala.tashi tayi ta Bata wuri,ruwan wanka ta Kara hadamata da turarika masu kanshin sanyi a ciki.

Hannunta tazo ta kama ta nufi toilet din da ita.har ciki ta kaita tana cewa mayar da hankali kiyi wanka Ina jiranki.ta fice ta barta anan a tsaye.

Ta Jima a wurin kafin ta cire Yar rigar dake jikinta ta shiga cikin bathroom din ta fara cuda jikinta da ruwan masu kanshin dadi,har wani lumshe ido take yi.

Lokacin data fito har an idar da sallar la"asar Dan haka ta dauro alwala ta gabatar da tata.tana zaune aunty ta Kara dawowa da wata matashiyar budurwa na biye da ita a bayanta."taso Farrah idan kin idar"cewar Aunty Husnah tana ba budurwar umarnin ta jirata.dakyar Farrah ta yarda aka fara yimata kwalliyar Dan cewa tayi Bata so.karfe biyar da rabi sun gama shiryata cikin doguwar rigar material mai danbanzan kyau da kyalli.mayafi aka sakamata da set na yari da banglos masu tsada dukda ba kunshi sai kanta da aka gyara mata,Amma kar kuso kuga kyan data zuba sai tashin kanshin humra da colakcer take yi irin na Yan maiduguri.tayi Shar abinta dukda bata yin fara'a tayi kicin-kicin da fuska ita a dole fishi take yi.


Falo suka nufo da ita,dakyar take tafiya saboda tsinin takalmin data saka.

Shiko Yasir tun dazu yazo,Yana zaune Yana kallan ta hanyar da zasu bilo saiko yaji alamin tahowar su ta wurin bedroom din,da sauri ya tashi da murmushi a saman fuskar shi.A hankali suke tahowa da mayafin aka rufe kanta har zuwa fuskarta dukda shara-shara yake ana iya hango fuskarta.suna karasowa gabanshi Suka tsaya Aunty Husnah ta ce "to nidai na huta gata na damka maka ita,Dan haka yanzu aiki ya rage naka sai kasan yadda zakayi da rigimar ta"tana dariya.

Cikin fargaba ya Kara matsota har suna jin numfashin juna ya saka hannuwan shi ya rungumota.kusan a tattare suka sauke wata ajiyar zuciya mai zafi.Farrah matsitsiya ta farayi tana son kwace jikinta Amma sai ya Kara matseta tsam Yana shinshinar kanshin jikinta.

Aunty Husnah ta fito mata da akwatinan kayanta.da sauran tarkacen ta na bukata sai wani akwati da ban da ta zuba kayan kanshi da wasu kayan sirri dukda kowanne da takadda a jiki na yadda zata yi amfani da su.
Driver ya kaisu cikin Burt din motar Yasir kafin Aunty Husnah ta sallami mai makeup ta tafi.har zuwa wannan  lokacin suna a yadda suke sai faman matsitsiya takeyi Dan kafafunta har sun fara sagewa.a hankali naga Yana yimata  rada a cikin kunne banji me take fada mata ba naga sai ta Kara shigewa jikin shi,shi Kuma Yana murmushi.

Aunty ce tayi gyaran murya ganin abin nasu bamai karewa ba ne,da sauri ya dago Yana dariya "to miss love ku mije ko,inba anan zaku kwana ba?"tana yin gaba.

Yana shafa Dan siririn sajen shi da dayan hannun ya ce "yo Aunty idan muka kwana nan ba kuba wallahi ko makotanku sai mun hanasu bacci ballantana ku"Yana kunshe dariya,hannunta yaja suka bi bayanta.


"Ohhh ni Asam'u jikar Hashim,yanzu Magaji saboda tsabar baka data ido ni kake fadawa haka?to wallahi kabi min kanwa a hankali na dai fadama,baiwar Allah shiyasa duk yau ta kasa sukuni duk a tsorace take"tana kamo hannun Farrah daidai sun karaso bakin motar.site din da zata zauna ya bude mata,a maimakon ta shiga sai ta rirrike Aunty Husnah tana fashewa da wani sabon kukan.tausayin Yar kanwarta ta  ji ya kamata  rarrashin ta ta farayi tana bata hakuri da haka har tabar kukan sai ajiyar zuciya take saukewa.Yasir duk Yana kallansu,sosai yaji tausayin kanshi ya kamashi  Wai Baby saboda an hadata da shi take wannan kukan.yana ganin Aunty ta sakata gaban motar shima ya bude mazaunin driver  ya zauna da addu'a a bakinshi.rufewa tayi tana yimasu Allah kiyaye hanya,itako Farrah bata iya amsawa ba saboda kukan dake cin zuciyar ta.da haka ya tayar da motar suka haura titi ba tare daya Kara kallon inda take ba.hanyar gidan Babanta taga ya nufa Dan haka ta danji sanyi a ranta Daman tana so taga Mamanta.

Har gaf da bakin kofar falon kafin yayi parking.shiya fara budewa ya zagayo kafin ya budemata,hannunta ya riko a hankali ta ziro kafafunta tana biye da shi suka nufi cikin falon.da sallama ya bude kofar suka shiga.Aunty ce kadai a falon tana kallon labarai Suka shigo.

"A a Magaji ai da kun wuce kawai dare Yana Kara kusantowa,Amma ka biyemata kuka taho nan"


Zama yayi itako da gudu ta fada Kan Aunty safiyya tana saka wani sabon kukan.dafe kanshi yayi a ranshi yana cewa "yauni na bani da wannan yarinyar,nasan haka zata yi ta min Dan ta hanani samun farinciki.

Dan tureta Aunty safiyya tayi tana cewa "ke ni bansan sakalci dagani kada ki gajiyar da ni,Kuma ki yiman shiru ko na tshi na baki guri"tana kallan yadda duk ta lafe a jikinta.

Ita dai shiru tayi tana sauke ajiyar zuciya,sai can da taji duk sinyi shiru ta ce Aunty kema yanzu bakya sona ko,duk kun tsaneni"tana lumshe idonta daya jike da ruwan hawaye.

"Inji waye ya ce mun tsane ki,ba tsanar ki mukayi ba Baby gatane mike San yimaki Wanda ko Bayan ranmu nasan zaki ji dadin hakan"tana gyara mata mayafin kanta.

Yasir fita yayi Dan jin ta fara yimata nasiha mai ratsa jiki da jini,Dan haka ya koma mota yana jiran fitowar su.

Sosai tayi mata nasiha kafin ta dauko wata jaka da Kaya a ciki ta kamo hannunta suka fito.a gaban motar ta ajiyeta tana doramata jakar akan jinyarta kafin ta rufe tana yimasu addu'a,da sauri ta juya jin wani kuka Yana kokarin kufcemata saboda sai yanzu take jin maraicin rabuwa da tilon diyarta ta,dukda ta Jima Bata gabanta,Amma taji ba dadi sai dai ba yadda zatayi wannan shi ne gatan da zasu yimata.

Cikin sanyi yake Jan motar kasancewar har an fara Kiran sallar magriba a masallacin unguwar.saida yahau babban titi kafin ya Kara gudun motar Yana hamdala da hailala ga Allah (s w a)daya nufeshi da ganin ranar auren shi.tun kafin su shiga cikin unguwar bacci ya dauketa saboda irin kukan da Tasha duk ta goge kolloliyar da aka yimata.yana zuwa bakin gate din ya Danna horn Wanda aka samomai a matsayin gate man shi ne ya leko ganin mai gidanne yasa da sauri ya bude yana yimai barka da zuwa.amsawa yayi kafin ya wuce ciki.
Har yayi parking bata farka ba Dan haka daya fito zagayowa yayi ta inda take jakar ya cire daga Kan cinyarta danak ya dauketa a hannunwan shi da kafa ya rufe motar kafin ya nufi hanyar cikin gidan.A hankali ya tura tare da yin addu'ar shiga gidan.itako Bata bude idonta ba Amma ta farka tun sadda taji ya dauketa .kyaleshi tayi Dan Farrah akwaita da San jiki kamar mage 🤣.

Darect cikin bedroom dinta ya nufa da ita.shimfideta yayi Yana cire mata mayafin da nadin dankwalin da akayimata.waje ya koma duk ya shigo da kayan da suka zo da su kafin yayi arwalla,bayan ya fito ya fara tayar da ita a hankali,Amma farrah da yake bata jin nasiha shiru tayi ta kyale shi.gajiya yayi kwai ya fita zuwa masallaci.tana jin ya fita ta mike tana turo Dan karamin bakinta,rigar ta cire da sarkar ta fitar da takalmin daga ita sai pant da bra sai ondy haka ta nufi toilet,towel ta daura suma inners ta aje su a toilet din wanka ta Kara yi ta dauro alwala.tana fitowa dakin ta rufe kafinta daga akwatin kayanta shiryawa tayi cikin atamfa ta tayar da salla.tana nan har lokacin isha'i yayi ta gabatar.kafin ta fesa turare tayi konciyar ta Dan Bata jin yunwa sosai saboda ciye-ciyen da tayi.


Shiko daga masallaci wani restaurant ya nifa take away yayi masu da wasu lafiyayyun dakwalen kaji hadi da lemuka kafin ya nufo gidan.

Yana aje motar kayan ya dauka ya nufi hanyar falon.wani sanyin kanshi yaji ya ratsashi,Wai yau shi ne da Baby a gida daya karkashin inuwa daya a matsayin ma'aurata,sosai yaji wani sanyi a ranshi dukda a wani bangaren Yana jin fargaba Dan baisan ya zasu Kare da Yar rigimar tashi ba.whooooo freinds gaskiya gidan ya hadu tun daga waje wasu mayun flawors ne masu Dan Karan kyau gate din kanshi abin kallo ne,babban falo ne Wanda yaji manyan kujeru papules color komai na falon papules ne idan ka cire plasma da freadge harta labulayen kalar su ne.sai bedrooms kusan guda biyar sai wurin dinning area da site din kitchen kusa da wurin,kowanne bedroom akwai kayan purnitures masu kyau da tsada,Amma kowani daki kalar su daban kitchen din kanshi yayi masifar haduwa.

Dakin ya wuce Yana murdawa yaji shi a rufe Dan haka ya dauko spare key ya bude,can kuryar gadon ya hangota ta kanannade guri daya.light ya kunna sannnan ya aje kayan hannun shi.

A hankali ya haura gadon,danak ya mikar da ita,Bata San da shigowar mutum ba sai ji tayi an tayar da ita zaune.da sauri ta wartsake tana neman fasa ihu,hannun shi ya saka ya rufe mata bakin lura da yayi da hakan.

"Shiiiiiiiii tashi muyi salla sai ki koma baccin kin ji?"Yana Kara tayar da ita.

"Ehmm ehmm ni bana sallah fa"tana sunne Kai.

Murmushi yayi jin abinda ta ce ya ce "eh naji tashi hakanan miyi"bai Bari ta Kara magana ba ya sungumeta zuwa tiolet din.a tare sukayi alwalar ya kamo hannunta hijaf ya Mika mata wadda ya gani akan gadon,abin sallah kuwa a shimfide ya ganshi alamar anan tayi sallah.
Shiya jasu suka gabatar da sallah raka'a biyu ta Mika godiyar su ga Allah.bayan sun idar dafa kanta yayi Yana kwararo addu'o'i kafin ya fara yimata tambayoyi a game da addini,Amma Farrah tayi kememe ta kyaleshi bawai Dan Bata sani ba Dan Farrah tun tana ss one tayi saukar alkur'ani mai girma.akan dole ya kyaleta.kayan daya kawo ya bata umarnin ta juye su cikin plate,tana zinburo baki ta nufi kitchen din shiko kaman wani soko haka yabi bayanta da kallo yadda take juya kugunta sai yaga kamar tana sane take yi,Amma yayi alkawarin yau sai ya saka tayi hankali yadda zata rinka respect dinshi.
Tana dawowa ta juye ko wanne a cikin plate din.kayan fruit din ta dauka ta sake komawa saida ta wanke su kafin ta yanko su a saman plate ta kawo gaban shi ta aje.har zata juya ya ruko hannunta tana juyowa bata sani ba gaba daya ta fado saman shi.kunnenta ya lalubo Yana radamata "ko ki tsaya muci abincin nan ko yanzu nayi maki dura Kuma kinsan zan iya ko?"Yana daga mata gira.

Tsam ta tsaya suka fara ci saboda Bata so a samu matsala,Allah Allah take su gama ya fita daga dakin ta saka ke a jiki.

Suna gamawa ta kuskure bakinta,dukda bata ci na kirki ba,turare ta Kara fesawa tana kokarin hawa gado wayanshi tayi Kara,dagawa tayi taji Yana cewa to Aunty gata nan.Miko mata yayi ta amsa.a kunne ta Kara sai taji muryar Aunty Husnah tana cewa "Farrah a cikin jakar nan akwai turare yana nan a wata gold din kolba kiyi amfani da shi zaki ji kanshin jikin ki kinji ko?"

Gyada kanta tayi kafin ta amsa da" to"ta Mika mai wayar.
Dariya yayi Yana cewa" tou Aunty mungode Allah ya saka da alkhairi".Yana kallan farrah da take kiciniyar bude troly.


"To Amin saida safe" ta kashe wayar

Wuceta yayi ya nufi toilet,tana ganin turaren ta budeshi shafawa tayi a lungu da sako na jikinta.kafin ta mayar ta saka wasu riga da wando na bacci masu santsi ta hayewarta gado ta konta bayan ta Kara iskar fanka.

Ya dade Yana wanke jikin shi kafin ya fito,ganinta yayi idonta a rufe a tunanin shi harta fara bacci,Amma sai yaji tana cewa "Dan Allah ya idan ka fita ka rufeman kofar"tana Kara gyara konciyar ta.


Dan Jim yayi kafin yace "to ba damuwa"yaci gaba da Shirin shi.itako tayi tinanin da gaske fita zaiyi kamar yadda ya ansa,Dan haka taci gaba da baccinta hankali konce.a kalla ya kwashe kusan two hours Yana shiryawa kafin ya idar a lokacin kusan karfe shadaya na dare.wayan shi ya dauka ya koma bakin gadon Yana duba wasu sakonni.

Lemu ya dauko a cup wani abu Naga ya tsiyaya a ciki bayan ya zuba lemun kafin a hankali ya fara kurba Yana lumshe ido har ya shanye shi tas ya aje cup din.

Ganin shabiyu ta gota duk Yana zaune hakan yasa ya kashe wayar tare da kashe light din ya kunna dum light kafin ya haura Kan gadon.hularta ya janye ganin ta kusa fita yana sinsinar kanshin kanta.ido ya lunshe jin wani sihirtaccen kanshi ya ratsa kwalwar shi.

A hankali yake shafa sumar kanta har zuwa Kan fuskarta a ranshi yana cewa "Baby ki tausayamin ki tashi bazan iya kwanan zaune ba Ina kallanki kina baccinki hankali  konce ba"yawo ya dingayi da hannun shi a jikinta har yayi nasarar janye Yar karamar rigar data saka,kadan ya hana ya fasa Kara saboda yadda yaga surar jikinta.tafin hannunshi ya kifa saman kirjinta jin wani taushi da sanyi daya rata tafin hannun shi yasa shi sauke wata munafikar ajiyar zuciya Yana Kara shigewa jikinta.kamar a mafalki ta ji ana lalubarta saita Dan motsa jin an Kara riketa yasa tayi saurin ware idonta tar sai akan fuskar shi,sosai taji faduwar gaba,a ranta ta ce "Wai shi wannan me yake nufi ya gama ceman Ina bin maza sannan yanzu ya zo Yana kwakube ni wallahi bazan yarda ba.shiko baisan ta farka ba sai ji yayi zata gantsara mai cizo a damtsen hannu yayi saurin janye hannunshi Yana Kara riketa,kokowa tahau yi dashi ganin zata Bata mai lokaci gashi ya Kai makura dole ta saka ya mirgina samanta gaba daya ya sakar mata nauyin shi yadda bazata iya motsawa ba.cikin karkarwa ya lalubo kunnenta da yaji Yana wani asirtaccen kanshi ya saka harshenshi Yana wasa da shi a ciki,Yana yi Yana hura mata isakar bakin shi,ai kafin kace me saiga yarinya nunfashinta na neman daukewa tana wani mimmike wa.dan dagata yayi kadan Yana Kara kaimi wurin hura mata iskar bakin shi.Ita ko baiwar Allah rukunkimeshi tayi tana juya kanta Dan ita kadai tasan abinda take ji tuni ta fice daga hankalinta tana neman taimako.da sauri ya kife fuskar shi saman kirjinta Yana mayar da numfashin da sauri da sauri kamar ya Kara zukata Bata San likocin data sanya hannunta ta kamo...........

🅿️43
       📝A hankali ya fara daga hannunshi ya dire su akan fararen yatsun hannunta Wanda suke bisa Kan kirjinta tana kokarin Kare su.wurin daya cijeta kwanaki yake shafawa ganin ya warke Amma ya danyi duhu alamar raunin ya dishe.gani yayi tana kokarin zubewa kasa yayi saurin tarota tana dawowa jikin shi yaji nunfashinta ya dauke dif babu alamar shi.cikin sauri ya janyo towel din daya yar a kasa ya nadeta a ciki ya nufi bedroom da ita,a Kan gadon ya ajeta Yana shafa sumar kanta,saboda tsabar kidima da yayi ya manta da suma tayi surar jikinta kawai yake kallo yadda duk yake ganinta cikin Kaya ashe a zahiri ta wuce haka,santala-santalan fararen jinyoyinta yake shafawa har suwa samanta Dan tunda ya ajeta towel din ya yaye daga jikinta hakan ya bashi damar Kara kallonta sosai.bakinshi ya dora saman nata ya lalubo halshenta cikin sauran karfin daya rage a jikin shi ya fara hura mata iskar bakinshi a hankali take responding shiko Yana ansar Wanda take fitarwa.kawai sai ji yayi taja wani dogon numfashi tana Kara rukunkimeshi kamar wadda zata koma cikinshi.kokarin bude idonta tayi tana ganin shi ne ta mayar dasu ruf ta rufe su.tana kokarin tureshi daga samanta.Nauyi ya Kara sake mata yadda ta kasa tashi.ganin ba zata iya tureshi ba yasa ta hakura tana Kara lafewa a jikinshi.shigo goganka ya samu abinda yake so Dan Kara kaimi yayi wurin bata kiss mai zafi saida ya tabbatar da tayi lakas kafin ya mike waje yayi,har yakai bakin kofa ya tsaya cak ba tare daya juyo ba ya ce "raguwa kawai dake,Amma sai cika baki kike zanyi maganin rashin kunyar nan taki" Yana karasa fadi ya fice da sauri,kicibis sukayi da Aunty Husnah tana kokarin shigowa dakin.kallon banyarda da kaiba take jifanshi da shi,Dan shafa keyar shi yayi Yana mazurai.rabawa yayi zai wuce tana kallan shi tace "Ina farrah?"

A takaice ya Bata amsa "tana ciki"
Yayi saurin ficewa daga gidan gabaki daya.ciki ta karasa hangota tayi ta lafe cikin bed tana juya kanta.karasawa tayi tana Kiran sunanta,Farrah!Farrah!!
A hankali ta juya tana bude idonta da suka sauya color.
"Ehhm Aunty bacci nake ji"tana Kara rufe idonta.

Aunty Husnah tsayawa tayi tana karemata kallo ganin yadda take mayar da numfashi da sauri da saur.a zuciyarta tace "wannan yaran Yan duniya ne,wato ni zasu nunawa *ZAMANI* ,shi daga bari in fito da ita yayi luf Yana lalube yar mutane,to da sake tunda naga tun jiya ya turo kudin gwamma na gyarata ya dauki kayanshi su tafi tun kafin Dady yaji labari yayi ta yimin fada"gani tayi ta Kara juyawa tana dafe barin kanta da hannunta.hakan yasa ta kyaleta sai tayi tinanin kila kanta yake ciwo.

Tana fita Farrah ta bude idonta tabi bayanta da kallo,tana addu'ar Allah yasa karta Kara shigowa Dan wata muguwar kasala taji ta rufeta.kawai juyawa ta karayi tana jan blanket ta Kara rufe jikinta.



Yasir kuwa tunda ya shiga mota ya kasa tayar da ita,Dan shi kadai yasan abinda yake ji Yana yimasa yawo a jikinsa.kwantar da kansa yayi bisa sitiyarin motar Yana Jan numfashi sosai jikinshi yake kakkarwa, addu'a yayi ta karantowa dakyar ya Mika hannunshi Yana kunna karatun kur'ani kafin yaji jikinshi yadan fara sassauta wa.A hankali ya tayar da motar ya samu ya fita daga gidan.




    _________________
Jabir bawan Allah soyayya tayi karfi shida Bahijja,Dan har iyayenshi sun shiga cikin maganar yanzu hankalinshi konce Bahijja na kula da shi yadda ya kamata,a dayan bangaren Kuma tana zuwa makarantar ta.


Momy taki hakura da batun Yasir ya saki farrah,Dan randa ta samu labarin an bashi ita sun tafi Abuja ranar tayi ruwan masifa ba adadi Dan sai Jabir yayi ta bata Baki kafin tadan sauko.shiko Abbah fita yayi daga gidan ya kyaleta tana ta kumfar Baki ita kadai.



Yanzu Bilkisu hankalinta ya Kara tashi,domin Bata da nutsuwa tsoratata akeyi sosai tun bayan sun dawo daga gurin boka gagara inda ya sanar da su sai dai suje wani gurin Amma shi bazai iya raba wannan ma'auratan ba,domin kaunace da Allah ya dasa a zukatan masoyan Wanda ba mai iya rabasu sai Allah.tun a sannnan jikinta yayi sanyi to tun daga ranar data konta sai ayi ta tsorata ta tana faman firgita.sosai innarta ta shiga damuwa ganin halin da yarinyar ta take ciki hakan yasa ta nufi wani isalamic CEMIST tayi masu bayanin abinda ke faruwa,magunguna suka Bata ta biya kudin tare da yimata bayanin yadda za'ayi amfani da su.ta dawo gida tana Bata magungunan.



        
      **************

Tun kafin ya karasa jikin gate din yake faman danne horn har ya karaso ,da gudu mai gadin ya bude mai gate din Yana yimai barka da dawowa.bai tsaya bashi amsaba saboda burinshi ya ganshi a toilet ya sakarma kanshi ruwa ko yaji saukin abinda yake yimai yawo a bargon jikinshi da jijiyar shi.ko parking din kirki bai yiba ya balle marfin motar ya fito wayar shi kadai ya dauko amma sauran kayan a cikin motar ya barsu.


Turus yayi ganin Aunty safiyya da mai gidanta sun tsaya suna kallanshi.a sanyaye ya karasa Yana Kara yin kasa da kanshi.


"Wai son lafiya kake wannan uban horn kamar mai Shirin tashi sama?"cewar Aunty safiyya tana Kara kallan yadda yanayin shi ya canza.


Gefen fuskarshi yadan shafa kafin yace "kaina ne yake ciwo shi ne nakeso nayi sauri Dan nasha magani".Yana kokarin juyawa.

Basuce da shi komai ba,shiko ganin haka yasa da sassarafa ya karasa part din shi Yana sauke ajiyar zuciya.

Juyawa sukayi zuwa falonsu a lokacin har an fara kiraye-kitayen sallar magriba a masallatan cikin unguwar,Dan haka Alhaji Ubah yayi arwallah ya nufi masallaci.
Itako Aunty safiyya dakinta ta wuce da waya a hannunta tana kokarin Kiran nomber Aunty Husnah.tana jin ta fara ringing ta Kara a kunnenta,itako Aunty Husnah a lokacin tana fama da Farrah ta tashi tayi salla abin ya gagara yarinya ta nade a Kan gado tana ta faman birgima kamar wadda aka yiwa duka.

"Assalamu Alaikum Aunty Ina yini"cewar Aunty Husnah da take yaye blanket din da Farrah ta rufe jikinta da shi.


"Amin wa'alaikas salam kina jina ko?a gobe ba sai jibi ba nake soki ba Magaji matarshi su tafi gidansu,idan ba so kike yaron mutane ya rasa ranshi ba,Banda shirmen ki kina babba amma sai ki rinka biye shirmen Beby,tau wallahi zuwa gobe da yamma ki bashi matarshi idan ba hakaba zan sanar da Yaya kinji na fadamaki"


"Aunty wallahi ba laifina ne ba,shi ya kawota kafin Adan share masu gidan shiyasa,Amma kiyi hakuri insha Allah gobe zai tafi da ita"cewar Aunty Husnah muryar ta cikin sanyi,Dan ita kanta ya Bata tausayi,Amma tasan ko sun tafi Farrah gwarashi zatayi saidai inta karfi zai anshi hakkinshi.

Ji tayi ta kashe wayar ba tare da tace komai ba.

Duk maganar da sukeyi Farrah tana jinsu,Bata tantanceba sai ji tayi Aunty Husnah ta zuba mata dundun a bayanta tana cewa "ni gashi nan kina so ki jamin tashin hankali ko?duk kokarin da nakeyi a kanki bakya gani sai kinsa Dady yaimin fada,tashi ni Kona kirashi yanzu yazo ya tafi dake idan ma cinyeki zaiyi ni ba ruwana,da haka har kin saba zama da shi".


Farrah tana jin haka da sauri ta mike tana mitstsike idanuwa, cikin sanyin muryarta tace "Dan Allah Aunty kiyi hakuri karki bari ya tafi dani,wallahi tsoranshi nake ji"tana kwabe Baki alamar shagwaba.

"Oh Ashe tsoranshi kike ji,ai ba yanzuba tukun sai nan gaba zaki gane,ke ba wulakanci ba,bawan Allah Yana sanki Amma sai garashi kikeyi Dan kawai yadan rinka tabaki,to ai lokacin da yake kaiki makarantar Baki taba sanarwa kowaba ga abinda yake yimaki,shiyasa yanzu ko kin fada ba Wanda zai yarda ni kaina Dan ya amsamin da bakinshi cewa ya aikata da bazan taba yadda ba,Kuma ma a wannan ragoncin naki kike tinanin idan kun zauna gida daya zaki iya ramawa mutum daya Dan tabaki kadan saiki wani narkemai,ai wallaki idan ya gano logonki to kin shiga ukku,gwamma ki ware idan zaki ware idan ko ba haka ba wallahi zakiji a jikin ki,bawai nace karki sakar mai jiki ba  a'a kisan dai yadda zaki ja ajinki a gunshi Dan na Fahimci wannan Magaji bazai yimaki da sauki ba,kila duk bayan wata shida zamu rika zuwa suna"ta karshe fada tana gimtse dariyar da take son kufce mata.


Farrah kicin-kicin tayi da rainta tana kokarin fashewa da kuka ta nufi hanyar tailet tana cewa "Ai wallahi ya dai nemi wacce zata haifa mai yaran badai ni Farrah ba"


"Au haka ki kace ja'ira?to Allah ya bamu rai da lafiya munga ta inda zasu fito,miskila kawai ki Bari kiyi sake wata ta kwace maki shi a sannan zaki gane shayi ruwa ne"tana gyara shimfidar gadon.

Ita dai Farrah gaba tayi ba tare data tsaya jin karshen zancen ba.

"Yi sauri ki gama wankan na miko maki turaren garwashi ki tsuna"cewar Aunty Husnah.


Bawan Allah tunda ya shige part dinshi ya wuce toilet kanshi ya sakarma ruwa Yana lumshe idon shi saboda wani sanyi da yaji Yana ratsa shi.A bayan ya fito arwallah yayi ya saka sallabiya ya tayar da sallah.isha'i ma a gida yayi ko abincin dare bai ciba saboda azabar da yake ji tana addabar shi,shiyasa yayi alkawarin gobe zaije yayi tsaye saiya dauko kayanshi kafin ya baro gidan tunda an karasa kintsamasu gidan, a wannan ranar dai dakyar ya samu bacci barawo ya sace shi.

Haka itama Farrah ta kasance a bangaranta,Domin kuwa data rufe idonta movement dinsu na dazu take hangowa,a ranta tace to randa ya damke ni Kuma ya kenan?oh ni Farrah na bani Allah ya hadani da wannan katon ya zanyi da shi?..da wannan tinanin itama bacci ya dauketa.
