🅿️45
      📝Batasan lokacin data saka hannunta ta kamo kugunshi ba tana Kara mannewa da shi.shiko Yasir hakan ya Kara bashi wata dama ta musamman,Dan haka yayi ta kokarin ganin ya cimma burinshi,sosai take taimaka mai dan tini ta manta da Wanda take tare da shi saida taji Yana karanto addu'a kafin ta ankare,kokarin kwatar kanta ta farayi Amma Ina Farrah ta makara Dan wani zafi na tsahin hankali da taji Yana neman fasa kolwarta .to dai nayi nan 🏃🏃🏃.

Ina falo zaune nayi kuyu dukdan na samo maku karshen labarin,gyangyadi ne ya fara daukata ta can naji farrah ta buga wani Kara da saida ya saka na zabura zan koma dakin Dan ganin Wai me wannan bawan Allah yake yimata,Amma kashhhh sai naji kofar a rufe da key,dole na dawo inata jimami 😔.



A daidai lokacin da Farrah ta fasa wannan Karan,yayi daidai da lokacin da Bilkisu ta farka da wani azababen Kara Wanda duka gidan saida ya amsa,innar ta ce ta fito daga dakinta ta leka dakin na Bilkisu,ganin tana ta faman fisge-fisge ga wasu sururtai tana yi marassa dadin ji,cikin sauri Inna ta karasa gareta tana riketa ganin yadda take kokarin rugawa.tana tambayar ta lafiya?meya sameta?.Amma Bilkisu ta kasa bayar da Amsa.Addu'a ta fara tofa mata, kafin ta dauko sauran magungunan ta Bata wasu ta shafa mata na shafawa kafin tadan lafa tana juya kanta.A haka Inna suka Kai har asuba ba tare da sun runtsaba da zummar zuwa da safe zata kaita *ISLAMIC DAN FODIO* *CENTER* a yimata rukiya duk a iya saninta da Bilkisu Bata da aljanu,sai dai ko daga bisani abin ya sameta.



Yasir kuwa da baisan duniyar da yake ba,ba tausayi ballantana saurare yake gudanar da aikinshi Dan akalla yaje makka sau goma Yana dawowa a tsakiyar Daren nan (🤣🤣Ina Aunty saudat kinji har garinku yaje saboda tsabar lulawa da yayi 🙈🙊).

Farrah kuwa baiwar Allah tun tana sanin me yakeyi garta fita hayyacinta tana suma tana farfadowa,a karshe tayi wata Kara tare da sakewa gaba daya jijiyoyin ta suka daina halbawa.sai a sannan ya fahimci aika aikar da yake yi,har gwamma lokacin data cije shi,da lokacin da take faman Kiran Momy,Aunty Husnah,Aunty safiyya,harda su Dady ta rinka Kira Amma baiji tausayinta ba.aiko tuni ya rude ganin bata motsi mirginawa yayi gefe daya Yana shafa fuskarta data jike sharkaf da zufa saboda wahala da ta sha.

Kayanshi ya mayar kafin ya kunna light din dakin a zuwa lokacin har an fara Kiran sallar Asubah.ruwa ya dauko masu sanyi a freadge ya zuba a tafin hannun shi Yana shafa mata a fuska,shairu yaji haka yayi ta shafa mata ruwannan kafin yaga taja wani dogon numfashi tana fisge-fisge,da sauri ya aje gorar ruwan Kan site drower kafin ya riketa, wani wahalallen kuka ta saki tana jijjuya kanta saboda azaba..sun Jima a haka kafin ya kwantar da ita bisa kirjinshi.har zuwa lokacin batayi magana ba sai ajiyar zuciya da take faman saukewa.

Suna a haka aka fara salla a masallatai,kafin ya samu a hankali ya kwantar da ita saboda yaga tayi bacci.bargo ya rufa mata jin yadda jikinta ya dau zafi sosai yaji tausayinta ya kamashi.toilet ya wuce,wanka yayi ya gyara jikin shi tare da dauro alwala,Yana fitowa ya canza kayanshi tare da fesa turare ya shimfida abin sallah Dan Yana gudun ya fita Kuma ta tashi.bayan ya idar da sallah Yana lazimi wayar shi ya dauko Yana kunnata ganin har shida saura yasa ya fara Kiran wayar Aunty Husnah tana ta ringing ba'a daga ba harta katse,Kara Kira yayi a karo na biyu kafin tayi picking.
Sallama yayi kafin ya gaisheta jin muryar shi wani iri yasa tace "Magaji lafiya meya sameka naji muryar ka ta canza ?"

"Ammh Aunty Dan Allah ki taimake ni kizo,wallahi Farrah ce ba lafiya yanzu haka zazzabi ne a jikinta,Dan Allah ki zo please"ya karasa maganar kamar zaiyi kuka ..


Tana jin haka a ranta ta ce "nasan a rina,wannan rawar Kan da yake yi Allah yasa bai kassara masu yarinya ba"a zahiri ko sai ta ce "to Bari ganinan zuwa yanzu zan biya na dauko wata freind dina Doctor ce sai muzo tare ta dubata ko"

Da "to"ya amsa kafin ya katse kiran ganin Farrah tana faman juyawa,daga gani kasan baccin wahala ne take yi.

Saman gadon ya haura ganin yadda take yakune fiska.A hanakli ya Kura mata manyan eyes balls din shi Yana kallan kyakkyawar fuskarta Yana Kara sanya mata albarka Dan yau sakayau yake jinshi kamar Wanda aka yiwa sabuwar hallitta ,ga wani Nishadi da yake ji na musamman da yake jin Yana ratsa illahirin bargon jikinshi.karan mota yaji alamar sun shigo Dan haka yayi suarin fita.

Kofar falon ya bude ganin su a tsaye ita da Doctor din yasa ya gaishe da su, Doctor din kadai ta amsa tana janshi Dan ita ko Aunty Husnah cikin falon ta nufa suka bita a baya.har dakin suka shiga kafin ya dawo zuwa daya daga cikin bedroom din yayi konciyar shi Yana jin wani farinciki Yana zagaya kolwar shi.da haka bacci ya dauke shi da farar jallabiya a jikin shi.


Aunty Husnah ta haura saman gadon tana aje hijaf din jikinta a Kan mirror kafin ta fara yaye bargon daya rufamata.da sauri ta mayar tana kawar da kanta dan jin yadda gabanta yayi wata faduwa.a sanyaye ta ce "na shaiga ukku ni Asma'u meye haka Magaji ya aikata?wannan rashin tausayi har Ina?tana saukowa ta nufi toilet Dan hada ruwan zafi . Doctor Hadiza ce ta matsa kusa da Farrah tana cewa "yaran *ZAMANINMU* ai rawar kansu tayi yawa shiyasa ake yawan samun matsala,Dan idan Kinga irin haka to gaddama tayi mai,Kuma kinsan karfinsu ba daya ne ba"taba jikinta tayi jin yadda yadau safi yasa taji yarinyar ta bata tausayi Dan haka ba tare da bata lokaci ba ta fara bata taimakon gaggawa.bayan Aunty Husnah ta gama hada ruwan cak Doctor ta kudindine ta cikin towel ta nufi toilet da ita,har a sannan Farrah bata San inda kanta yake ba saida taji an dauketa duk a tinaninta shi ne,Dan haka ta saka kuka tana magiyar ya kyaleta itakam yau mutuwa zata yi.saida ta jita cikin ruwan zafi kafin taji Doctor'n tana cewa "yi hakuri kin ji insha Allah zaki ji sauki"kafin tayi saurin bude idonta tana ganin sune Aiko ta Kara karfin kukanta,tana cewa "Dan Allah Aunty ki tafi dani wallahi ya bashi da Imani kashe ni zaiyi,wallahi nidai bazan zauna da shiba mugu ne,cutata yake yi"sai hawaye cur cur daga idonta.



"Me yayi maki?"cewar Aunty Husnah

Harta bude baki zatayi magana sai Kuma tayi shiru tana cije lebanta na kasa cin ta fara jin sauki.sai ajiyar zuciya take saukewa.

Sun dade Doctor Hadiza tana ta canza mata wasu ruwan kafin suka barta ta gyara jikin ta tazo tayi sallah Dan tuni karfe bakwai ta kusa.


Tana wanka tana zubar da hawaye da haka ta karasa,wani ruwan ta gani masu kayan kanshi a ciki tayi amfani da su kafin ta daura wani towel din ta fara kokarin fitowa.

Bango take bi a hankali tana runtse idonta harta samu ta fito,a lokacin Doctor Hadiza ce kadai a dakin ita Aunty Husnah tana kitchen Dan hada masu breakfast.

Wata doguwar riga ta ciro mata mai taushi da hijaf ta saka abin busar da Kai ta sanya mata gashinta ya bushe,kasancewar ba kitso a kanta.da haka ta dorata bisa abin sallah tana janta "gaskiya kedai Farrah raguwa ce,da haka zamu zo amsar haihuwar kina langwabewa?"ita dai batace komai ba,ta fara sallar ta a zaune.


Bayan ta idar Aunty Husnah ta kawo mata kayan Kari kasancewar akwai komai na bukata a gidan shiayasa tayi saurin karasawa,a lokacin karfe tara har tayi.

Doctor Hadiza ganin Bata ga Yasir ba yasa tayi tinanin ko ya fita Dan haka ta ba Baba mai gadi takadda da kudi ya amso masu magani dukda da Dan nisa.


Kafin a kawo maganin ta hada mata tea mai kauri da Arish sai farfesun kayan ciki da yaji tarugu da kayan kanshi.kadan ta ci tana ta faman zubar da hawaye.
"Aunty Husnah ta ce "yarinya gwamma ki zage ki ci Dan anjima da dare ma Yana iya karawa"tana dariya.

Aiki Farrah tana jin haka ta Kara rudewa tana ta karkarwa da hawaye."Dan Allah aunty ki tafi dani wallahi tsoranshi nake ji,bazan iya ba mutuwa zanyi".tana Kara volume din kukanta.


Saida Doctor Hadiza tayi ta kontar mata bata hakuri tana kontar mata da hankali kafin ta Dan ji ta samu nutsuwa,Dan Doctor Hadiza ta ce zata fadamai kada ya Kara.

Bayan an kawo maganin suka Bata tasha,kafin suka Kara gyara gadon ta konta bacci.

Doctor Hadiza tafiya tayi bayan Aunty Husnah ta anshi nomber account dinta,Dan sallamar ta tana ta yimata godiya.


Bayan Aunty Husnah ta dawo falon ta fara neman inda Yasir ya konta.can daki na biyun karshe ta ganshi Yana ta sharar baccin shi hankali konce.kyaleshi tayi ta wuce gidanta da zummar idan ya tashi ta kirashi waya tayimai bayanin yadda zai Bata maganin,sai yaje da kanshi ya anso masu abincin rana.bayan ta tare mai nafef taji Karan wayarta dubawa tayi ganin nomber Aunty safiyya yasa tayi saurin dagawa tana gaisheta.

"Amm Husnah Dan Allah kice ki duba kanwar ki kiga ko lafiya,Dan wallahi tunda jiya da suka bar nan hankalina ya kasa konciya".

Murmushi Aunty Husnah tayi kafin ta ce "Aunty ki kontar da hankalin ki yanzu haka daga can nake tunda safe ya kirani sai naje da wata kawata ta dubata, insha Allah ba matsala,komai ya daidaita".


Ajiyar zuciya ta sauke tana ji a ranta Farrah an girma yanzu kila ta Kara hankali da wayo kafin ta ce"to Husnah na gode Allah ya Basu zaman lafi ki rinkayimata nasiha da nunamata yadda rayuwa take,Dan Wallahi Ina tausayin Baby duk randa Magaji zai Kara aure zata gane kuranta".


"A a Aunty insha Allah ma bazai Kara ba saboda Yana santa,itama Kuma zata yimai biyayya insha Allah"tana dariya danjin yadda Auntyn tasu tace.


"To Allah yasa ta gyara"cewar Aunty safiyya.

Aunty Husnah ta amsa da "Amin ya Allah"kafin sukayi sallama ta katse call din.daidai mai nafef din ya iso gate din gidanta,sallamar shi tayi kafin ta nufi cikin gidan tana murmushi Dan tadan drama Bata kareba idan Farrah ta farka taga sun taho,to shikam ya bani wuni zaiyi faman rarrashinta.

      📝Washe garin ranar ya kama juma'a ne Yasir da wani farinciki Wanda bazai iya misaltashi ba ya tashi,sosai yake jin wata sabuwar annashuwa tana lullube illahirin jikinshi.da haka ya shirya ya nufi CBN wurin aikin shi dukda ya Dan so ya makara.


Farrah tunda Aunty Husnah ta tayar da ita da asuba bata Kara samun damar Kara runtsawa ba da sunan bacci,domin irin tsoro da fargabar da suka makaye zuciyar ta,duk yinin ranar cikin sanyin jiki take yinshi,sai wasu magunguna da Aunty take faman duramata wasu idan taji da zaki ta shanye wasu ko saboda baurin su sai dai ta saka mata  kuka.yanzuma Bayan an sauko masallaci tana cikin kujera a konce tinani duk yayi mata yawa ga shi tana tsammanin yanzu luctures sun kankama school ta rasa wa zata tinkara da batun komawa makaranta.hannu biyu ta saka tayi tagumi tv take kallo Amma a badini tinani ne take yi.

Aunty Husnah da yanzu ta shigo falon da babban tray a hannunta tsayawa tayi tana kallanta ganin yadda tayi nisa a duniyar tunani.tary'n ta aje akan table din wurin kafin ta zauna kusa da ita,a hankali ta dafa kafadarta . firgigit tayi tana mazurai kamar marar gaskiya.

Aunty Husnah bata ce da ita komai ba illah wata kula data bude kaza ce guda a dafe da romonta sai tashin kanshi take yi.a karamin plate ta juye ta dora mata akan cinyoyin ta cikin tubune fuska ta ce "yi sauri ki cinye Ina jiranki "

Marairai fuska ta farayi,tana kwabe baki cikin zubda kolla ta dubi Auntynta ta tace "please Aunty wallahi cikina zai fashe,kiyi hakuri na koshi"tana hade hannuwanta alamar ban hakuri 🙏

Shiru tayi ta kyaleta tana ci gaba da Danna wayarta.
Itama Farrah shiru tayi tana goge kolla,Dan tunda taji tayi shiru to ba zata kulata na,sai kawai ta wanke hannunta ta fara cin kazar.

"Banda kashin zaki ci,ki cireshi tsokar kawai ake bukatar kici"haka taji muryar Auntyn Husnah tana fada mata.

Itama shiru tayi Bata Kara cewa komai ba har saida ta cinye tsokar tas ta shanye roman,duk da ta koshi turawa kawai take yi Dan ba yadda zata yi.

Gani tayi ta yanko mata kayan fruits ciki da wani Dan madaidaicin plate ta Kara Miko mata.da sauri ta zaro idonta tana kallan Auntyn tata.ita ko da ido tayi mata inkiya da cinye ki bani plate din.shiyasa cikin sanyi farrah ta ansa.A hankali take tura kayan fruits din har ta kusan shanyewa,da taji kamar zata yi amai sai kawai ta fashe da kuka.Aunty Husnah sororo tayi tana kallanta,wato ita dai abin kuka baya yimata wahala.tashi tayi ta Bata wuri,ruwan wanka ta Kara hadamata da turarika masu kanshin sanyi a ciki.

Hannunta tazo ta kama ta nufi toilet din da ita.har ciki ta kaita tana cewa mayar da hankali kiyi wanka Ina jiranki.ta fice ta barta anan a tsaye.

Ta Jima a wurin kafin ta cire Yar rigar dake jikinta ta shiga cikin bathroom din ta fara cuda jikinta da ruwan masu kanshin dadi,har wani lumshe ido take yi.

Lokacin data fito har an idar da sallar la"asar Dan haka ta dauro alwala ta gabatar da tata.tana zaune aunty ta Kara dawowa da wata matashiyar budurwa na biye da ita a bayanta."taso Farrah idan kin idar"cewar Aunty Husnah tana ba budurwar umarnin ta jirata.dakyar Farrah ta yarda aka fara yimata kwalliyar Dan cewa tayi Bata so.karfe biyar da rabi sun gama shiryata cikin doguwar rigar material mai danbanzan kyau da kyalli.mayafi aka sakamata da set na yari da banglos masu tsada dukda ba kunshi sai kanta da aka gyara mata,Amma kar kuso kuga kyan data zuba sai tashin kanshin humra da colakcer take yi irin na Yan maiduguri.tayi Shar abinta dukda bata yin fara'a tayi kicin-kicin da fuska ita a dole fishi take yi.


Falo suka nufo da ita,dakyar take tafiya saboda tsinin takalmin data saka.

Shiko Yasir tun dazu yazo,Yana zaune Yana kallan ta hanyar da zasu bilo saiko yaji alamin tahowar su ta wurin bedroom din,da sauri ya tashi da murmushi a saman fuskar shi.A hankali suke tahowa da mayafin aka rufe kanta har zuwa fuskarta dukda shara-shara yake ana iya hango fuskarta.suna karasowa gabanshi Suka tsaya Aunty Husnah ta ce "to nidai na huta gata na damka maka ita,Dan haka yanzu aiki ya rage naka sai kasan yadda zakayi da rigimar ta"tana dariya.

Cikin fargaba ya Kara matsota har suna jin numfashin juna ya saka hannuwan shi ya rungumota.kusan a tattare suka sauke wata ajiyar zuciya mai zafi.Farrah matsitsiya ta farayi tana son kwace jikinta Amma sai ya Kara matseta tsam Yana shinshinar kanshin jikinta.

Aunty Husnah ta fito mata da akwatinan kayanta.da sauran tarkacen ta na bukata sai wani akwati da ban da ta zuba kayan kanshi da wasu kayan sirri dukda kowanne da takadda a jiki na yadda zata yi amfani da su.
Driver ya kaisu cikin Burt din motar Yasir kafin Aunty Husnah ta sallami mai makeup ta tafi.har zuwa wannan  lokacin suna a yadda suke sai faman matsitsiya takeyi Dan kafafunta har sun fara sagewa.a hankali naga Yana yimata  rada a cikin kunne banji me take fada mata ba naga sai ta Kara shigewa jikin shi,shi Kuma Yana murmushi.

Aunty ce tayi gyaran murya ganin abin nasu bamai karewa ba ne,da sauri ya dago Yana dariya "to miss love ku mije ko,inba anan zaku kwana ba?"tana yin gaba.

Yana shafa Dan siririn sajen shi da dayan hannun ya ce "yo Aunty idan muka kwana nan ba kuba wallahi ko makotanku sai mun hanasu bacci ballantana ku"Yana kunshe dariya,hannunta yaja suka bi bayanta.


"Ohhh ni Asam'u jikar Hashim,yanzu Magaji saboda tsabar baka data ido ni kake fadawa haka?to wallahi kabi min kanwa a hankali na dai fadama,baiwar Allah shiyasa duk yau ta kasa sukuni duk a tsorace take"tana kamo hannun Farrah daidai sun karaso bakin motar.site din da zata zauna ya bude mata,a maimakon ta shiga sai ta rirrike Aunty Husnah tana fashewa da wani sabon kukan.tausayin Yar kanwarta ta  ji ya kamata  rarrashin ta ta farayi tana bata hakuri da haka har tabar kukan sai ajiyar zuciya take saukewa.Yasir duk Yana kallansu,sosai yaji tausayin kanshi ya kamashi  Wai Baby saboda an hadata da shi take wannan kukan.yana ganin Aunty ta sakata gaban motar shima ya bude mazaunin driver  ya zauna da addu'a a bakinshi.rufewa tayi tana yimasu Allah kiyaye hanya,itako Farrah bata iya amsawa ba saboda kukan dake cin zuciyar ta.da haka ya tayar da motar suka haura titi ba tare daya Kara kallon inda take ba.hanyar gidan Babanta taga ya nufa Dan haka ta danji sanyi a ranta Daman tana so taga Mamanta.

Har gaf da bakin kofar falon kafin yayi parking.shiya fara budewa ya zagayo kafin ya budemata,hannunta ya riko a hankali ta ziro kafafunta tana biye da shi suka nufi cikin falon.da sallama ya bude kofar suka shiga.Aunty ce kadai a falon tana kallon labarai Suka shigo.

"A a Magaji ai da kun wuce kawai dare Yana Kara kusantowa,Amma ka biyemata kuka taho nan"


Zama yayi itako da gudu ta fada Kan Aunty safiyya tana saka wani sabon kukan.dafe kanshi yayi a ranshi yana cewa "yauni na bani da wannan yarinyar,nasan haka zata yi ta min Dan ta hanani samun farinciki.

Dan tureta Aunty safiyya tayi tana cewa "ke ni bansan sakalci dagani kada ki gajiyar da ni,Kuma ki yiman shiru ko na tshi na baki guri"tana kallan yadda duk ta lafe a jikinta.

Ita dai shiru tayi tana sauke ajiyar zuciya,sai can da taji duk sinyi shiru ta ce Aunty kema yanzu bakya sona ko,duk kun tsaneni"tana lumshe idonta daya jike da ruwan hawaye.

"Inji waye ya ce mun tsane ki,ba tsanar ki mukayi ba Baby gatane mike San yimaki Wanda ko Bayan ranmu nasan zaki ji dadin hakan"tana gyara mata mayafin kanta.

Yasir fita yayi Dan jin ta fara yimata nasiha mai ratsa jiki da jini,Dan haka ya koma mota yana jiran fitowar su.

Sosai tayi mata nasiha kafin ta dauko wata jaka da Kaya a ciki ta kamo hannunta suka fito.a gaban motar ta ajiyeta tana doramata jakar akan jinyarta kafin ta rufe tana yimasu addu'a,da sauri ta juya jin wani kuka Yana kokarin kufcemata saboda sai yanzu take jin maraicin rabuwa da tilon diyarta ta,dukda ta Jima Bata gabanta,Amma taji ba dadi sai dai ba yadda zatayi wannan shi ne gatan da zasu yimata.

Cikin sanyi yake Jan motar kasancewar har an fara Kiran sallar magriba a masallacin unguwar.saida yahau babban titi kafin ya Kara gudun motar Yana hamdala da hailala ga Allah (s w a)daya nufeshi da ganin ranar auren shi.tun kafin su shiga cikin unguwar bacci ya dauketa saboda irin kukan da Tasha duk ta goge kolloliyar da aka yimata.yana zuwa bakin gate din ya Danna horn Wanda aka samomai a matsayin gate man shi ne ya leko ganin mai gidanne yasa da sauri ya bude yana yimai barka da zuwa.amsawa yayi kafin ya wuce ciki.
Har yayi parking bata farka ba Dan haka daya fito zagayowa yayi ta inda take jakar ya cire daga Kan cinyarta danak ya dauketa a hannunwan shi da kafa ya rufe motar kafin ya nufi hanyar cikin gidan.A hankali ya tura tare da yin addu'ar shiga gidan.itako Bata bude idonta ba Amma ta farka tun sadda taji ya dauketa .kyaleshi tayi Dan Farrah akwaita da San jiki kamar mage 🤣.

Darect cikin bedroom dinta ya nufa da ita.shimfideta yayi Yana cire mata mayafin da nadin dankwalin da akayimata.waje ya koma duk ya shigo da kayan da suka zo da su kafin yayi arwalla,bayan ya fito ya fara tayar da ita a hankali,Amma farrah da yake bata jin nasiha shiru tayi ta kyale shi.gajiya yayi kwai ya fita zuwa masallaci.tana jin ya fita ta mike tana turo Dan karamin bakinta,rigar ta cire da sarkar ta fitar da takalmin daga ita sai pant da bra sai ondy haka ta nufi toilet,towel ta daura suma inners ta aje su a toilet din wanka ta Kara yi ta dauro alwala.tana fitowa dakin ta rufe kafinta daga akwatin kayanta shiryawa tayi cikin atamfa ta tayar da salla.tana nan har lokacin isha'i yayi ta gabatar.kafin ta fesa turare tayi konciyar ta Dan Bata jin yunwa sosai saboda ciye-ciyen da tayi.


Shiko daga masallaci wani restaurant ya nifa take away yayi masu da wasu lafiyayyun dakwalen kaji hadi da lemuka kafin ya nufo gidan.

Yana aje motar kayan ya dauka ya nufi hanyar falon.wani sanyin kanshi yaji ya ratsashi,Wai yau shi ne da Baby a gida daya karkashin inuwa daya a matsayin ma'aurata,sosai yaji wani sanyi a ranshi dukda a wani bangaren Yana jin fargaba Dan baisan ya zasu Kare da Yar rigimar tashi ba.whooooo freinds gaskiya gidan ya hadu tun daga waje wasu mayun flawors ne masu Dan Karan kyau gate din kanshi abin kallo ne,babban falo ne Wanda yaji manyan kujeru papules color komai na falon papules ne idan ka cire plasma da freadge harta labulayen kalar su ne.sai bedrooms kusan guda biyar sai wurin dinning area da site din kitchen kusa da wurin,kowanne bedroom akwai kayan purnitures masu kyau da tsada,Amma kowani daki kalar su daban kitchen din kanshi yayi masifar haduwa.

Dakin ya wuce Yana murdawa yaji shi a rufe Dan haka ya dauko spare key ya bude,can kuryar gadon ya hangota ta kanannade guri daya.light ya kunna sannnan ya aje kayan hannun shi.

A hankali ya haura gadon,danak ya mikar da ita,Bata San da shigowar mutum ba sai ji tayi an tayar da ita zaune.da sauri ta wartsake tana neman fasa ihu,hannun shi ya saka ya rufe mata bakin lura da yayi da hakan.

"Shiiiiiiiii tashi muyi salla sai ki koma baccin kin ji?"Yana Kara tayar da ita.

"Ehmm ehmm ni bana sallah fa"tana sunne Kai.

Murmushi yayi jin abinda ta ce ya ce "eh naji tashi hakanan miyi"bai Bari ta Kara magana ba ya sungumeta zuwa tiolet din.a tare sukayi alwalar ya kamo hannunta hijaf ya Mika mata wadda ya gani akan gadon,abin sallah kuwa a shimfide ya ganshi alamar anan tayi sallah.
Shiya jasu suka gabatar da sallah raka'a biyu ta Mika godiyar su ga Allah.bayan sun idar dafa kanta yayi Yana kwararo addu'o'i kafin ya fara yimata tambayoyi a game da addini,Amma Farrah tayi kememe ta kyaleshi bawai Dan Bata sani ba Dan Farrah tun tana ss one tayi saukar alkur'ani mai girma.akan dole ya kyaleta.kayan daya kawo ya bata umarnin ta juye su cikin plate,tana zinburo baki ta nufi kitchen din shiko kaman wani soko haka yabi bayanta da kallo yadda take juya kugunta sai yaga kamar tana sane take yi,Amma yayi alkawarin yau sai ya saka tayi hankali yadda zata rinka respect dinshi.
Tana dawowa ta juye ko wanne a cikin plate din.kayan fruit din ta dauka ta sake komawa saida ta wanke su kafin ta yanko su a saman plate ta kawo gaban shi ta aje.har zata juya ya ruko hannunta tana juyowa bata sani ba gaba daya ta fado saman shi.kunnenta ya lalubo Yana radamata "ko ki tsaya muci abincin nan ko yanzu nayi maki dura Kuma kinsan zan iya ko?"Yana daga mata gira.

Tsam ta tsaya suka fara ci saboda Bata so a samu matsala,Allah Allah take su gama ya fita daga dakin ta saka ke a jiki.

Suna gamawa ta kuskure bakinta,dukda bata ci na kirki ba,turare ta Kara fesawa tana kokarin hawa gado wayanshi tayi Kara,dagawa tayi taji Yana cewa to Aunty gata nan.Miko mata yayi ta amsa.a kunne ta Kara sai taji muryar Aunty Husnah tana cewa "Farrah a cikin jakar nan akwai turare yana nan a wata gold din kolba kiyi amfani da shi zaki ji kanshin jikin ki kinji ko?"

Gyada kanta tayi kafin ta amsa da" to"ta Mika mai wayar.
Dariya yayi Yana cewa" tou Aunty mungode Allah ya saka da alkhairi".Yana kallan farrah da take kiciniyar bude troly.


"To Amin saida safe" ta kashe wayar

Wuceta yayi ya nufi toilet,tana ganin turaren ta budeshi shafawa tayi a lungu da sako na jikinta.kafin ta mayar ta saka wasu riga da wando na bacci masu santsi ta hayewarta gado ta konta bayan ta Kara iskar fanka.

Ya dade Yana wanke jikin shi kafin ya fito,ganinta yayi idonta a rufe a tunanin shi harta fara bacci,Amma sai yaji tana cewa "Dan Allah ya idan ka fita ka rufeman kofar"tana Kara gyara konciyar ta.


Dan Jim yayi kafin yace "to ba damuwa"yaci gaba da Shirin shi.itako tayi tinanin da gaske fita zaiyi kamar yadda ya ansa,Dan haka taci gaba da baccinta hankali konce.a kalla ya kwashe kusan two hours Yana shiryawa kafin ya idar a lokacin kusan karfe shadaya na dare.wayan shi ya dauka ya koma bakin gadon Yana duba wasu sakonni.

Lemu ya dauko a cup wani abu Naga ya tsiyaya a ciki bayan ya zuba lemun kafin a hankali ya fara kurba Yana lumshe ido har ya shanye shi tas ya aje cup din.

Ganin shabiyu ta gota duk Yana zaune hakan yasa ya kashe wayar tare da kashe light din ya kunna dum light kafin ya haura Kan gadon.hularta ya janye ganin ta kusa fita yana sinsinar kanshin kanta.ido ya lunshe jin wani sihirtaccen kanshi ya ratsa kwalwar shi.

A hankali yake shafa sumar kanta har zuwa Kan fuskarta a ranshi yana cewa "Baby ki tausayamin ki tashi bazan iya kwanan zaune ba Ina kallanki kina baccinki hankali  konce ba"yawo ya dingayi da hannun shi a jikinta har yayi nasarar janye Yar karamar rigar data saka,kadan ya hana ya fasa Kara saboda yadda yaga surar jikinta.tafin hannunshi ya kifa saman kirjinta jin wani taushi da sanyi daya rata tafin hannun shi yasa shi sauke wata munafikar ajiyar zuciya Yana Kara shigewa jikinta.kamar a mafalki ta ji ana lalubarta saita Dan motsa jin an Kara riketa yasa tayi saurin ware idonta tar sai akan fuskar shi,sosai taji faduwar gaba,a ranta ta ce "Wai shi wannan me yake nufi ya gama ceman Ina bin maza sannan yanzu ya zo Yana kwakube ni wallahi bazan yarda ba.shiko baisan ta farka ba sai ji yayi zata gantsara mai cizo a damtsen hannu yayi saurin janye hannunshi Yana Kara riketa,kokowa tahau yi dashi ganin zata Bata mai lokaci gashi ya Kai makura dole ta saka ya mirgina samanta gaba daya ya sakar mata nauyin shi yadda bazata iya motsawa ba.cikin karkarwa ya lalubo kunnenta da yaji Yana wani asirtaccen kanshi ya saka harshenshi Yana wasa da shi a ciki,Yana yi Yana hura mata isakar bakin shi,ai kafin kace me saiga yarinya nunfashinta na neman daukewa tana wani mimmike wa.dan dagata yayi kadan Yana Kara kaimi wurin hura mata iskar bakin shi.Ita ko baiwar Allah rukunkimeshi tayi tana juya kanta Dan ita kadai tasan abinda take ji tuni ta fice daga hankalinta tana neman taimako.da sauri ya kife fuskar shi saman kirjinta Yana mayar da numfashin da sauri da sauri kamar ya Kara zukata Bata San likocin data sanya hannunta ta kamo...........

🅿️47
       📝Asubar farko ita ta fara farkawa,kafin shi jin tana motsi a cikin jikinshi yasaka shi bude idonshi Yana kallon Fuskar ta tana ta faman turo Dan karamin bakinta,Dan so take yi ta tashi Amma ba hali saboda yanda yayi mata kyakykyawan riko.A hankali ya mike da ita a jikinshi ya nufi toilet,a tare suka dauro alwala shi ko ya fita zuwa masallacin dake kusa da su,ita ko ta kabbara tata a anan.

Bayan ya dawo again bacci Suka koma kasancewar ranar lahadi ne ba aiki,Dan shi Daman a office dinsu an bashi hutun one week Dan ya samu damar hutawa da iyalinshi.




Sosai Bilkisu ta Kara tsurewa da al'amuran duniyar nan,Dan yanzu samarinta duk sun gujeta saboda har yanzu bata dawo normal ba time to time rashin lafiyar takan motsa mata,wannan shi ne dalilin da yasa ta daina fita ko'ina har school ma ta hakura,daman wani saurayinta ne yake biyamata kudin makaranta to shima ya gudu,sosai Inna take yimata fada da nasiha ganin yadda ta dawo,shi yayanta salisu baisan abinda ake ciki ba saida rashin lafiyar ta kamata kafin Inna take sanar da shi,shima fada tayi tayi akan me yasa ba'a sanar mai ba sai yanzu to shi yayanke shawarar akwai wani abokinshi Daman Yana ta kwawar Yana San ita Bilkisun Amma yaki bashi goyon baya,to tinda hakane zai yimai magana akan ya turo magabatan shi tinda haka ta zaba.
Tuni Bilkisu ta saka kukan takaici,a zuciyar ta tace "yanzu no duk gayuna da fantamawa da nayi Amma na Kare a auren mai tuka adaidaita,ohhh ni Bilkisu ya zanyi da rayuwata?"hakuri inna tayi ta bata da nusantar da ita illar biyewa rudin *ZAMANINMU* ,Wanda dole mutum sai Yana Kai zuciya nesa tare da kokarin rike amanar duk Wanda ya baka amanar shi.da haka dai tayi ta yimata nasiha da wa'azantar da ita da nuna mata mahimmancin yadda da kaddara mai kyau ko marar kyau.sai a sannan hankali Bilkisu yadan konta,jikinta duk yayi sanyi.





Haka rayuwar su Farrah ta kasance a gidan auren su ita da Yasir soyayya suke Sha babu kama hannun yaro,dukda har yanzu shirmenta Yana nan Amma yanzu tadan Kara nutsuwa.Amma fa wani time din sai an dan Kara musamman idan ya nemeta wani lokacin kememe take yi ta hanashi,shiko tausayinta yake ji sai ya kyaleta Dan Yana ma ganin kokarinta da take iya juremai.sabuwar waya ya kawomata hade da sabon layi,duk nomber Yan family dinsu saida ya saka mata Amma Banda ta maza Dan yace ba Wanda zata Kira yajiye mai muryar mata.da haka dai kwanaki Suka Dan shuda har yau satin su biyar da tarewa Wanda yayi daidai da sauran sati daya bikin su Jabir da Bahijja,abu daya ke Kara Bata haushi duk lokacin da tayi mai zancen komawa makarantar ta sai ya hade rai ya kyaleta,sosai abin yake yimata ciwo wato saboda tsabar ya rainata ita tana faranta mai rai Amma shi bazai yimata abinda take so ba,to saita dau mataki kuwa akan hakan.


Bayan takira Aunty Husnah suna waya take cemata "Aunty Dan Allah ki yima ya Yasir magana Ina so na koma school har an kusa fara exam fa ,bana so nayi losing karatuna"tana marairaice murya.

"A a Farrah ni bazan shiga wannan uzirin ba,Dan kunfi kusa ki samu ki lallaba kayanki ya yarda,Dan kinsan yanzu ko su Dady Basu da iko da ke sai idan ya amince ,Dan haka ki bishi a hankali kinji"

Sosai tayi rau-rau da idonta kamar zata yi kuka ta amsa da "to"ta katse kiran.


Bayan sallar isha'i ya dawo gidan,a gajiye ya shigo Dan haka yasa ta hada mai ruwan Wonka ya shiga kafin ya fito ta fitar mai da kayan da zai saka.dan haka Yana fitowa ta kawo mai abinci da drink masu sanyi ta aje akan kafet.tana ta zunburar baki,lura da yayi da hakan yasa ya kyaleta baice mata komai ba har ya shirya ya ci abinci.itama Shirin konciya tayi tana chat a wayarta har zuwa time din ba Wanda ya tankawa dan'uwan shi.sai wuraren karfe 10 harta fara gyangyadi taji ya hayo bisa gadon,Dan haka tayi saurin mirginawa can karshen gadon tana gunguni,Sarai yaji abinda tace Amma sai ya kyaleta so yake yaga karshen gudunta kafin ya kamata.a hankali bacci ya fara fisgarta kamar a mafalki taji Yana lalubar kirjinta da sauri ta rike hannunshi tana bude idonta akan fuskar shi,dukda ba haske sosai Amma tana hango jikinshi saboda hasken duplight.rikeshi da tayi baisa ya fasa abinda yake yiba saima bakinshi da yake kokarin sakawa cikin nata tayi saurin kawar da kanta tana ture shi,cikin fitar da numfashi tace "Ni ka kyaleni bana so"


"Ni inaso,Kuma kinsan idan kika hanani zaki kwana mala'iku na yimaki tsinuwa ko?Yana kokarin fitar da Yar karamar rigar baccin data saka.


Ta rasa me zatayi kawai saita saka mai kuka,hakan yasa yayi saurin dakatarwa Yana rungume ta cikin jikinshi.

Cikin salkewar murya da San kasancewa da ita ya fara magana "Haba......my ..zu..m. ..a kinsan...ke..ce. farinciki...na ko...?"dakyar maganar take fitowa,Dan haka ta Kara sautin kukanta tana kokarin tashi.


Kyaleta yayi Yana mirginawa gefe daya,itama blanket taja ta Kara rufe jikinta tana fitar da hawaye.sun Jima a haka kafin a hankali ya ce "saboda me zaki hanani?"


Kai tsaye ta bashi amsa da "saboda bakasan farincikina,ni na Bari kana ta sassaka ta kaman wani tuwo Amma kaki barina na koma school,Kuma wallahi gobe saina tafi gidan Aunty tinda haka ne"tana Kara wani sabon kukan.



Da sauri ya ce "Ai kina tafiya Niko saina auro wacce ko yaushe bazata guje ni ba,Kuma na hana su Dady su barki ki koma school din.



Ai Farrah tana jin haka Bata San lokacin data rarrafo inda yakeba kanshi ta fada tana kuka ta ce "yi hakuri kaji Dan Allah bazan iya shearing dinka da wata ba,wallahi zuciyata zata iya bugawa,bazan iya jurewa ba"tana kontar da fuskarta a kirjinshi.


Dariya ta kusan kufce mai ganin yadda duk ta rude,Daman duk randa ta yimai gardama haka yake yimata to da taji haka zaka ga ta bashi hadin Kai,to yanzuma hakance ta kasance.

Cikin tsare gida fuska shaye da toka yace "ni tashi ki bani wuri,tunda kin zabi tsinuwar mala'iku akan ki faranta min,Kuma Bari kiji makaranta ba zaki taba komawa ba Kuma kinsan Nike da iko akan haka,Dan haka gwamma tun wuri ki cireta a ranki,saboda duk abinda kike bukata Ina da kudin da zan siyamaki inhar baifi karfin arziki na ba,to Banga dalilin yin wata makaranta ba,Dan ko kinyi Daman ba zakiyi aiki ba,tau gwamma ki hakura shi zai fiyemaki sauki.

Sosai kalaman shi suka bata haushi,Amma ba yadda zata yi illa ta hakura,Dan ita zancen Kara auren da yace shi yafi daga mata hanakli.da haka dai a karshe ya lallabata akan cewar ranar laraba zai kaita Katsina sai Bayan bikin su Jabir saita dawo.baiwar Allah Farrah ba yadda ta iya tunda yayi mata wayo Dan haka ta hakura tare da bashi hadin Kai suka Lula.




Ya cika alkawari Dan a ranar laraba Suka yiwa garin katsina tsinke,a Barhim Suka sauka sosai Momy tahau yin nan nan da su ganin yadda Farrah tayi tubarkalla daka ganta kaga mai ciki,dukda ta lura su Basu San da cikin ba a zuciyarta ta ce "hmmmm Yaran *ZAMANINMU* sai a barsu kamar ba itace take borin Bata sanshi ba,Amma ga sai wani shagawaba take yimai".shi suna gama gaisawa da Momy ya wuce GRA gidan iyayen shi itako ya barta anan.


Abinci Momy ta kawo mata Dan lokacin Nusaiba tana makaranta.ganin jolof din taliya yasa ta ce ita dai wallahi kodon zogale da kuli take so,ba zata ci wannan abinba.


Momy cewa tayi "ni dai bani da shi wallahi sai dai ki fadawa mijinki shi ke da wannan alhakin,sai ya siyomaki ko akan hanya ne" ta shigewar ta kitchen ta barta anan tana faman hamma.kiranshi tayi a waya tana sanar mai dan haka yace tayi hakuri yanzu zai kawo mata,kafin ta kashe wayar.anan Kan kujerar wani bacci mai dadi ya dauketa.saida Momy ta fito ta ganta har tayi bacci girgiza Kai tayi tana kara gyara mata konciyar .

🅿️45
      📝Batasan lokacin data saka hannunta ta kamo kugunshi ba tana Kara mannewa da shi.shiko Yasir hakan ya Kara bashi wata dama ta musamman,Dan haka yayi ta kokarin ganin ya cimma burinshi,sosai take taimaka mai dan tini ta manta da Wanda take tare da shi saida taji Yana karanto addu'a kafin ta ankare,kokarin kwatar kanta ta farayi Amma Ina Farrah ta makara Dan wani zafi na tsahin hankali da taji Yana neman fasa kolwarta .to dai nayi nan 🏃🏃🏃.

Ina falo zaune nayi kuyu dukdan na samo maku karshen labarin,gyangyadi ne ya fara daukata ta can naji farrah ta buga wani Kara da saida ya saka na zabura zan koma dakin Dan ganin Wai me wannan bawan Allah yake yimata,Amma kashhhh sai naji kofar a rufe da key,dole na dawo inata jimami 😔.



A daidai lokacin da Farrah ta fasa wannan Karan,yayi daidai da lokacin da Bilkisu ta farka da wani azababen Kara Wanda duka gidan saida ya amsa,innar ta ce ta fito daga dakinta ta leka dakin na Bilkisu,ganin tana ta faman fisge-fisge ga wasu sururtai tana yi marassa dadin ji,cikin sauri Inna ta karasa gareta tana riketa ganin yadda take kokarin rugawa.tana tambayar ta lafiya?meya sameta?.Amma Bilkisu ta kasa bayar da Amsa.Addu'a ta fara tofa mata, kafin ta dauko sauran magungunan ta Bata wasu ta shafa mata na shafawa kafin tadan lafa tana juya kanta.A haka Inna suka Kai har asuba ba tare da sun runtsaba da zummar zuwa da safe zata kaita *ISLAMIC DAN FODIO* *CENTER* a yimata rukiya duk a iya saninta da Bilkisu Bata da aljanu,sai dai ko daga bisani abin ya sameta.



Yasir kuwa da baisan duniyar da yake ba,ba tausayi ballantana saurare yake gudanar da aikinshi Dan akalla yaje makka sau goma Yana dawowa a tsakiyar Daren nan (🤣🤣Ina Aunty saudat kinji har garinku yaje saboda tsabar lulawa da yayi 🙈🙊).

Farrah kuwa baiwar Allah tun tana sanin me yakeyi garta fita hayyacinta tana suma tana farfadowa,a karshe tayi wata Kara tare da sakewa gaba daya jijiyoyin ta suka daina halbawa.sai a sannan ya fahimci aika aikar da yake yi,har gwamma lokacin data cije shi,da lokacin da take faman Kiran Momy,Aunty Husnah,Aunty safiyya,harda su Dady ta rinka Kira Amma baiji tausayinta ba.aiko tuni ya rude ganin bata motsi mirginawa yayi gefe daya Yana shafa fuskarta data jike sharkaf da zufa saboda wahala da ta sha.

Kayanshi ya mayar kafin ya kunna light din dakin a zuwa lokacin har an fara Kiran sallar Asubah.ruwa ya dauko masu sanyi a freadge ya zuba a tafin hannun shi Yana shafa mata a fuska,shairu yaji haka yayi ta shafa mata ruwannan kafin yaga taja wani dogon numfashi tana fisge-fisge,da sauri ya aje gorar ruwan Kan site drower kafin ya riketa, wani wahalallen kuka ta saki tana jijjuya kanta saboda azaba..sun Jima a haka kafin ya kwantar da ita bisa kirjinshi.har zuwa lokacin batayi magana ba sai ajiyar zuciya da take faman saukewa.

Suna a haka aka fara salla a masallatai,kafin ya samu a hankali ya kwantar da ita saboda yaga tayi bacci.bargo ya rufa mata jin yadda jikinta ya dau zafi sosai yaji tausayinta ya kamashi.toilet ya wuce,wanka yayi ya gyara jikin shi tare da dauro alwala,Yana fitowa ya canza kayanshi tare da fesa turare ya shimfida abin sallah Dan Yana gudun ya fita Kuma ta tashi.bayan ya idar da sallah Yana lazimi wayar shi ya dauko Yana kunnata ganin har shida saura yasa ya fara Kiran wayar Aunty Husnah tana ta ringing ba'a daga ba harta katse,Kara Kira yayi a karo na biyu kafin tayi picking.
Sallama yayi kafin ya gaisheta jin muryar shi wani iri yasa tace "Magaji lafiya meya sameka naji muryar ka ta canza ?"

"Ammh Aunty Dan Allah ki taimake ni kizo,wallahi Farrah ce ba lafiya yanzu haka zazzabi ne a jikinta,Dan Allah ki zo please"ya karasa maganar kamar zaiyi kuka ..


Tana jin haka a ranta ta ce "nasan a rina,wannan rawar Kan da yake yi Allah yasa bai kassara masu yarinya ba"a zahiri ko sai ta ce "to Bari ganinan zuwa yanzu zan biya na dauko wata freind dina Doctor ce sai muzo tare ta dubata ko"

Da "to"ya amsa kafin ya katse kiran ganin Farrah tana faman juyawa,daga gani kasan baccin wahala ne take yi.

Saman gadon ya haura ganin yadda take yakune fiska.A hanakli ya Kura mata manyan eyes balls din shi Yana kallan kyakkyawar fuskarta Yana Kara sanya mata albarka Dan yau sakayau yake jinshi kamar Wanda aka yiwa sabuwar hallitta ,ga wani Nishadi da yake ji na musamman da yake jin Yana ratsa illahirin bargon jikinshi.karan mota yaji alamar sun shigo Dan haka yayi suarin fita.

Kofar falon ya bude ganin su a tsaye ita da Doctor din yasa ya gaishe da su, Doctor din kadai ta amsa tana janshi Dan ita ko Aunty Husnah cikin falon ta nufa suka bita a baya.har dakin suka shiga kafin ya dawo zuwa daya daga cikin bedroom din yayi konciyar shi Yana jin wani farinciki Yana zagaya kolwar shi.da haka bacci ya dauke shi da farar jallabiya a jikin shi.


Aunty Husnah ta haura saman gadon tana aje hijaf din jikinta a Kan mirror kafin ta fara yaye bargon daya rufamata.da sauri ta mayar tana kawar da kanta dan jin yadda gabanta yayi wata faduwa.a sanyaye ta ce "na shaiga ukku ni Asma'u meye haka Magaji ya aikata?wannan rashin tausayi har Ina?tana saukowa ta nufi toilet Dan hada ruwan zafi . Doctor Hadiza ce ta matsa kusa da Farrah tana cewa "yaran *ZAMANINMU* ai rawar kansu tayi yawa shiyasa ake yawan samun matsala,Dan idan Kinga irin haka to gaddama tayi mai,Kuma kinsan karfinsu ba daya ne ba"taba jikinta tayi jin yadda yadau safi yasa taji yarinyar ta bata tausayi Dan haka ba tare da bata lokaci ba ta fara bata taimakon gaggawa.bayan Aunty Husnah ta gama hada ruwan cak Doctor ta kudindine ta cikin towel ta nufi toilet da ita,har a sannan Farrah bata San inda kanta yake ba saida taji an dauketa duk a tinaninta shi ne,Dan haka ta saka kuka tana magiyar ya kyaleta itakam yau mutuwa zata yi.saida ta jita cikin ruwan zafi kafin taji Doctor'n tana cewa "yi hakuri kin ji insha Allah zaki ji sauki"kafin tayi saurin bude idonta tana ganin sune Aiko ta Kara karfin kukanta,tana cewa "Dan Allah Aunty ki tafi dani wallahi ya bashi da Imani kashe ni zaiyi,wallahi nidai bazan zauna da shiba mugu ne,cutata yake yi"sai hawaye cur cur daga idonta.



"Me yayi maki?"cewar Aunty Husnah

Harta bude baki zatayi magana sai Kuma tayi shiru tana cije lebanta na kasa cin ta fara jin sauki.sai ajiyar zuciya take saukewa.

Sun dade Doctor Hadiza tana ta canza mata wasu ruwan kafin suka barta ta gyara jikin ta tazo tayi sallah Dan tuni karfe bakwai ta kusa.


Tana wanka tana zubar da hawaye da haka ta karasa,wani ruwan ta gani masu kayan kanshi a ciki tayi amfani da su kafin ta daura wani towel din ta fara kokarin fitowa.

Bango take bi a hankali tana runtse idonta harta samu ta fito,a lokacin Doctor Hadiza ce kadai a dakin ita Aunty Husnah tana kitchen Dan hada masu breakfast.

Wata doguwar riga ta ciro mata mai taushi da hijaf ta saka abin busar da Kai ta sanya mata gashinta ya bushe,kasancewar ba kitso a kanta.da haka ta dorata bisa abin sallah tana janta "gaskiya kedai Farrah raguwa ce,da haka zamu zo amsar haihuwar kina langwabewa?"ita dai batace komai ba,ta fara sallar ta a zaune.


Bayan ta idar Aunty Husnah ta kawo mata kayan Kari kasancewar akwai komai na bukata a gidan shiayasa tayi saurin karasawa,a lokacin karfe tara har tayi.

Doctor Hadiza ganin Bata ga Yasir ba yasa tayi tinanin ko ya fita Dan haka ta ba Baba mai gadi takadda da kudi ya amso masu magani dukda da Dan nisa.


Kafin a kawo maganin ta hada mata tea mai kauri da Arish sai farfesun kayan ciki da yaji tarugu da kayan kanshi.kadan ta ci tana ta faman zubar da hawaye.
"Aunty Husnah ta ce "yarinya gwamma ki zage ki ci Dan anjima da dare ma Yana iya karawa"tana dariya.

Aiki Farrah tana jin haka ta Kara rudewa tana ta karkarwa da hawaye."Dan Allah aunty ki tafi dani wallahi tsoranshi nake ji,bazan iya ba mutuwa zanyi".tana Kara volume din kukanta.


Saida Doctor Hadiza tayi ta kontar mata bata hakuri tana kontar mata da hankali kafin ta Dan ji ta samu nutsuwa,Dan Doctor Hadiza ta ce zata fadamai kada ya Kara.

Bayan an kawo maganin suka Bata tasha,kafin suka Kara gyara gadon ta konta bacci.

Doctor Hadiza tafiya tayi bayan Aunty Husnah ta anshi nomber account dinta,Dan sallamar ta tana ta yimata godiya.


Bayan Aunty Husnah ta dawo falon ta fara neman inda Yasir ya konta.can daki na biyun karshe ta ganshi Yana ta sharar baccin shi hankali konce.kyaleshi tayi ta wuce gidanta da zummar idan ya tashi ta kirashi waya tayimai bayanin yadda zai Bata maganin,sai yaje da kanshi ya anso masu abincin rana.bayan ta tare mai nafef taji Karan wayarta dubawa tayi ganin nomber Aunty safiyya yasa tayi saurin dagawa tana gaisheta.

"Amm Husnah Dan Allah kice ki duba kanwar ki kiga ko lafiya,Dan wallahi tunda jiya da suka bar nan hankalina ya kasa konciya".

Murmushi Aunty Husnah tayi kafin ta ce "Aunty ki kontar da hankalin ki yanzu haka daga can nake tunda safe ya kirani sai naje da wata kawata ta dubata, insha Allah ba matsala,komai ya daidaita".


Ajiyar zuciya ta sauke tana ji a ranta Farrah an girma yanzu kila ta Kara hankali da wayo kafin ta ce"to Husnah na gode Allah ya Basu zaman lafi ki rinkayimata nasiha da nunamata yadda rayuwa take,Dan Wallahi Ina tausayin Baby duk randa Magaji zai Kara aure zata gane kuranta".


"A a Aunty insha Allah ma bazai Kara ba saboda Yana santa,itama Kuma zata yimai biyayya insha Allah"tana dariya danjin yadda Auntyn tasu tace.


"To Allah yasa ta gyara"cewar Aunty safiyya.

Aunty Husnah ta amsa da "Amin ya Allah"kafin sukayi sallama ta katse call din.daidai mai nafef din ya iso gate din gidanta,sallamar shi tayi kafin ta nufi cikin gidan tana murmushi Dan tadan drama Bata kareba idan Farrah ta farka taga sun taho,to shikam ya bani wuni zaiyi faman rarrashinta.

🅿️46
      📝Akalla sun Kai karfe shadaya suna bacci,sai sannan Yasir ya juya da sauri ya bude idonshi Yana kallan agogo, ganin yadda lokaci ya shude ya makara shi yaushe rabon da yayi bacci mai dadi irin na yau,badan ya soba ya tashi da addu'a a bakinshi kafin ya nufi dakinta.yana shiga yaga baccinta take yi,Daman yayi tinanin Aunty Husnah sun wuce.wayar shi ya dauka ya dawo falo Yana Kiranta.bayan ta daga ya ce "Aunty ashe har kun tafi?"Yana Dan lumshe idon shi alamar har yanzu baccin bai sake shi ba.

"To ai Magaji dole muke tafiya,kuna ta ramuwar bacci mun zauna muna gadinku?kaga ni anjima kadan idan na karasa girki kazo ka amsar maku,Kuma bayan ta farka ta Kara shiga ruwan zafi,Kuma Dan Allah kana jina?"


"Eh Aunty Ina jinki" cewar Yasir da yake kokarin konciya bisa kujera.

"Dan Allah Magaji ka kyale yarinyar nan,kadan daga mata kafa Kona sati biyu ne,kaga yanzu wallahi idan ta tashi nasan zaka Sha rigima dan cewa tayi saina taho da ita bazata zauna ba kaji ko?"tana tona miyar data Dora akan gas.



Ajiyar zuciya ya sauke can kasan makoshi ya ce "Haba Aunty two weeks ai yayi yawa,ni Baku tausayin dan kanin nan naku,kunfi jinta a kaina,to ba damuwa zanga idan zan iya jurewa idan ko bana iyawa to,zan dan........"
Sai Kuma yayi shiru Yana rufe bakin shi da tafin hannu.


"A a Magaji gwamma ka karasa fadi,acan baya ma baka jin kunyata ballanta na yanzu da kake jin kanka a sama ko?to nidai da Allah na hadaka,idan Kuma kaki wallahi zan Kira Abbah na cemai Farrah bata da lafiya Amma zan kawota gidana harta warke kafin na mayar da ita,kasan cewar yarda zaiyi ko?"


Dariya ya danyi kafin ya ce "Haba uwar dakina kada miyi haka da ke,wallahi wasa nake yi saita warke kafin Dan Nima tsoranta nake ji,ita data kusa sumar da ni"Yana Kara sautin dariyar shi jin Aunty Husnah tayi saurin katse kiran.kwanciyar shi yayi anan Yana faman doka murmushi.














        *KATSINAN DUKKO* 


Da misalin karfe daya saura Dady, Abbah,sai kawu Yakubu da ya iso daga dutsamma Suka nufi gidan mahaifin Bahijja dan nemawa Jabir auren Yar shi.a wannan ranar su Dady sunga karamci da mutunta Baki,sosai iyayenta sukayi farinciki saboda ranar weekend ne,kanin mahaifinta shi ya anshi kayan auren inda aka saka wata biyu masu zuwa,kafin azimi kenan .a lokacin Bahijja tana gidan matar yayanta dan haka acan Jabir ya risketa suna fira,ya juna farinciki shi kwarai da gaske.
"Wai ni ya Jabir ina labarin kawata"tana kallanshi.
"Hmmm ke zan tambaya dan tun ranar da suka tafi raban da muyi waya da brother,Amma Bari na Kira shi sai ya bata wayan tinda kina son magana da ita"Yana laluben wayar shi.tana ta ringing ba'a daga ba.

Shiko Yasir a lokacin Yana ta fama da rigimar Beby dan sau lokacin ta farka tana ta yimai kuka da complen din bayanta da kafafunta ciwo suke yi,to so yake tayi wanka ya bata magani,Amma ta kiya.suna haka yaji Karan wayar.mika hannu yayi ya daukota sai yaga sunan Jabir picking yayi ya sakata a speaker Yana magana.

Tana jin muryar Yaya Jabir sai tayi lamo a jikin shi tana zubda kolla.

"Yaya ya gida"cewar Jabir da yake kallon Bahijja ganin yadda take ta faman doka murmushi.


"Lafiya Lau ya yan gidan,da fatan duk kuna lafiya?"

"Lafiya Lau,munga kun manta da mu tunda kuka tafi,ko Kira a waya bakwayi sai Sweety take tambaya ta Ina Beby shiyasa na Kira Dan su gaisa"Yana mikawa Bahijja wayar.


"Ok bata wayar"cewar Yasir Yana dorawa Farrah wayar bisa kunnenta.

Ai Farrah najin haka kawai sata fashe da kuka .

Cikin rudewa Bahijja ta fara tambayar ta "lafiya kawata meya same ki haka kike kuka"?tana kallan Jabir da shima ita yake kallo.


Yasir dauke wayar yayi Yana cewa "sorry Bahijja bata da lafiya ne,Amma anjima San kawo mata new phone zata kiraki da wayanta"


"To Yaya Allah ya bata lafiya nagode"kafin ta katse wayar.


Ajiyar zuciya ta sauke tana kallan Jabir ta ce "Wai sweety ka ji bata da lafiya,kuka take yima,wallahi hankalina harya tashi kada ya rinka cutar da ita"cewar Bahijja cikin sanyin murya.


Jabir kallanta yayi ya ce "A a sweety kada kiyi wannan tinanin,nasan brother Yana santa saidai in rigimarta ce ta motsa,Dan nasan halin Beby kamar Yar goye haka take idan ta kama rigima daman shi kadai take tsoro,yanzu ko tunda sun zama daya ai sai dai yayi hakuri"Yana kashe mata ido daya.

Sinnr kanta tayi tana murmushi,da haka suka Dan taba hira ya wuce gida ita Kuma ta shiga gidan yayanta.


Yasir kuwa Yana kashe wayar ya sungumeta zuwa tiolet Dan ya fahimci bata mai time zata yi ga lokacin azahar har ya karato,Dan haka kawai ya sakata cikin bathroom kafin shima ya shiga.sosai take kokarin ya saketa Amma ya kiya saida ya tabbatar ta gasu kafin ya cireta yasaka tayi alwala ya mayar da ita bisa gado,shima ya koma tiolet din Dan Kara yin wani wankan.



Bilkisu kuwa tunda suka dawo daga Danfodio tana konce tana faman kuka da nadama,Dan da akayi mata ruqiyya aljanin kin fita yayi saboda ya ce anyi da ita Idan baiyi nasara ba kanta zai dawo,to a karshe dai dakyar aka samu ya fice.aka Basu magunguna suka dawo gida inna tana ta yimata fada.


Bayan ya fito tana konce ya dauko mata wani leshin ta saka da hijaf shima ya shiya ya jasu suka gabatar da sallah azahar.kafin ya fita Dan anso masu abinci gidan Aunty Husnah.







Ummah ce gaban Abbah bayan sun dawo daga gidan su Bahijja ta tsuguna a gaban shi tana bashi hakuri,Amma ya kyaleta.
Sai can yace "wato sai yanzu kika hakura da Magaji ya zauna da Amina,ai wallahi kin bani kunya nayi tinanin tsare shi zakiyi ya saketa,shiyasa muka sashi ya dau matar shi suka tafi tunda ke bakyasan konciyar hankalin mu ko,ni tashi ki bani wuri ,Kuma yauma minkai kudin auren Jabiru gidan su Bahijja Diyar Alhaji murtala,shima kice Baki so kiga yadda zan Bata maki rai,Banda wulakanci kiri-kiri ki nuna bakisan yarinya idan da rabo to ya zakiyi,Dan kila yanzuma idan aka ce tana da ciki kar kiyi mamaki da hukuncin Ubangiji"haka Abbah yayi ta mata fada tana bashi hakuri,har a karshe dai ya ce ya yafemata.kafin suka daidaita.


Yasir tunda ya amso abincin ya hadomata da iccecrem mai sanyi Dan yasanta akwai San zaki.a  haka suka yini a wannan ranar Farrah tana ta zuba mai shagwaba san ranta,shiko bawan Allah duk yabi ya rude sai faman  tarairayarta yakeyi tana Kara rudashi.Amma a zuciyarta tausayin shi take ji ganin yadda yake ta rawar kafa a kanta,Dan zuwa wannan lokacin ta daina kukan.haka suka wuni har dare Yana ta tarairayarta.salloli ma duk shi ya jasu jam"i Suka yi a gida.sai Bayan sallar isha'i Aunty Safiyya ta Kira wayar shi Dan ta kasa daurewa so take yi taji muryar Autarta ta.

Cikin kunya ya daga ga Farrah duk ta kanainaye shi yadda bazai iya tashi daga gurin ba.dole ya amsa anan bayan sun gaisa take cemai ankai kudin auren Jabir, addu'a yayi masu da fatan alkhairi,sannan yace ga Beby zasu gaisa.
"A a Magaji kyaleta ni Daman kaina Kira a gaisa da naga baka shigo ka gaishe mu ba"cewar Aunty safiyya cikin jin kunya.


Cewa yayi "zan shigo gobe insha Allah"

Sauri Farrah tayi ta anshe wayar tana dariya ta ce "Aunty yanzu duk kun tsane ni,ni ko tun dazu nake cewa yakiraman ke mu gaisa"ta karasa fada tana turo bakinta.

"Ba haka neba Baby so nake yi ki nutsu kiyi hankali tukunna sai na rinka kiranki"cewar Aunty safiyya,dukda taji dadin jin yadda Diyarta ta hakura ta zauna.

"To Aunty yanzuma ai nayi hankali tunda ya jiman rauni ban fadamaku ba ko"cikin yanayin sakalci.

Da sauri Aunty safiyya ta katse wayar tana girgiza kanta a fili ta furta "Baby har haryanzu da sauranki.


Shiko Yasir amshe wayar yayi Yana saka bakin shi cikin nata yayi niyyar sai yayi fornishing dinta tinda Bata jin magana shi zata kunyata,ya kasa zuwa gidan.tayi ta faman ta kwace Amma ta kasa saida ya gaji dan kanshi kafin ya zare bakinshi daga cikin nata Yana mayar da numfashi.
Kwanciya tayi a jikin shi tana jin yadda jijiyoyin jikinta suka saki.lumshe ido tayi tana jin wani yarrrrrrrr a cikin jikinta.sun Dade a haka kafin yace "ni ki kagani duk kin tumurmushe ni,Amma sai kice Ina cutar ki"Yana leken fuskarta.

A hankali ta mirgina gefe tana Kara kudindine jikinta guri guda jin wani sanyi na ratsa ta.

A haka suka Kai har lokacin konciya sai kayan fruits daya yanko masu a tray suka sha.da zasu yi bacci sosai ta tsorata tana ta raba eyes lura da yayi da hakan yasa ya fara lallabata da cewar ba abinda zai yi mata.kafin ta yarda suka konta,Amma dukda haka ya kwakumeta cikin jikinshi ya hanata sukuni.ita ko baiwar Allah sai fargaba take yi Dan ita yanzu harga Allah tsoro yake bata.shiya fara yin bacci, saida ta tabbatar yayi nisa a baccin kafin taji hankalin ta yadan konta itama tayi bacci.

🅿️48
       📝Tinda ya shiga gidan ya fahimci akwai canji musamman a fuskar mahaifiyar tasu,yadda ta tarbeshi cikin sakin fuska sai tambayar shi take yi Ina Farrah,sai ya ce "tana gidan Momy Amma anjima zuwa da dare zan kawota ta gaisheki.taji dadin hakan sosai,anan ta gabatar mai da abinci cikin farinciki ya ci ganin Ummahn tasu ta huce daga fishin data dauka.yana cikin cin abincin Jabir Suka shigo shida little Umar.anan Suka ci gaba da hira Jabir Yana ta tsokanar shi ganin yadda yai bul gwanin kyau abinshi.kafin yayi masu sallama da zummar zuwa dare zasu dawo tare  da Farrah.akan hanya ya siyamata zogala da kuli dakakke ya tafi da su.




A wannan yammacin farisu abokin salisu yayan Bilkisu yazo gidan su,da farko kin fita tayi saida Inna ta hauta da fada kafin ta fita tana goge kolla.A zauren gidan ta riskeshi Yana ganinta yahau doka murmushi,Dan shikam ya dade Yana San Bilkisu tunda dadewa.A hankali tayi sallama bayan ya amsa ta gaisheshi.dukansu shiru sukayi na Dan wani lokaci kafin ya fara magana kamar haka "Kiyi hakuri Bilkisu idan na takura ki,zuciyata ce ta kasa daurewa akan irin son da take yimaki,Ina fatan a wannan karon zan samu karbuwa"Yana kallan yadda duk ta sauya kamar ba Bilkisun daya sani ba.

Har zuwa wannan lokacin nata tanka ba,sai ma Kara yin kasa da kanta da tayi tana wasa da karamin zoben hannunta.

Haka yayi ta zuba shi kadai,sai a karshe ta ce "na amimce,Amma ka sani ni yanzu Ina da banbanci da sauran yan'mata domin ya zama dole na fadama gaskiyar abinda ya faru da rayuwata,kada sai Bayan auren mu na fuskanci wulakanci daga gareka......"da sauri ya katseta ta hanyar cewa "Bakomai Bilkisu duk salisu yayi min bayani,insha Allah zan rikeki da amana,burina ki kasance mai hakuri da biyayyya nidai kinsan sana'ata bakai kudi ne ba ni,Amma Insha Allah mai nema Yana tare da samu".
Sosai taji dadin kalamanshi daga nan suka Kara daidaita kansu,ya sanar da ita zuwa ranar juma'a za'a manyan shi zasu zo Dan saka ranar auren su.godiya tayi mai kafin yayi mai kafin ya Miko mata wata leda,da farko kin ansa tayi saida yayi ta rokonta kafin ta amsa ta wuce cikin gida bayan tayi mai godiya shima tafiya yayi Yana Addu'ar Allah yasa ta sanadin faruwar al'amarin nan ta shiryu.
Tana shiga gidan ta nunawa Inna abinda ya kawo mata,leda daya kayan fruits ne dangin su kankana,lemo,ayaba da sauransu.dayar ledar kuwa kayan kolloliya ne dukda ba masu tsada ne ba Amma ba laifi sinyi kyau,ajewa tayi kafin ta wuce wurin kitchen Dan karasa aikin data bari.



Tinda ya shigo falon yake jin kukanta da sauri ya aje ledar zogalan Yana nufar inda take a konce.rungumota jikinsa yayi Yana cewa "A a Baby meya faru?Baki da lafiya ne?ko wani abu yake maki ciwo?duk bata bashi amsa ba sai cewa tayi "ni Ina zogalan,yunwa nake ji idan ban ci ba mutuwa zanyi"tana Kara kontar da kanta bisa kirjinshi.
Dariya takusan kufcemai Amma sai yayi murmushi Yana share mata hawaye yace "Kinga yi hakuri gashi na kawo ko"Yana tayar da ita zaune.duk abinda take yi Momy tana kallansu sai taji sun birgeta dukda ta fahimci Farrah tana Dan bashi wahala saboda yadda baya iya boye soyayyar da yake yimata a gaban kowa nunawa yake yi.
Da kanshi ya dauko tumatur da albasa ya jika kulin ya hada da mai ster ya kwada mata.sai a lokacin ya lura da Momy a wurin,sosai kunya ta rufe shi,Amma ba yadda zaiyi kinci tayi dole ya fara bata a baki ganin yadda ta birkice,Momy tashi tayi ta basu wuri ta wuce bedroom Dan jin an fara Kiran sallar magriba.
Ai zogala kaman jira take ta karasa cinyewa ta fara kokarin amai,ganin haka Yasir ya rude Yana ta faman girgizata.jin haka yasa Momy har tayi alwala ta fito da sauri Dan ganin meke faruwa.
Taimaka mata tayi zuwa toilet ana tayi ta amai saida ta amayar da zogalan nan tas kafin taji saukin tashin zuciyar da yake addabar ta.ji tayi jikinta yayi sanyi lakos Bata da kuzari.alwala ta dauro shiko ya fita zuwa masallaci, anan dakin Momy tayi sallah a zaune tana ta faman zubar da kollah.a bisa abin sallah ta konta.kafin kace meye wannan bacci ya dauketa ga wani zazzafan zazzabi daya sauko mata lokaci daya.

Shiru Momy tayi tana nazarinta,Dan da Alama Farrah zata yi laulayin ciki irin na Mamanta mai wahalarwa,Dan ita Aunty safiyya a lokacin har saida Alhaji Ubah ya kawota katsina aka Dan kula da ita kafin bayan taji sauki ta koma.



Bayan ya dawo tare suka shigo da doctor Umar Dan gani yayi gwamma ya Kira shi a dubata,suda Suka zo biki Kuma rashin lafiya ta samu.anan falo doctor Umar ya zauna shi Kuma ya shiga ciki Dan fito da ita.bacci ya taras tana yi sai Momy dake kuda da ita.
Sanar da ita yayi kafin ya fara tayar da ita.dakyar ta bude idonta Dan tana jin baccin sosai,hannunta ya kamo suka fito.ana doctor ya dubata Yana yimasu albishi da tana dauke da juna biyu na sati biyar da kwana hudu.Yasir tsabar farinciki baisan time din daya rungimi doctor ba .ita ko Farrah tsoro ne ya kamata ganin yadda yau kadai Amma duk ta fita hayyacinta,to Ina ga nan gaba".
Da haka dai ya rubuta masu magani kafin ya wuce.shiko kusa da ita ya dawo ganin yadda tayi shiru.rungumota yayi Yana kissing din wuyanta ya ce "Baby Naga bakiyi murna ba,ko bakison yaron da zamu haifa?".

Shiru ta mai tana kasa da kanta.ji yayi jikinshi yayi sanyi Dan haka ya tashi Yana cewa anjima ki shirya muje zaki gaishe da Ummah Dan dazu tana tambayarki,yanzu zan kawo maganin sai kiyi kokari ki sha.kafin ya fice ba tare daya Kara kallanta ba.ajiyar zuciya ta sauke tana shigewa cikin kujerar jin wani sanyi Yana ratsata a zuciyarta tana jin wani farinciki Wai ita ce zata haihu?hannunta ta dora tana shafa shafaffen cikinta da kamar ba komai a ciki.wayarta ta janyo tana Kiran nomber shi.yana gani ya kyale da kamar nazai daga ba,Amma sai yayi picking ji yayi cikin sanyin muryar ta ce "Dan Allah ka siyoman agwalima"tana Jan numfashi.
A zuciyar shi yace "yau ni dai na gode Allah"Amma a fili amsawa yayi da "to"kafin ta katse kiran tana gunguni, kasa-kasa take cewa "yanzu ni shikenan a hanaka bacci,shiko nan zaiyi ta baccin shi niko ko ohoo sai dai abar mutum da wahala"mtssss tana Jan siririn tsaki,mikewa tayi jin lokacin sallah yayi ta shige dakin Momy.


A ranar dakyar ta yarda suka nufi gidan Abbah bayan tasha magani.Ummah tana ganinsu ta hau murna ba kamar data fahimci cikin dake jikin Farrah tana ta kaffa-kaffa da ita har wuraren kafe goma lokacin Abbah ya dawo Suka je suka gaisheshi shima yaji dadin ganin su haka yayi ta saka masu albarka.Amma har suka tafi Bata ga Jabir ba.a bayan sun dawo ya wuce tsohon part din shi ita ko tayi cikin gidan tana jin jiri Yana daukar ta.a gyare tsaf riski part din Dan haka ya shige wanka.


Farrah ko abinci ta dan ci kadan sai agwalumar daya kawo mata take ta faman Sha tana sude Baki Momy duk tana kallanta sai Nusaiba da take faman yi mata dariya.

Sai wuraren goma da rabi Momy ta ce "Farrah tashi ki tafi wurin mijinki Dan kayanki suna can tun dazu nasa aka kaimaki"

Sosai ta gwalo ido tana kallan Momy.

"Ko Baki ji Mena fada ba" cewar Momy tana mikewa  "Dan Nima wurin mai gidana zan wuce,Nusaiba ko tuni ta wuce dakin Farrah na da Wanda yanzu ya zama mallakinta.
Tana kallo har Momy ta fita kafin itama cikin sanyin jiki ta wuce dakin mai gidan nata tana faman gunguni da mita.



Bata ganshi a Falo ba Dan haka derect ta wuce dakin shi.a wata yar kujera ta ganshi Yana duba system din shi.a hankali ta karasa Yana kallonta ta nufi toilet wanka tayi ta saka kayan bacci ta fesa turaren oud mai kanshi da humra irin ta Yan maiduguri.gadon ta haye tayi konciyar ta tana shakar kanshin jikinta.a hankali ta rinka jin yanayin ta Yana sauyawa,wasu igiyoyo suna yimata yawo a cikin jikinta.kafin kace me tuni ta rude ita kanta tayi mamakin kanta,meyake neman samunta ne? Sosai yanayinta ya sauya ta fara mirginawa.jin motsinta ya sakashi juyowa Yana kallon yadda take birgima.rufe system din yayi Yana rage hasken dakin kafin ya haura inda take.dagota yayi Yana kallan yadda take zillewa."Baby meya same ki ne?ai tana jin muryar shi da irin rungumar da yayi mata ta Kara kankame shi bata Bari ya Kara magana ba tayi saurin saka bakin ta cikin nashi.hakan da yaji ya gane matsalar ta,aiki Daman mai neman kuka aka jefe shi da kashin awaki ai sai yi.yaji dadi sosai Dan Daman tunanin tunkarar ta yake yi.da haka da haka dai nikam nayi nan 🏃🏃🏃.




   


    ******************


Washe gari haka Farrah ta wuni a part din Momy daga tace wannan take so sai tace wancan take so,haka akayi ta fama da ita ko gidan yinin biki Basu jeba kasancewar sai da dare Aunty Husnah ta iso daga Abuja.ganin Farrah da tayi a haka yasa tayi ta yimata dariya tana janta itako sai kuka take yi tana jin haushi har zuwa lokacin da Yasir ya dawo daga amso mata dinkinta daga gidan su Bahijja.fita ya karayi bayan sun gaisa da Aunty Husnah.



Cikin gimtse dariyarta ta kalli Farrah cikin salon zolaya ta ce "ooooh ni Farrah ashe kice zaki haifa mai yaran,keda kika ce saidai ya nemo wadda zata haifa mai badai keba,to ya'akayi ciki ya fito haka"tana Kara sautin dariyar ta.

Farrah kuka kawai ta saka tana sharbewa,Momy girgiza kanta tayi tana murmushi.

Ita ko Aunty Husnah cikin rarrashin Yar kanwarta ta ta dafa kafadarta tana cewa "Kinga yi hakuri kinji Yar kanwarta,yanzu abinda za'ayi anjima idan ya dawo zan anshi kudi a hada maki kayan gyara kafin ku koma Dan wannan mijin naki zafin Kaine da shi"


"Ni ba wannan nake so ba,kayan lefena nake so ya hadaman idan ko ba hakaba to bazan koma ba,sai dai ya tafi shi kadai"cewar farrah tana goge hawayen fuskar ta.


Sosai Aunty Husnah take dariya gaskiya Farrah rigimar ki tayi yawa,wato da kuna cin amarci Baki tina da baimaki lefe ba sai yanzu da ciki ya samu,to zamu saka yayi maki Dan na Fahimci cikin ne baki so ko?to insha Allah sai kin haife Mana kayanmu,tunda Baki so a karu da ke sai dai kici ki konta ko?"tana gimtse dariyarta Dan ganin tana neman Kara saka mata wani kukan.

Bayan ya dawo Aunty Husnah tayi mai bayanin abinda Yar rigimar tashi ta ce.murmushi yayi ya ce "to Aunty za'ayi komai,Amma sai an karasa bikin su Jabir tukkunna".

A wannan matsayar Suka bar zancen.

Sai zuwa dare har sun konta dakin Nusaiba ita da Aunty Husnah sai little Hibbah.can wuraren karfe shabiyu saura ta kasa bacci ta tashi.wayarta ta janyo ta tura mai texs kamar haka :Dan Allah kazo mu tafi wallahi na kasa bacci.ta tura mai.ba'a dau tsawon lokaci ba taga reply din shi "ki fito mayn falo ganinan zuwa"ai tana gani ta mike cikin tsuma ta saka silifas da hijaf saman kayan baccin ta.cikin sanda ta fice daga dakin,harta fice daga dakin Aunty Husnah tana kallanta.saida taga ficewarta tayi murmushi tana Kara gyara konciyar ta.


A tsaye ta riske shi Dan haka bai tsaya wata wata ba ya sungumeta ta Suka shige part dinshi.



Tunda asuba Farrah ta nufo dakin Nusaiba,a zaune ta riski Aunty Husnah bisa abin sallah.cikin sanyi ta wuce falo ita ko batace da ita komai ba.


Bayan itama tayi sallah,cikin turo baki ta ce "Aunty yunwa nake ji"tana kallanta.


"To Farrah ai dole kiji yunwa,irin haka tana" tana dariya.

Batace komai ba saboda wata azababbiyar yunwa da taji tana tukota,ta nufi kiccin da sauri ta dafa imdomie anan cikin tukunyar ta cinye tasha ruwan sanyi kafinta koma bacci.


A ranar ma haka suka wuni bayan sun dawo daga gidan bikin.kuma a ranar aka saka auren Bilkisu da faresu nan da wata daya,Dan yace bayaso a saka da yawa,kawai duk abinda Allah ya hore akai mata.


Itama Aunty safiyya a ranar ta shigo garin,sai gidan su Jabir daya cika da Baki na nesa Dana cikin gari,itama Aunty Fatima tazo daga kaduna dangi duk an hadu ga Yan Dutsinma iyalan kawu Yakubu da sauran yan'uwa.


Sosai Aunty safiyya taji dadi ganin yadda Farrah ta kontar da hankalin ta,ga labari mai dadi da taji na cikin da take dauke da shi.hakan yasa ta sake mata da dare sukayi ta fira a dakin Nusaiba.saida lokacin konciya yayi Farrah harta konta Aunty Husnah ta tada ta saboda yawan Kira da Yasir ya dameta da shi.ba yadda ta iya haka ta nufi part dinshi tana rangaji irin na masu jin bacci.





A washe gari dubban mutane Suka sheda daurin auren Jabir da Bahijja akan sadaki mafi daraja.sosai Yan uwa aka hadu suna ta Jan Farrah ganin yadda take ta longabewa irin na masu laulayin karamin cikin.da haka dai aka yi yinin biki duk sunsha ankon leshin mai tsada.da dare aka Kai amarya gidanta dake can ckin GRA gaba da su Ummah.kafin su tafi Farrah tana ta yiwa Bahijja dariyar mugunta ganin yadda duk take a tsorace Dan haka tayi ta Kara tsorata ta,har saida Aunty Husnah ta fita da ita daga dakin ita da Matar yayan Bahijjah.kafin aka ce Ya Yasir Yana kiranta a wajen gate din gida.a mota ta same shi suka tafi gida.tana ta mitar ita wallahi duk an saka ta gaji ga yunwa tana ji.a hanya suna tafiya ta hango mai siyar da kwakwar manja,Aiko da sauri ta saka ya tsaya tace "ya Dan Allah siyaman kwakwar can gobe tunda safe ita nake son ci".

Yasir ba yadda ya iya dole ya fita kwano guda ya siyo mata,kafin suka wuce.sauran yan bikin ko sai bayan sun Jima da dawowa kafin suma Suka karaso.




 *Wai fns Ina labarin* *khairiyya? 🤔to ku biyo* *ni dan jin inda take* .

🅿️49
       📝A washe garin biki ranar lahadi ne sauran baki suka fara komawa gidajen su musamman na nesa.sai Yan tsirari Suka yi saura.Aunty Fatima ta wuce kaduna,sai Aunty safiyya itama a ranar ta juya Zuwa Abuja,inda Yan Dutsinma suma duk a ranar suka tafi.ya rage daga Aunty Husnah sai su Farrah kadai .



Khairiyya ce a wani babban Hotel dake cikin garin Abuja,durkushe take gaban wani Alhaji mai tukeken ciki kamar Wanda zai haihu.tun tana fitar da sautin kukan ta a hankali har yakai tanayi da karfi.cikin jin haushi Alhajin nan ya nufo kanta kamar zai duketa nunata yayi da yatsa Yana cewa "na ce kizo ki tattare kayanki kibar sakin nan,Amma kinzo kin isheni da kukan banza,ada da kina biyoni baki taba tinanin wannan ranar zata iya zuwa ba ko?to kinyi kuskere khairi duk Wanda ya biyewa wannan *ZAMANIN* namu to tabbasa zaiga sharrin *ZAMANINMU* na bibiyar shi gwargwadon abinda mutum ya aikata,a yanzu alfarma daya zan iya yimaki zan tura maki one millions a account dinki Dan ki rinka siyan magani da su".

Nan take ta Kara rushewa da matsanancin kuka,cikin Kumar zuciya da tarin nadama ta bude bakinta dakyar muryarta take fita saboda tsabar tashin hankali ta fara magana "ka cuceni!ka yaudare ni,yanzu shikenan ka kasheman rayuwa!sai Allah ya saka man"tana mikewa ta nufi inda trolyn kayanta take.

Sosai Alhaji lawi ya fashe da dariya cikin karaji ya ce "wallahi karya kike yi khairi ke kika cuci kanki,domin kin biyewa San zuciyar ki da kwadayin abin hannuna,Kinga ko dole kiyi kuka da kanki ni kaina wannan cutar gogamin akayi a sanadin bin matan banza, Kinga ko Danna gogawa irinku masu kwadayi da tsallake maganar iyayensu ai ba laifi ne ba,Dan haka ki gaggauta fita daga dakin nan ga wata yarinya ta nan zata zo yanzu,Amma duk lokacin da kike da bukatar taimako to ki nemeni zan taimaka maki,nagode da shayar da ni da kikayi"Yana juyawa zuwa toilet din cikin dakin.


Da haka khairiyya taja akwatin kayanta ta fita tana sharar kollah.(khairiyya tun Bayan ta raka Farrah gurin boka gagara ta hadu da Alhaji lawi inda ya dauketa Suka tafi Abuja suna sheke ayar su,inda tayiwa mamarta karyar ta koma makaranta da zama,to shi ne a Yan kwanakin nan ta fara rashin lafiya zuwansu asibiti Bayan Yan gwaje-gwaje doctor ya tabbatar masu da tana dauke da cuta mai karya garkuwar jiki wato HIV,to wannan shi ne yayi sanadin rabuwarta da Alhaji lawi yanzu dai ta nufi katsina Dan komawa gidan iyayenta domin kuwa ta hadu da sharrin *ZAMANINMU* mai koyar da darasin rayuwar zaman duniya)Allah ya Kara tsare mu yasa muyi kyakkyawan karshe.


A haka da wannan safiyar ta wuce tasha inda tana shiga motar ta ciga Suka kama hanyar katsina,har zuwa wannan lokacin kuka take yi,har Yan cikin motar suka gaji suna tambayar ta ko lafiya Bata basu amsa ba Kuma bata daina kukan ba.da haka dai suka ci gaba da tafiya.









       ------------------------

A washe garin litinin Farrah dakyar ta yarda suka tafi Dan da farko cewa tayi ita dai ba zata koma ba ya barta anan,saboda laulayin cikin ya matsa mata sosai komai taci saita amayar da shi.akan dole ta bishi Dan yadda Dady ya bude mata wuta da fada.tun akan hanya take bacci har suka kusa shiga Abuja kafin ta farka,Aiko tana tashi ta saka rigimar saidai ya kaita gidan Aunty Safiyya,Yasir ba yadda ya iya ganin yadda duk ta galabaita  yasa ya wuce.sai kwatsam Aunty safiyya ta gansu batayi mamaki ba ganin yadda Diyarta ta duk ta mugun galabaita.anan ya barta shi Kuma ta nufi gidan su.
Itama Aunty Husnah daga Barhim darect Aubja ta wuce gidan mijinta.



A bangaren khairiyya kuwa bayan taje gida Mamanta ta tsareta saida ta sanar da ita abinda ke faruwa,anan take ta yanke jiki ta fadi sai daukanta akayi ba rai.hakan yasa Bayan rasuwar ta dawo gidan yayanta dan Hausawa nacewa "hannunka baya rubewa ka yanke shi ka Yar"Dan haka yau da dadi gobe ba dadi da haka take hakuri,a karshe dai ta roki Alhaji lawi da ta taimaka ya aureta,ba tare da bata lokaci ba ya amince Dan shi Daman Yana San Khairiyya ba tare da bata lokaci ba ya biya komai cikin sati biyu yayanta yayi wakilci aka daura auren su inda ya kaita Abuja cikin iyalinshi ,haka uwar gidan da yaranta suke faman gana mata wahala danma saukinta daya shi Alhajin Yana santa Kuma Yana kokarin kyautata mata,ga gida har gida Kuma ga kayan alatu Amma haka khairiyya take rayuwa cikin zillimin cutar da suke dauke da ita har uwar gidan.shiyasa ta kafa mata Karan tsana tana ganin su suka gogawa mijinta cuta.(Kaico khairiyya har kin bani tausayi  😭).





Bayan sati biyu aka daura auren Bilkisu da farisu akan sadaki naira dubu ashirin,inda Bayan yinin biki aka kaita gidanta dake unguwar filin samji,Dan madaidaicin gidane mai dauke da kananun dakuna guda biyu sai toilet a gefe da Kan karamin kitchen,ba laifi yayanta salisu sinyi kokari Dan ba laife aka yimata na kudi suka amsa sai ya hada da abinda yake hannunshi aka yimata kayan daki irin namu na talakkawa.ganin inda aka kaita yasa jikin Bilkisu karayin sanyi tabbas sai yanzu ta Kara tabbatar da cewa duniya bata da tabbas duk Wanda ya biyewa rudin *ZAMANI* to tabbas sharrin *ZAMANINMU* zaiyi ta bibiyarsa.da haka dai ta anshi bakunci mai gidanta wafo farisu inda tayi Dana sani a wannan Daren yafi cikin kondo,Dan sai yanzu ta gane mahimmancin rike mutunci,Amma ita gashi ta zubar da nata a titi yanzu shikenan Bata da wata sauran daraja kenan a idonshi?haka dai tun tana jin nauyin shi harta sake ganin yadda ko a fuska bai nuna mata ba, ballanta ya goranta mata.






       *BAYAN SATI BIYU* 


tunda Farrah ta koma gidan Aunty Safiyya take shan fama,cikin ikon Allah yanzu da sauki,shiko Yasir duk lokacin da yaje ganinta sai yayi ta faman yimata Allah annabi tazo su tafi shifa ya gaji da rashinta Amma sai ta kiya,kusan ko yaushe Rai bace yake tafiya.dan haka wani zuwa da yayi ya sanarwa da Aunty Husnah abinda ke faruwa,sosai tayi ta fada kan cewar ya hada kayan lefen aje akai mata idan taga ankai ai dole ta dawo.haka kuwa akayi shida Aunty Husnah Suka je wani katon shago Suka hadi lefe na gani na fada inda aka yi akwatina guda shabiyu da sauran tarkacen,haka Aunty Husnah ta Kara yimata tsarabar kayan gyara.



A randa suka Kai ko Aunty safiyya tana gani ta rufe Farrah da fadan rashin wayonta,tana cewa "wallahi Farrah Baki da wayo yadda kika samu a wannan *ZAMANIN* namu har kika samu mai yimaki so irin haka,amma jin kasa tsayar da hankalin ki guri daya,to ba inda Zaki je da wannan lefen Kuma dole yanzu zaki bishi basai anjima ba"haka dai tayi ta fada su Aunty Husnah suna bata hakuri .shiko Yasir harda key din sabuwar mota ya kawo mata,Amma jin irin fadan da Aunty safiyya take yi yasa ya Bari sai idan sun koma gida .da haka dai Aunty Safiyya ta shiyata da Kara tsimata kafin suka mikata ga Yasir Suka nufi hanyar gidan su,a lokacin cikinta nada wata ukku da sati daya.



To a wannan rana dai Yasir yaga shogoba da salan soyayya,Dan sosai Farrah ta kula da shi Dan ita Daman tayi missing dinshi na tsawon wannan lokacin daurewa kawai take yi Dan kwatoma kanta yanci.a wannan Daren ya damka mata key din tsaleliyar motar daya siya mata saboda yadda yaji dadin ta shayar da shi zuma mai dadi.sosai yake jin shi cikin nishadi.godiya tayi ta yimai,yanzu ta samu abinda take so makaranta ce kadai ya ragemata,Amma tunda baya so dole zata hakura har zuwa time din da zai amince.

🅿️50
      📝Haka rayuwar gidajen ma'auratan ta kasance da dadi ba dadi,saboda Daman yau da gobe sai Allah.inda Bilkisu take dauke da karamin ciki na tsawon wata ukku,haka itama Bahijja nata cikin ya haura wata hudu sai uwar gayya Farrah inda ita haihuwa yau ko gobe.




Allah mai kashewa mai rayawa a inda ya dauki ran Khairiyya a cikin lokaci kalilan sakamokon ciwon daya matsa mata ga hawan jini Wanda sanadin yawan tunani da irin wahalar da take fuskanta ya haifar mata.sosai Alhaji lawi da yayanta sukayi kuka Dan koba komai tayi mai biyayya da hakurin zaman gidan shi.a haka dai aka yimata sallah aka kaita gidanta na gaskiya inda ba makawa kowa sai yaje.bayan sadakar kwana ukku yayanta ya koma katsina shida matar shi da kanwar innar su.(sai muce Allah ya jikanta,ya yafe mata kurakuranta,yasa ta huta muko Allah ya kyautata tamu Bayan tasu😭)amen.





   *TWO MONTHS LETTERS* 

 *BAYAN WATA BIYU* 



Haka rayuwar tayi ta juyawa,a lokacin da cikin Farrah ya cika wata Tara da kwana Tara,a ranar da dare ta fara nakuda da sauri ya Kira Aunty Husnah Dan lokacin ko karfe goma batayi ba.ba tare da bata lokaci ba mijinta ya kawota Suka wuce wani pravet Hospital.basu sanar da kowa ba.bayan awa biyu data dauka tana doguwar nakuda cikin ikon Allah ta sunkuto diyarta fara sol kyakykyawa mai kama da ita.zo kuga murna a wurin Yasir Dan farinciki baisan time din daya rungume su dukansu ba ita da babyn Aunty Husnah tana yimasu dariya.kafin Suka fara sanar da Aunty safiyya jin diyarta ta sauka lafiya yasa tayi ta hamdala da tasbihi ga Allah mai kowa Mai komai.kasancewar lafiya ta haihu yasa a wannna daren aka sallame su,Suka koma gida.

Acan Aunty Husnah ta kwana bayan ta Kara gyara baby da Farrah,kasancewar sun tanadi kayansu (kunsan abinka ga masu abu 🤣).





A washe gari akayi ta sanar da yan'uwa da abokan arziki.itako Farrah Bahijja ta Kira tana sanar mata inda tayi ta murna tana tambayar ta Amma haihuwar ba wahala ko?dariya Farrah tayi tana cewa "hmm haba yarinya idan taki tazo zakiyi bayani Dan sai kin ji bazaki Kara yarda da yaya Jabir ba"da haka dai sukayi sallama tana ta tsokanart.


A ranar Aunty safiyya da Alhaji Ubah suka je ganin Diya tare da kaimasu shatara ta arziki.kai farra taga gata suk Wanda yazo to da abinda zai kawo na kayan barka.

Ranar ya gobe suna Yan katsina suka fara kwararowa birnin tarayya Abuja Momy ita da Nusaiba,Bahijja ita da angonta Jabiru itama tana dama da nata cikin sai Ummah ita da Aunty zenat da Aunta Umar,Aunty Fatima kowa daga Kaduna ta wuce.har yan Dutsinma ba'a barsu a baya ba mota guda sukayi da kayan su na suna Dan suna kaunar iyalan yan'uwan nasu saboda duk kudin su to basa wulakanta su Kuma suna kyautata masu fiye da tunanin mai tinani.


Ranar suna sunyi anko yakai kala biyar-biyar sai shiga suke yi suna canzawa,inda a filin gidan aka gabatar da kayattacciyar walimar cin abinci,sosai Yasir yayi kyau kamar ranar yake sabon ango ga abokanshi duk sunzo inda kowa yayiwa Baby kyauta mai tsoka.yarinya taci sunan Aunty safiyya inda suke kiranta da (Amrah) sosai aka Sha hidima kayan da mai jego ta samu Basu lissafuwa Banda tsabar kudade da kayan yara,Dan Yaya Jabir akwati guda ya kawo na kayan baby.a ranar kowa da farinciki ya kwana.bayan suna da kwana biyu kowa ya koma inda Momy ta kafe saida aka Bata Farrah ta koma katsina da ita danta kula da ita,danta fahimci nan da wata shida ma suna iya dawowa suna saboda rawar Kan da taga masu jegon sunayi.







       *BAYAN ARBA'IN* 

Amrah tayi tubarkalla kyanta sai Kara fitowa yakeyi,Farrah mai jego kuwa sai Kara wani kiba da haske take yi.duk a satin Bahijja ta haifi danta namiji mai kama da su Jabir yaci sunan Dady Bishir inda suke cemai Dady, ansha  shagalin suna  sosai da hotuna.


Itama Bilkisu tayi diyarta mace inda Suka saka mata sunan Innar su.cikin konciyar hankali da hakuri take zaune da mijinta cikin ikon Allah ya samu teaching a wata primary nan kusa da unguwar su Daman yayi N C E dinshi Dan haka daya taso daga koyarwa sai ya tafi sana'ar nafef dinshi.hakan yasa suka Kara samun wadata fiye da da,har takan taimaki innar su cikin abinda yake Bata na kashewa.


A wani yammaci ne kafin Farrah ta koma Abuja taje gidan Bahijja suna fira take tambayarta Wai Ina labarin Bilkisu cewa tayi gaskiya bata sani ba Amma gobe su shiya sai suje gidan su.hakan kuwa sukayi a washe gari da yamma sukaje gidan su Bilkisu inda innarta take sanar masu ai tayi aure har tana da diyarta daganan tasa wani yaro a unguwar ya shiga Motar su ya rakasu har kofar gidan Bilkisu bayan sun yima innarta kyautar kudi.

Lokacin da suka shiga tana jajjajaga kayan miya Dan dora girkin dare,taji ana sallama hakan yasa ta amsa tana cewa "ku shigo Mana inanan"suna shigowa ta dako kanta kamar a mafarki taga Farrah da Bahijja dauke da yaransh a hannu.da gudu ta ruga tana rungume Farrah,baiwar Allah nan take sai ta fara kuka mai dauke da nadama.

Sosai ta Basu tausayi nan tayi masu iso zuwa cikin dan madaidaicin falonta mai dauke da kukeru irin namu na talakkawa.

Bayan sun gaisa ta kawo masu rufa mai sanyi na randa a cikin cup.har zuwa wannan lokacin Bata daina sharar kolla ba.

Can Bahijja ta ce "kiyi hakuri Bilkisu ki daina wannan kukan kada kanki yayi ciwo,haka Allah ya tsara Mana rayuwa kowa da irin yadda tashi kaddarar take zowa,Kuma mu tuni mun yafe maki,yanzu duk mun zama daya"itama tana goge kollar da take son zubo mata ganin yadda kawar tasu ta koma,ada Bilkisu Yar gayu ce,dukda yanzuma Bata da kazanta sai halin rashi da ya mayar da ita haka.jin kukan small Inna yasa ta mike ta daukota ta tashi daga baccin da takeyi.

Haka itama Farrah tayi ta Bata hakuri da nuna mata amfanin hakuri.sosai suka kontar mata da hankali inda a karshe ta saki jikinta suna ta fira.har bayan magriba kafin suka fara Shirin tafiya.tana rakosu kofar gida saiga Farisu ya dawo a Adaidaita sahunsa.har wurin su ya karaso cikin girmamawa suka gaisa kafin ya wuce cikin gidan,sai a lokacin take sanar da su labarin mutuwar khairiyya,sosai sukayi jimami tare da Addu'ar Allah ya jikanta.

Farrah rafar kudi ta dauko a gaban motar ta na duhu hamsin ta mikawa Bilkisu inda ta nuna bazata amsa ba,saida taga ta Bata rai kafin ta amsa,itako Bahijja cewa tayi gobe zata ba Jabir kayayyaki ya kawo mata Dana Babyn ta sosai Bilkisu taji dadi tana ta hawaye tayi masu godiya Suka tafi da zummar zasu Kara dawowa kafin Farrah ta tafi.



Bahijja ta cika alkawari inda tayi wata akwati guda na kayan sawa da nata Dana Diyar ta sai sababbin atamfa da leshin da kayan yara taba Jabir tare da address din gidan bai Sha wahala ba ya gano gidan.inda Suka gaisa da farisu Wanda ya fito da zummar zuwa wurin teaching dinshi.ya bashi cewar inji Bahijja a baiwa Bilkisu.sosai yayi godiya.daya kaiwa Bilkisu kayan tana dagawa ta gani ga tsabar kudin da Farrah ta bata,batasan lokacin data fashe da kukan farin ciki ba.Farisu Yana ta lallashin ta.



Ana ya jibi Farrah zata koma Suka Kara dawowa gidan Bilkisu inda tayi masu tarba mai kyau ana Suka wuni tare da Kara kawo mata tsaraba ta kayan kolliya.kafin sukayi sallama inda Bahijja ta Bata address din gidan da take Dan su rinka zuminci.


Anyi haka da kwana biyu Farrah ta daga Abuja saboda damunta da Gogan nata yake yi.sosai yaji dadin ganin yadda diyar shi tayi tubarkalla ga zumar shi ta Kara kiba da kyau.kai fns a wannnan rana an rakashe an soye abinka ga an dade ba'a haduba 🤣🙊🙈.


A haka rayuwa tayi ta tafiya a cikin wannan gidaje cikin farinciki da kaunar juna.farrah ta tura kudi an gyarawa au Bilkisu gidan su yayi gwanin kyau tare da bata jari akan cewar ta kama sana'a.


Bayan wani Dan lokaci Allah yayiwa Baba Yakubu na Dutsinma rasuwa,sosai familyn sukayi kuka na rashin dattijo mai hada zuminci da San Yan uwanshi.haka su farrah Suka zo gaisuwa inda ta kawowa Bilkisu karamar waya domin su rinka gaisawa a waya.Bilkisu tayi ta murna tana Kara neman yafiyar su Farrah Dan ita ko taga irin sharrin *ZAMANINMU* mai saka nadama da Dana sani (to Allah karemu daga nadama da Dana sani🤲).


A Abuja kuwa Aunty Husnah ta Kara haihuwar danta namiji inda aka rada mai sunan kawu Yakubu suna kiranshi da kawu.


Sosai soyayya tayi Kara karfi a tsakanin Farrah da Yasir,inda wani lokacin yakan jata da cewar zai yimata kishi dariya take yi ta ce "Ai koka karota Bata da wurin zama anan gidan,Dan nida kai mun zama mutu ka raba takalmin kaza "😂Kai Farrah to Nima dai nace Allah yabar kauna da dankon soyayya.🏃🏃🏃nidai nayi nan Bari na barku hakanan ko Allah zaisa Nima nayi dace da Wanda zai nunaman kauna irin haka 😂🤣🙈.
